\lesssim&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f(t)\|_{\infty}\int_{\sqrt{|z_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2}}<t}\frac{1}{(|z_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})^{1/2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{1+|v|^{4+\delta}}dv\mathrm{d}z_{\parallel}\\
\lesssim&t\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f(t)\|_{\infty}.\end{split}

(3.14)

Next, we go to estimate for
B(t,x)
in (
2.67
)–(
2.78
). Similar to (
3.2
), we have

|\eqref{Besttat0pos}|+|\eqref{Besttat0neg}|\lesssim\|B_{0}\|_{C^{1}}.

(3.15)

From (
3.3
) we have

|\frac{(\omega\times\hat{v})\left(1-|\hat{v}|^{2}\right)}{(1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega)^{2}}|\leq 2|\frac{\omega\times v}{(1+|v|^{2})^{3/2}(1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega)^{2}}|\leq 8\frac{|\omega\times v|\sqrt{1+|v|^{2}}}{(1+|\omega\times v|^{2})^{2}}\leq 8\sqrt{1+|v|^{2}}.

(3.16)

Thus

\begin{split}|\eqref{Bestbulkpos}|+|\eqref{Bestbulkneg}|\lesssim&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f(t)\|_{\infty}\int_{B(x;t)}\frac{1}{|y-x|^{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{1+|v|^{3+\delta}}dvdy\\
\lesssim&t\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f(t)\|_{\infty}.\end{split}

Next, using the equation (
0.40
) and the definition of
Sf
, from integration by parts in
v
,

\begin{split}&\eqref{BestSpos}\\
=&\int_{B(x;t)\cap\{y_{3}>0\}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{(\omega\times\hat{v})_{i}}{1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega}((E+E_{\text{ext}}+\hat{v}\times(B+B_{\text{ext}})-g\mathbf{e}_{3})\cdot\nabla_{v}f)(t-|y-x|,y,v)\mathrm{d}v\frac{\mathrm{d}y}{|y-x|}\\
=&\int_{B(x;t)\cap\{y_{3}>0\}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nabla_{v}\left(\frac{(\omega\times\hat{v})_{i}}{1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega}\right)\cdot(E+E_{\text{ext}}+\hat{v}\times(B+B_{\text{ext}})-g\mathbf{e}_{3})f(t-|y-x|,y,v)\mathrm{d}v\frac{\mathrm{d}y}{|y-x|}\\
=&\int_{B(x;t)\cap\{y_{3}>0\}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathcal{S}^{B}_{i}(v,\omega)\cdot(E+\hat{v}\times(B+B_{\text{ext}})-g\mathbf{e}_{3})f(t-|y-x|,y,v)\mathrm{d}v\frac{\mathrm{d}y}{|y-x|},\end{split}

(3.17)

where by direct calculation we have

\begin{split}\mathcal{S}^{B}_{i}(v,\omega)=\nabla_{v}\left(\frac{(\omega\times\hat{v})_{i}}{1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega}\right)=&\nabla_{v}\left(\frac{(\omega\times v)_{i}}{\sqrt{1+|v|^{2}}+v\cdot\omega}\right)\\
=&\frac{\nabla_{v}[(\omega\times v)_{i}]}{\sqrt{1+|v|^{2}}+v\cdot\omega}+\frac{(\omega\times v)_{i}(\hat{v}+\omega)}{(\sqrt{1+|v|^{2}}+v\cdot\omega)^{2}}\\
=&\frac{\nabla_{v}[(\omega\times v)_{i}]}{\sqrt{1+|v|^{2}}(1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega)}+\frac{(\omega\times v)_{i}(\hat{v}+\omega)}{(\sqrt{1+|v|^{2}}(1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega))^{2}}.\end{split}

(3.18)

Therefore from (
3.3
), we have

\begin{split}|\mathcal{S}^{B}_{i}(v,\omega)|\leq\frac{2}{\sqrt{1+|v|^{2}}(1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega)}+8\frac{|\omega\times v|\sqrt{1+|v|^{2}}}{(1+|\omega\times v|^{2})^{2}}\leq 12\sqrt{1+|v|^{2}}.\end{split}

(3.19)

So we can use the same argument as in (
3.6
)–(
3.10
) with (
3.59
) and (
3.19
) to get

\begin{split}&|\eqref{BestSpos}|+|\eqref{BestSneg}|\\
\lesssim&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f(t)\|_{\infty}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|E(t)\|_{\infty}+\|B(t)\|_{\infty}+g+E_{e}+|B_{e}|\right)\int_{B(x;t)}\frac{1}{|y-x|}dy\\
\lesssim&t^{2}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f(t)\|_{\infty}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|E(t)\|_{\infty}+\|B(t)\|_{\infty}+g+E_{e}+|B_{e}|\right).\end{split}

Next, again from (
3.3
),

|\frac{(\omega\times\hat{v})_{i}}{1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega}|\leq 2\sqrt{1+|v|^{2}}.

(3.20)

So similar to (
3.12
), (
3.13
) we get

|\eqref{Bestbdrypos}|+|\eqref{Bestbdryneg}|\lesssim t\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f(t)\|_{\infty},

(3.21)

and

|\eqref{Bestinitialpos}|+|\eqref{Bestinitialneg}|\lesssim t\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f(0)\|_{\infty}.

(3.22)

And finally same as (
3.14
), we have

|\eqref{Bestbdrycontri}|\lesssim t\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f(t)\|_{\infty}.

(3.23)

Combining (
3.2
), (
3.5
), (
3.10
), (
3.12
), (
3.13
), (
3.14
), and (
3.15
)–(
3.23
), we get

\begin{split}&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|E(t)\|_{\infty}+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|B(t)\|_{\infty}\\
\lesssim&\|E_{0}\|_{\infty}+\|B_{0}\|_{\infty}+t(\|E_{0}\|_{C^{1}}+\|B_{0}\|_{C^{1}})\\
&+t\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f(t)\|_{\infty}\left(1+t(\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|E(t)\|_{\infty}+\|B(t)\|_{\infty}\right)+g+E_{e}+|B_{e}|)\right).\end{split}

(3.24)

Taking
0<T\ll 1
, we conclude (
3.1
).

∎

Next we estimate the derivatives of the fields.

Lemma 9
.

With the formula
E(t,x)
as in (
2.42
)–(
2.53
), and
B(t,x)
as in (
2.67
)–(
2.78
), there exists a
T\ll 1
such that for any
t\in[0,T]
,

\begin{split}\|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}E(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}B(t)\|_{\infty}\lesssim&\|E_{0}\|_{C^{2}}+\|B_{0}\|_{C^{2}}+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{\infty}\right)\\
&+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f(t)\|_{\infty},\end{split}

(3.25)

\begin{split}\|\partial_{x_{3}}E(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\partial_{x_{3}}B(t)\|_{\infty}\lesssim&\|E_{0}\|_{C^{2}}+\|B_{0}\|_{C^{2}}+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{\infty}\right)\\
&+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f(t)\|_{\infty},\end{split}

(3.26)

and

\begin{split}\|\partial_{t}E(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\partial_{t}B(t)\|_{\infty}\lesssim&\|E_{0}\|_{C^{2}}+\|B_{0}\|_{C^{2}}+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(t)\|_{\infty}\right)\\
&+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f(t)\|_{\infty}.\end{split}

(3.27)

Proof.

We take derivative to
\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{k}}}E_{i}(t,x)
in (
2.42
)-(
2.53
) and estimate each term.

First, by using the change of variables
z=y-x
and spherical coordinate for
z
, we have

\begin{split}\eqref{Eesttat0pos}=&\frac{1}{4\pi t^{2}}\int_{\{|z|=t,x_{3}+z_{3}>0\}}\left(t\partial_{t}E_{0,i}(x+z)+E_{0,i}(x+z)+\nabla E_{0,i}(x+z)\cdot z\right)dS_{z}\\
=&\frac{1}{4\pi t^{2}}\int_{\{t\cos\phi>-x_{3}\}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\left(t\partial_{t}E_{0,i}(x+z)+E_{0,i}(x+z)+\nabla E_{0,i}(x+z)\cdot z\right)t^{2}\sin\phi d\theta d\phi.\end{split}

(3.28)

Thus

\begin{split}\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{k}}}\eqref{Eesttat0pos}=&\frac{1}{4\pi t^{2}}\int_{\{|z|=t,x_{3}+z_{3}>0\}}\left(t\partial_{x_{k}}\partial_{t}E_{0,i}(x+z)+\partial_{x_{k}}E_{0,i}(x+z)+\nabla\partial_{x_{k}}E_{0,i}(x+z)\cdot z\right)dS_{z}\\
&+\frac{1}{4\pi t^{2}}\delta_{k3}\int_{0}^{2\pi}(t\partial_{t}E_{0,i}(x_{\parallel}+z_{\parallel},0)+E_{0,i}(x_{\parallel}+z_{\parallel},0)+\nabla E_{0,i}(x_{\parallel}+z_{\parallel},0)\cdot z)\\
&\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\times\left(\frac{d}{dx_{3}}\cos^{-1}\left(\frac{-x_{3}}{t}\right)\right)\left(\sin\left(\cos^{-1}\left(\frac{-x_{3}}{t}\right)\right)\right)t^{2}d\theta\\
=&\frac{1}{4\pi t^{2}}\int_{\{|z|=t,x_{3}+z_{3}>0\}}\left(t\partial_{x_{k}}\partial_{t}E_{0,i}(x+z)+\partial_{x_{k}}E_{0,i}(x+z)+\nabla\partial_{x_{k}}E_{0,i}(x+z)\cdot z\right)dS_{z}\\
&+\frac{1}{4\pi t}\delta_{k3}\int_{0}^{2\pi}(t\partial_{t}E_{0,i}(x_{\parallel}+z_{\parallel},0)+E_{0,i}(x_{\parallel}+z_{\parallel},0)+\nabla E_{0,i}(x_{\parallel}+z_{\parallel},0)\cdot z)d\theta.\end{split}

(3.29)

For
i=1,2
,
E_{0,i}(x_{\parallel}+z_{\parallel},0)=0
, thus we have

|\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{k}}}\eqref{Eesttat0pos}_{i=1,2}|\lesssim t\|\nabla_{x}\partial_{t}E_{0}\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla_{x}E_{0}\|_{\infty}+t\|\nabla_{x}^{2}E_{0}\|_{\infty}+\|\partial_{t}E_{0}\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla_{x}E_{0}\|_{\infty}\lesssim\|E_{0}\|_{C^{2}}.

And we apply the same estimate for
\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{k}}}\eqref{Eesttat0neg}_{i=1,2}
to obtain

|\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{k}}}\eqref{Eesttat0pos}_{i=1,2}|+|\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{k}}}\eqref{Eesttat0neg}_{i=1,2}|\lesssim\|E_{0}\|_{C^{2}}.

For
i=3
, we use the cancellation for
\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{k}}}\eqref{Eesttat0pos}_{i=3}+\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{k}}}\eqref{Eesttat0neg}_{i=3}
at
y_{3}=0
to get

\begin{split}&\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{k}}}\eqref{Eesttat0pos}_{i=3}+\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{k}}}\eqref{Eesttat0neg}_{i=3}\\
=&\frac{1}{4\pi t^{2}}\int_{\{|z|=t,x_{3}+z_{3}>0\}}\left(t\partial_{x_{k}}\partial_{t}E_{0,3}(x+z)+\partial_{x_{k}}E_{0,3}(x+z)+\nabla\partial_{x_{k}}E_{0,3}(x+z)\cdot z\right)dS_{z}\\
&+\frac{1}{4\pi t^{2}}\int_{\{|z|=t,x_{3}+z_{3}<0\}}\iota_{k}\left(t\partial_{x_{k}}\partial_{t}E_{0,3}(x+z)+\partial_{x_{k}}E_{0,3}(x+z)+\nabla_{\parallel}\partial_{x_{k}}E_{0,3}(x+z)\cdot z_{\parallel}-\partial_{x_{3}}\partial_{x_{k}}E_{0,3}(x+z)\cdot z_{3}\right)dS_{z}\\
&+\frac{2}{4\pi t^{2}}\delta_{k3}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\left(\partial_{x_{3}}E_{0,3}(x_{\parallel}+z_{\parallel},0)\right)\frac{-x_{3}}{t}t^{2}d\theta.\end{split}

(3.30)

Thus
|\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{k}}}\eqref{Eesttat0pos}_{i=3}|+|\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{k}}}\eqref{Eesttat0neg}_{i=3}|\lesssim\|E_{0}\|_{C^{2}}
, and therefore

|\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{k}}}\eqref{Eesttat0pos}|+|\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{k}}}\eqref{Eesttat0neg}|\lesssim\|E_{0}\|_{C^{2}}.

(3.31)

Next, using the change of variables
z=y-x
we have

\begin{split}\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{k}}}\eqref{Eestbulkpos}=&\int_{\{|z|<t\}\cap\{z_{3}>-x_{3}\}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{(|\hat{v}|^{2}-1)(\hat{v}_{i}+\omega_{i})}{|z|^{2}(1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega)^{2}}\partial_{x_{k}}f(t-|z|,z+x,v)\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}z\\
&+\delta_{k3}\int_{\{|z|<t\}\cap\{z_{3}=-x_{3}\}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{(|\hat{v}|^{2}-1)(\hat{v}_{i}+\omega_{i})}{|z|^{2}(1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega)^{2}}f(t-|z|,z+x,v)\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}z_{\parallel}\\
=&\underbrace{\int_{\{|y-x|<t\}\cap\{y_{3}>0\}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{(|\hat{v}|^{2}-1)(\hat{v}_{i}+\omega_{i})}{|y-x|^{2}(1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega)^{2}}\partial_{x_{k}}f(t-|y-x|,y,v)\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}y}_{\eqref{Eestbulkpos1}_{1}}\\
&+\underbrace{\delta_{k3}\int_{\{(|y_{\parallel}-x_{\parallel}|^{2}+|x_{3}|^{2})^{1/2}<t\}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{(|\hat{v}|^{2}-1)(\hat{v}_{i}+\omega_{i})}{(|y_{\parallel}-x_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})(1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega)^{2}}f(t-|y-x|,y_{\parallel},0,v)\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}y_{\parallel}}_{\eqref{Eestbulkpos1}_{2}}.\end{split}

(3.32)

Using (
3.4
), we have for
k=1,2
,

\begin{split}&\int_{\{|y-x|<t\}\cap\{y_{3}>0\}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{(|\hat{v}|^{2}-1)(\hat{v}_{i}+\omega_{i})}{|y-x|^{2}(1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega)^{2}}\partial_{x_{k}}f(t-|y-x|,y,v)\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}y\\
\lesssim&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{\infty}\int_{\{|y-x|<t\}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{|y-x|^{2}}(1+|v|)^{-3-\delta}\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}y\\
\lesssim&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{\infty}.\end{split}

(3.33)

For
k=3
, we have for any
1<p<\frac{3}{2}
, from (
3.4
), and Lemma
11
which will be proved in the next section,

\begin{split}&\int_{\{|y-x|<t\}\cap\{y_{3}>0\}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{(|\hat{v}|^{2}-1)(\hat{v}_{i}+\omega_{i})}{|y-x|^{2}(1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega)^{2}}\partial_{x_{3}}f(t-|y-x|,y,v)\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}y\\
\lesssim&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(t)\|_{\infty}\int_{\{|y-x|<t\}\cap\{y_{3}>0\}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{|y-x|^{2}}\frac{(1+|v|)^{-4-\delta}}{\alpha(t-|y-x|,y,v)}dvdy\\
\lesssim&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(t)\|_{\infty}\right)\int_{\{|y-x|<t\}\cap\{y_{3}>0\}}\frac{1}{|y-x|^{2}}\left(1+\ln(1+\frac{1}{y_{3}})\right)dy\\
\lesssim&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(t)\|_{\infty}\right)\int_{\{|y-x|<t\}\cap\{y_{3}>0\}}\left(\frac{1}{|y-x|^{2p}}+|\ln(1+\frac{1}{y_{3}})|^{\frac{p}{p-1}}\right)dy\\
\lesssim&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(t)\|_{\infty}.\end{split}

(3.34)

We leave the estimate of
\eqref{Eestbulkpos1}_{2}
together with the estimate of
\partial_{x_{k}}\eqref{Eestbdrypos}
later.

Next, from (
3.6
), and using the change of variables
z=y-x
and taking
\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{k}}}
derivative to (
2.47
) we have

\begin{split}\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{k}}}\eqref{EestSpos}=&\int_{B(x;t)\cap\{y_{3}>0\}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathcal{S}^{E}_{i}(v,\omega)\cdot(\partial_{x_{k}}E+\hat{v}\times\partial_{x_{k}}B)f(t-|y-x|,y,v)\mathrm{d}v\frac{\mathrm{d}y}{|y-x|}\\
&+\int_{B(x;t)\cap\{y_{3}>0\}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathcal{S}^{E}_{i}(v,\omega)\cdot(E+\hat{v}\times(B+B_{\text{ext}})-g\mathbf{e}_{3})\partial_{x_{k}}f(t-|y-x|,y,v)\mathrm{d}v\frac{\mathrm{d}y}{|y-x|}\\
&+\delta_{k3}\int_{B(x;t)\cap\{y_{3}=0\}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathcal{S}^{E}_{i}(v,\omega)\cdot(E+\hat{v}\times(B+B_{\text{ext}})-g\mathbf{e}_{3})f(t-|y-x|,y_{\parallel},0,v)\mathrm{d}v\frac{\mathrm{d}y_{\parallel}}{|y-x|}.\end{split}

(3.35)

Thus for
k=1,2
, from (
3.9
) we have,

\begin{split}&|\int_{B(x;t)\cap\{y_{3}>0\}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathcal{S}^{E}_{i}(v,\omega)\cdot(\partial_{x_{k}}E+\hat{v}\times\partial_{x_{k}}B)f(t-|y-x|,y,v)\mathrm{d}v\frac{\mathrm{d}y}{|y-x|}|\\
\lesssim&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|(1+|v|^{4+\delta})f(t)\|_{\infty}\int_{B(x;t)\cap\{y_{3}>0\}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|(\partial_{x_{k}}E+\hat{v}\times\partial_{x_{k}}B)|\left(\frac{1}{1+|v|^{3+\delta}}\right)dv\frac{\mathrm{d}y}{|y-x|}\\
\lesssim&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|(1+|v|^{4+\delta})f(t)\|_{\infty}\left(\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}E(t)\|_{\infty}+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}B(t)\|_{\infty}\right)\times\int_{B(x;t)}\frac{dy}{|y-x|}\\
\lesssim&t^{2}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|(1+|v|^{4+\delta})f(t)\|_{\infty}\left(\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}E(t)\|_{\infty}+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}B(t)\|_{\infty}\right).\end{split}

(3.36)

Similarly, for
k=3
,

\begin{split}&|\int_{B(x;t)\cap\{y_{3}>0\}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathcal{S}^{E}_{i}(v,\omega)\cdot(\partial_{x_{k}}E+\hat{v}\times\partial_{x_{3}}B)f(t-|y-x|,y,v)\mathrm{d}v\frac{\mathrm{d}y}{|y-x|}|\\
\lesssim&t^{2}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|(1+|v|^{4+\delta})f(t)\|_{\infty}\left(\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\partial_{x_{3}}E(t)\|_{\infty}+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\partial_{x_{3}}B(t)\|_{\infty}\right),\end{split}

(3.37)

for any
0<\delta\ll 1
. Thus for
t\ll 1
, the terms from (
3.36
), (
3.37
) will be absorbed into the LHS. From (
3.9
), we have for
k=1,2
,

\begin{split}&|\int_{B(x;t)\cap\{y_{3}>0\}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathcal{S}^{E}_{i}(v,\omega)\cdot(E+E_{\text{ext}}+\hat{v}\times(B+B_{\text{ext}})-g\mathbf{e}_{3})\partial_{x_{k}}f(t-|y-x|,y,v)\mathrm{d}v\frac{\mathrm{d}y}{|y-x|}|\\
\lesssim&\left(\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|E(t)\|_{\infty}+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|B(t)\|_{\infty}+|B_{e}|+g\right)\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{\infty}\right)\\
&\quad\quad\times\int_{B(x;t)\cap\{y_{3}>0\}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{(1+|v|^{-3-\delta})}{|y-x|}dvdy\\
\lesssim&\left(\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|E(t)\|_{\infty}+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|B(t)\|_{\infty}+|B_{e}|+g\right)\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{\infty}\right).\end{split}

(3.38)

And, for
k=3
,

\begin{split}&|\int_{B(x;t)\cap\{y_{3}>0\}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathcal{S}^{E}_{i}(v,\omega)\cdot(E+E_{\text{ext}}+\hat{v}\times(B+B_{\text{ext}})-g\mathbf{e}_{3})\partial_{x_{3}}f(t-|y-x|,y,v)\mathrm{d}v\frac{\mathrm{d}y}{|y-x|}|\\
\lesssim&\left(\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|E(t)\|_{\infty}+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|B(t)\|_{\infty}+|B_{e}|+E_{e}+g\right)\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(t)\|_{\infty}\right)\\
&\quad\quad\quad\times\int_{B(x;t)\cap\{y_{3}>0\}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{|y-x|}\left(\frac{\langle v\rangle^{-4-\delta}}{\alpha(t-|y-x|,y,v)}\right)dvdy\\
\lesssim&\left(\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|E(t)\|_{\infty}+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|B(t)\|_{\infty}+|B_{e}|+E_{e}+g\right)\\
&\times\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(t)\|_{\infty}\right)\int_{B(x;t)\cap\{y_{3}>0\}}\frac{1}{|y-x|}|\ln(y_{3})|dy\\
\lesssim&\left(\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|E(t)\|_{\infty}+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|B(t)\|_{\infty}+|B_{e}|+E_{e}+g\right)\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(t)\|_{\infty}\right)\\
&\quad\quad\times\int_{B(x;t)\cap\{y_{3}>0\}}\left(\frac{1}{|y-x|^{2}}+\left(\ln(y_{3})\right)^{2}\right)dy\\
\lesssim&\left(\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|E(t)\|_{\infty}+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|B(t)\|_{\infty}\right)\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(t)\|_{\infty}\right).\end{split}

(3.39)

And

\begin{split}&|\int_{B(x;t)\cap\{y_{3}=0\}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathcal{S}^{E}_{i}(v,\omega)\cdot(E+E_{\text{ext}}+\hat{v}\times(B+B_{\text{ext}})-g\mathbf{e}_{3})f(t-|y-x|,y_{\parallel},0,v)\mathrm{d}v\frac{\mathrm{d}y_{\parallel}}{|y-x|}|\\
\lesssim&\left(\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|E(t)\|_{\infty}+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|B(t)\|_{\infty}+|B_{e}|+E_{e}+g\right)\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f(t)\|_{\infty}\\
&\quad\quad\times\int_{\{(|y_{\parallel}-x_{\parallel}|^{2}+|x_{3}|^{2})<t^{2}\}}\frac{1}{(|y_{\parallel}-x_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})^{1/2}}dy_{\parallel}\\
\lesssim&\left(\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|E(t)\|_{\infty}+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|B(t)\|_{\infty}+|B_{e}|+E_{e}+g\right)\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|(1+|v|^{4+\delta})f(t)\|_{\infty}.\end{split}

(3.40)

Thus combining (
3.35
), (
3.36
), (
3.37
), (
3.38
), (
3.39
), (
3.40
), we get

\begin{split}|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}\eqref{EestSpos}|\lesssim&t^{2}\left(\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}E(t)\|_{\infty}+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}B(t)\|_{\infty}\right)+\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{\infty}\\
|\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{3}}}\eqref{EestSpos}|\lesssim&t^{2}\left(\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\partial_{x_{3}}E(t)\|_{\infty}+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\partial_{x_{3}}B(t)\|_{\infty}\right)\\
&+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(t)\|_{\infty}\right)+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|(1+|v|^{4+\delta})f(t)\|_{\infty}.\end{split}

By the same argument we get the same estimate for
|\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{k}}}\eqref{EestSneg}|
. Therefore

\begin{split}|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}\eqref{EestSpos}|+|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}\eqref{EestSneg}|\lesssim&t^{2}\left(\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}E(t)\|_{\infty}+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}B(t)\|_{\infty}\right)+\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{\infty}\\
|\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{3}}}\eqref{EestSpos}|+|\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{3}}}\eqref{EestSneg}|\lesssim&t^{2}\left(\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\partial_{x_{3}}E(t)\|_{\infty}+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\partial_{x_{3}}B(t)\|_{\infty}\right)\\
&+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(t)\|_{\infty}\right)+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|(1+|v|^{4+\delta})f(t)\|_{\infty}.\end{split}

(3.41)

Next, using the change of variables
z_{\parallel}=y_{\parallel}-x_{\parallel}
we have

\begin{split}&\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{k}}}\eqref{Eestbdrypos}\\
=&\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{k}}}\left(\int_{\sqrt{t^{2}-|z_{\parallel}|^{2}}>x_{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(\delta_{i3}-\frac{(\omega_{i}+\hat{v}_{i})\hat{v}_{3}}{1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega}\right)f(t-(|z_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})^{1/2},z_{\parallel}+x_{\parallel},0,v)\mathrm{d}v\frac{\mathrm{d}z_{\parallel}}{(|z_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})^{1/2}}\right)\\
=&(1-\delta_{k3})\int_{\sqrt{t^{2}-|z_{\parallel}|^{2}}>x_{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(\delta_{i3}-\frac{(\omega_{i}+\hat{v}_{i})\hat{v}_{3}}{1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega}\right)\partial_{x_{k}}f(t-(|z_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})^{1/2},z_{\parallel}+x_{\parallel},0,v)\mathrm{d}v\frac{\mathrm{d}z_{\parallel}}{(|z_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})^{1/2}}\\
&+\delta_{k3}\int_{\sqrt{t^{2}-|z_{\parallel}|^{2}}>x_{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{x_{3}}\left(\frac{1}{(|z_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})^{1/2}}\left(\delta_{i3}-\frac{(\omega_{i}+\hat{v}_{i})\hat{v}_{3}}{1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega}\right)\right)f(t-(|z_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})^{1/2},z_{\parallel}+x_{\parallel},0,v)\mathrm{d}v\ \mathrm{d}z_{\parallel}\\
&+\delta_{k3}\int_{\sqrt{t^{2}-|z_{\parallel}|^{2}}>x_{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(\delta_{i3}-\frac{(\omega_{i}+\hat{v}_{i})\hat{v}_{3}}{1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega}\right)\partial_{t}f(t-(|z_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})^{1/2},z_{\parallel}+x_{\parallel},0,v)\mathrm{d}v\frac{-x_{3}}{(|z_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})}\mathrm{d}z_{\parallel}\\
&-\delta_{k3}\int_{\sqrt{t^{2}-|z_{\parallel}|^{2}}=x_{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(\delta_{i3}\frac{(\omega_{i}+\hat{v}_{i})\hat{v}_{3}}{1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega}\right)f(0,z_{\parallel}+x_{\parallel},0,v)\mathrm{d}v\frac{x_{3}}{\sqrt{t^{2}-x_{3}^{2}}}\left(\frac{z_{\parallel}}{\sqrt{t^{2}-x^{2}}}\cdot\frac{z_{\parallel}}{|z_{\parallel}|}\right)\frac{\mathrm{d}S_{z_{\parallel}}}{t}.\end{split}

(3.42)

The first term is only contribute as the tangential derivative, from (
3.11
),

\begin{split}&|(1-\delta_{k3})\int_{\sqrt{t^{2}-|z_{\parallel}|^{2}}>x_{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left(\delta_{i3}-\frac{(\omega_{i}+\hat{v}_{i})\hat{v}_{3}}{1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega}\right)\partial_{x_{k}}f(t-(|z_{\parallel}|+x_{3}^{2})^{1/2},z_{\parallel}+x_{\parallel},0,v)\mathrm{d}v\frac{\mathrm{d}z_{\parallel}}{(|z_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})^{1/2}}|\\
\lesssim&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|(1+|v|^{4+\delta})\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{\infty}\int_{\sqrt{t^{2}-|z_{\parallel}|^{2}}>x_{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{(1+|v|^{4+\delta})^{-1}}{(|z_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})^{1/2}}dvdz_{\parallel}\\
\lesssim&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|(1+|v|^{4+\delta})\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{\infty}\int_{r^{2}+x_{3}^{2}<t^{2}}\frac{r}{(r^{2}+x_{3}^{2})^{1/2}}dr\lesssim\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|(1+|v|^{4+\delta})\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{\infty}.\end{split}

(3.43)

For the second term, recall
\eqref{Eestbulkpos1}_{2}
using the identity (
[
12
]
)

\frac{(|\hat{v}|^{2}-1)(\hat{v}_{i}+\omega_{i})}{(|y_{\parallel}-x_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})(1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega)^{2}}=\left.\sum_{j=1}^{3}\frac{\partial}{\partial y_{j}}\left[\frac{1}{|y-x|}\left(\delta_{ij}-\frac{(\omega_{i}+\hat{v}_{i})\hat{v}_{j}}{1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega}\right)\right]\right|_{y_{3}=0},

we have

\begin{split}&|\eqref{Eestbulkpos1}_{2}+\int_{\sqrt{t^{2}-|z_{\parallel}|^{2}}>x_{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{x_{3}}\left(\frac{1}{(|z_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})^{1/2}}\left(\delta_{i3}-\frac{(\omega_{i}+\hat{v}_{i})\hat{v}_{3}}{1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega}\right)\right)f(t-(|z_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})^{1/2},z_{\parallel}+x_{\parallel},0,v)\mathrm{d}v\ \mathrm{d}z_{\parallel}|\\
=&|\delta_{k3}\int_{\{(|y_{\parallel}-x_{\parallel}|^{2}+|x_{3}|^{2})^{1/2}<t\}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left.\sum_{j=1}^{3}\frac{\partial}{\partial y_{j}}\left[\frac{1}{|y-x|}\left(\delta_{ij}-\frac{(\omega_{i}+\hat{v}_{i})\hat{v}_{j}}{1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega}\right)\right]f(t-|y-x|,y,v)\right|_{y_{3}=0}\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}y_{\parallel}\\
&+\delta_{k3}\int_{\{|y_{\parallel}-x_{\parallel}|^{2}+|x_{3}|^{2})^{1/2}<t\}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{x_{3}}\left(\frac{\left(\delta_{i3}-\frac{(\omega_{i}+\hat{v}_{i})\hat{v}_{3}}{1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega}\right)}{(|y_{\parallel}-x_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})^{1/2}}\right)f(t-(|y_{\parallel}-x_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})^{1/2},y_{\parallel},0,v)\mathrm{d}v\ \mathrm{d}y_{\parallel}|\\
=&|\delta_{k3}\int_{\{(|y_{\parallel}-x_{\parallel}|^{2}+|x_{3}|^{2})^{1/2}<t\}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left.\sum_{j=1}^{2}\frac{\partial}{\partial y_{j}}\left[\frac{1}{|y-x|}\left(\delta_{ij}-\frac{(\omega_{i}+\hat{v}_{i})\hat{v}_{j}}{1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega}\right)\right]f(t-|y-x|,y,v)\right|_{y_{3}=0}\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}y_{\parallel}\\
&+\delta_{k3}\int_{\{(|y_{\parallel}-x_{\parallel}|^{2}+|x_{3}|^{2})^{1/2}<t\}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\left.\left(\frac{\partial}{\partial y_{3}}\left[\frac{1}{|y-x|}\left(\delta_{i3}-\frac{(\omega_{i}+\hat{v}_{i})\hat{v}_{j}}{1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega}\right)\right]f(t-|y-x|,y,v)\right)\right|_{y_{3}=0}\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}y_{\parallel}\\
&+\delta_{k3}\int_{\{|y_{\parallel}-x_{\parallel}|^{2}+|x_{3}|^{2})^{1/2}<t\}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\partial_{x_{3}}\left(\frac{1}{(|y_{\parallel}-x_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})^{1/2}}\left(\delta_{i3}-\frac{(\omega_{i}+\hat{v}_{i})\hat{v}_{3}}{1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega}\right)\right)\\
&\quad\quad\times f(t-(|y_{\parallel}-x_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})^{1/2},y_{\parallel},0,v)\mathrm{d}v\ \mathrm{d}y_{\parallel}|\\
=&|\delta_{k3}\int_{\{(|y_{\parallel}-x_{\parallel}|^{2}+|x_{3}|^{2})^{1/2}<t\}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\sum_{j=1}^{2}\frac{\partial}{\partial y_{j}}\left[\frac{1}{(|y_{\parallel}-x_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})^{1/2}}\left(\delta_{ij}-\frac{(\omega_{i}+\hat{v}_{i})\hat{v}_{j}}{1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega}\right)\right]\\
&\quad\quad\times f(t-(|y_{\parallel}-x_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})^{1/2},y_{\parallel},0,v)\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}y_{\parallel}|,\end{split}

(3.44)

where we’ve used the cancellation
\left.\frac{\partial}{\partial y_{3}}\left[\frac{1}{|y-x|}\left(\delta_{i3}-\frac{(\omega_{i}+\hat{v}_{i})\hat{v}_{j}}{1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega}\right]\right)\right|_{y_{3}=0}=-\partial_{x_{3}}\left(\frac{1}{(|y_{\parallel}-x_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})^{1/2}}\left(\delta_{i3}-\frac{(\omega_{i}+\hat{v}_{i})\hat{v}_{3}}{1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega}\right)\right)
. Thus from integration by parts and (
3.3
),

\begin{split}&|\delta_{k3}\int_{\{(|y_{\parallel}-x_{\parallel}|^{2}+|x_{3}|^{2})^{1/2}<t\}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\sum_{j=1}^{2}\frac{\partial}{\partial y_{j}}\left[\frac{1}{|y-x|}\left(\delta_{ij}-\frac{(\omega_{i}+\hat{v}_{i})\hat{v}_{j}}{1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega}\right)\right]f(t-|y-x|,y,v)\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}y_{\parallel}|\\
\lesssim&|-\int_{\{(|y_{\parallel}-x_{\parallel}|^{2}+|x_{3}|^{2})^{1/2}<t\}}\left[\frac{||y_{\parallel}-x_{\parallel}|}{(|y_{\parallel}-x_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})}\left(\delta_{ij}-\frac{(\omega_{i}+\hat{v}_{i})\hat{v}_{j}}{1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega}\right)\right]\partial_{t}f(t-|y-x|,y,v)\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}y_{\parallel}\\
&-\int_{\{(|y_{\parallel}-x_{\parallel}|^{2}+|x_{3}|^{2})^{1/2}<t\}}\left[\frac{1}{(|y_{\parallel}-x_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})^{1/2}}\left(\delta_{ij}-\frac{(\omega_{i}+\hat{v}_{i})\hat{v}_{j}}{1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega}\right)\right]\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t-|y-x|,y,v)\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}y_{\parallel}\\
&+\int_{\{(|y_{\parallel}-x_{\parallel}|^{2}+|x_{3}|^{2})^{1/2}=t\}}\sum_{j=1}^{2}\frac{\omega_{j}}{t}\left(\delta_{ij}-\frac{(\omega_{i}+\hat{v}_{i})\hat{v}_{j}}{1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega}\right)f(0,y,v)\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}S_{y_{\parallel}}|\\
\lesssim&|\int_{\{(|y_{\parallel}-x_{\parallel}|^{2}+|x_{3}|^{2})^{1/2}<t\}}\left[\frac{||y_{\parallel}-x_{\parallel}|}{(|y_{\parallel}-x_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})}\left(\delta_{ij}-\frac{(\omega_{i}+\hat{v}_{i})\hat{v}_{j}}{1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega}\right)\right]\partial_{t}f(t-|y-x|,y,v)\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}y_{\parallel}|\\
+&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|(1+|v|^{4+\delta})\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{\infty}\int_{|z_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2}<t^{2}}\frac{1}{(|z_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})^{1/2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{1+|v|^{3+\delta}}dvdz_{\parallel}\\
&+\|(1+|v|^{4+\delta})f_{0}\|_{\infty}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{1+|v|^{3+\delta}}dv\\
\lesssim&|\int_{\{(|y_{\parallel}-x_{\parallel}|^{2}+|x_{3}|^{2})^{1/2}<t\}}\left[\frac{||y_{\parallel}-x_{\parallel}|}{(|y_{\parallel}-x_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})}\left(\delta_{ij}-\frac{(\omega_{i}+\hat{v}_{i})\hat{v}_{j}}{1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega}\right)\right]\partial_{t}f(t-|y-x|,y,v)\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}y_{\parallel}|\\
&+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|(1+|v|^{4+\delta})\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{\infty}+\|(1+|v|^{4+\delta})f_{0}\|_{\infty}.\end{split}

(3.45)

Now from (
0.40
), we write
\partial_{t}f=-\hat{v}\cdot\nabla_{x}f-(E+E_{\text{ext}}+\hat{v}\times(B+B_{\text{ext}})-g\mathbf{e}_{3})\cdot\nabla_{v}f
. Then using
\alpha=\hat{v}_{3}
on
\partial\Omega
, integration by parts in
v
, and that
\nabla_{v}\cdot(\hat{v}\times B)=0
, we get

\begin{split}&|\int_{\sqrt{t^{2}-|z_{\parallel}|^{2}}>x_{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{(\omega_{i}+\hat{v}_{i})\hat{v}_{3}}{1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega}\partial_{t}f(t-(|z_{\parallel}|+x_{3}^{2})^{1/2},z_{\parallel}+x_{\parallel},0,v)\mathrm{d}v\frac{x_{3}}{(|z_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})}\mathrm{d}z_{\parallel}|\\
\leq&|\int_{\sqrt{t^{2}-|z_{\parallel}|^{2}}>x_{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{(\omega_{i}+\hat{v}_{i})\hat{v}_{3}}{1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega}\left(\hat{v}\cdot\nabla_{x}f(t-(|z_{\parallel}|+x_{3}^{2})^{1/2},z_{\parallel}+x_{\parallel},0,v)\right)\mathrm{d}v\frac{x_{3}}{(|z_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})}\mathrm{d}z_{\parallel}|\\
&+|\int_{\sqrt{t^{2}-|z_{\parallel}|^{2}}>x_{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{(\omega_{i}+\hat{v}_{i})\hat{v}_{3}}{1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega}\\
&\quad\quad\times\left((E+E_{\text{ext}}+\hat{v}\times(B+B_{\text{ext}})-g\mathbf{e}_{3})\cdot\nabla_{v}f(t-(|z_{\parallel}|+x_{3}^{2})^{1/2},z_{\parallel}+x_{\parallel},0,v)\right)\mathrm{d}v\frac{x_{3}}{(|z_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})}\mathrm{d}z_{\parallel}|\\
\lesssim&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|(1+|v|^{5+\delta})\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(t)\|_{\infty}+\|(1+|v|^{4+\delta})\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{\infty}\right)\int_{\sqrt{t^{2}-|z_{\parallel}|^{2}}>x_{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\langle v\rangle^{-4-\delta}\frac{x_{3}}{(|z_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})}dv\mathrm{d}z_{\parallel}\\
&+|\int_{\sqrt{t^{2}-|z_{\parallel}|^{2}}>x_{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\nabla_{v}\left(\frac{(\omega_{i}+\hat{v}_{i})\hat{v}_{3}}{1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega}\right)(E+E_{\text{ext}}+\hat{v}\times B-g\mathbf{e}_{3})f(t-(|z_{\parallel}|+x_{3}^{2})^{1/2},z_{\parallel}+x_{\parallel},0,v)\mathrm{d}v\\
&\quad\times\frac{x_{3}}{(|z_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})}\mathrm{d}z_{\parallel}|\\
\lesssim&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|(1+|v|^{5+\delta})\left(\|(1+|v|^{5+\delta})\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(t)\|_{\infty}+\|(1+|v|^{4+\delta})\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{\infty}\right)\\
&+\left(\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|(1+|v|^{4+\delta})f(t)\|_{\infty}\right)\int_{\sqrt{t^{2}-|z_{\parallel}|^{2}}>x_{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(1+|v|^{3+\delta})^{-1}\frac{x_{3}}{(|z_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})}dv\mathrm{d}z_{\parallel}\\
\lesssim&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|(1+|v|^{5+\delta})\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(t)\|_{\infty}+\|(1+|v|^{4+\delta})\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{\infty}\right)+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|(1+|v|^{4+\delta})f(t)\|_{\infty},\end{split}

(3.46)

where we’ve used from (
3.7
), (
3.9
), and (
3.11
) that

\left|\nabla_{v}\left(\frac{(\omega_{i}+\hat{v}_{i})\hat{v}_{3}}{1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega}\right)\right|=\left|\mathcal{S}^{E}_{i}(\omega,\hat{v})\hat{v}_{3}+\frac{(\omega_{i}+\hat{v}_{i})}{1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega}\nabla_{v}\hat{v}_{3}\right|\leq 14\sqrt{1+|v|^{2}}.

By the same argument we have

\begin{split}&|\int_{\{(|y_{\parallel}-x_{\parallel}|^{2}+|x_{3}|^{2})^{1/2}<t\}}\left[\frac{||y_{\parallel}-x_{\parallel}|}{(|y_{\parallel}-x_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})}\left(\delta_{ij}-\frac{(\omega_{i}+\hat{v}_{i})\hat{v}_{j}}{1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega}\right)\right]\partial_{t}f(t-|y-x|,y,v)\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}y_{\parallel}|\\
\lesssim&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|(1+|v|^{5+\delta})\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(t)\|_{\infty}+\|(1+|v|^{4+\delta})\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{\infty}\right)+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|(1+|v|^{4+\delta})f(t)\|_{\infty}.\end{split}

(3.47)

We also have

\begin{split}&|\int_{\sqrt{t^{2}-|z_{\parallel}|^{2}}=x_{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{(\omega_{i}+\hat{v}_{i})\hat{v}_{3}}{1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega}f(0,z_{\parallel}+x_{\parallel},0,v)\mathrm{d}v\frac{x_{3}}{\sqrt{t^{2}-x_{3}^{2}}}\left(\frac{z_{\parallel}}{\sqrt{t^{2}-x_{3}^{2}}}\cdot\frac{z_{\parallel}}{|z_{\parallel}|}\right)\frac{\mathrm{d}S_{z_{\parallel}}}{t}|\\
\lesssim&\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f_{0}\|_{\infty}\int_{|z_{\parallel}|=\sqrt{t^{2}-x_{3}^{2}}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}(1+|v|^{3+\delta})^{-1}\frac{x_{3}}{\sqrt{t^{2}-x_{3}^{2}}}dv\frac{\mathrm{d}S_{z_{\parallel}}}{t}\\
\lesssim&\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f_{0}\|_{\infty}\left(\frac{x_{3}}{t}\right)\lesssim\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f(t)\|_{\infty}.\end{split}

(3.48)

Thus from (
3.42
), (
3.43
), (
3.44
), (
3.45
), (
3.46
), (
3.47
), (
3.48
), and together with (
3.32
)–(
3.34
), we have

\begin{split}|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}\eqref{Eestbulkpos}+\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}\eqref{Eestbdrypos}|\lesssim&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left\{\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{\infty}\right\},\\
|\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{3}}}\eqref{Eestbulkpos}+\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{3}}}\eqref{Eestbdrypos}|\lesssim&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left\{\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{\infty}\right\}.\end{split}

By the same argument we get the same estimate for
\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{k}}}\eqref{Eestbulkneg}+\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{k}}}\eqref{Eestbdryneg}
. Thus

\begin{split}&|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}\eqref{Eestbulkpos}+\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}\eqref{Eestbdrypos}|+|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}\eqref{Eestbulkneg}+\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}\eqref{Eestbdryneg}|\lesssim\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left\{\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{\infty}\right\},\\
&|\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{3}}}\eqref{Eestbulkpos}+\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{3}}}\eqref{Eestbdrypos}|+|\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{3}}}\eqref{Eestbulkneg}+\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{3}}}\eqref{Eestbdryneg}|\\
\lesssim&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left\{\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{\infty}\right\}.\end{split}

(3.49)

Next, by using the change of variables
z=y-x
and spherical coordinate for
z
, we have

\begin{split}\eqref{Eestinitialpos}=&\int_{|z|=t,\ z_{3}>-x_{3}}\sum_{j}\omega_{j}\left(\delta_{ij}-\frac{(\omega_{i}+\hat{v}_{i})\hat{v}_{j}}{1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega}\right)f(0,z+x,v)\mathrm{d}v\frac{\mathrm{d}S_{z}}{t}\\
=&\int_{\ t\cos\phi>-x_{3}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\sum_{j}\omega_{j}\left(\delta_{ij}-\frac{(\omega_{i}+\hat{v}_{i})\hat{v}_{j}}{1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega}\right)f(0,z+x,v)\mathrm{d}v\frac{t^{2}\sin\phi\,\mathrm{d}\theta\mathrm{d}\phi}{t}.\end{split}

(3.50)

Thus

\begin{split}&\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{k}}}\eqref{Eestinitialpos}\\
=&-\int_{\ t\cos\phi>-x_{3}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\sum_{j}\omega_{j}\left(\delta_{ij}-\frac{(\omega_{i}+\hat{v}_{i})\hat{v}_{j}}{1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega}\right)\partial_{x_{k}}f(0,z+x,v)(t\sin\phi)\,\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}\theta\mathrm{d}\phi\\
&-\delta_{k3}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\sum_{j}\omega_{j}\left(\delta_{ij}-\frac{(\omega_{i}+\hat{v}_{i})\hat{v}_{j}}{1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega}\right)f(0,z_{\parallel}+x_{\parallel},0,v)\left(\frac{d}{dx_{3}}\cos^{-1}\left(\frac{-x_{3}}{t}\right)\right)\left(t\sin\left(\cos^{-1}\left(\frac{-x_{3}}{t}\right)\right)\right)\,\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}\theta\\
=&-\int_{\ t\cos\phi>-x_{3}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\sum_{j}\omega_{j}\left(\delta_{ij}-\frac{(\omega_{i}+\hat{v}_{i})\hat{v}_{j}}{1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega}\right)\partial_{x_{k}}f(0,z+x,v)(t\sin\phi)\,\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}\theta\mathrm{d}\phi\\
&-\delta_{k3}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\sum_{j}\omega_{j}\left(\delta_{ij}-\frac{(\omega_{i}+\hat{v}_{i})\hat{v}_{j}}{1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega}\right)f(0,z_{\parallel}+x_{\parallel},0,v)\frac{-1}{\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{x_{3}}{t}\right)^{2}}}\frac{-1}{t}\left(t\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{x_{3}}{t}\right)^{2}}\right)\,\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}\theta.\end{split}

(3.51)

So from (
3.11
), for
k=1,2
,

\begin{split}&|\int_{\ t\cos\phi>-x_{3}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\sum_{j}\omega_{j}\left(\delta_{ij}-\frac{(\omega_{i}+\hat{v}_{i})\hat{v}_{j}}{1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega}\right)\partial_{x_{k}}f(0,z+x,v)(t\sin\phi)\,\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}\theta\mathrm{d}\phi|\\
\lesssim&\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f_{0}\|_{\infty}\int_{\ t\cos\phi>-x_{3}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\langle v\rangle^{-4-\delta}(t\sin\phi)\,\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}\theta\mathrm{d}\phi\\
\lesssim&\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f_{0}\|_{\infty}.\end{split}

(3.52)

And for
k=3
,

\begin{split}&|\int_{\ t\cos\phi>-x_{3}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\sum_{j}\omega_{j}\left(\delta_{ij}-\frac{(\omega_{i}+\hat{v}_{i})\hat{v}_{j}}{1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega}\right)\partial_{x_{3}}f(0,z+x,v)(t\sin\phi)\,\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}\theta\mathrm{d}\phi|\\
\lesssim&\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f_{0}\|_{\infty}\int_{\ t\cos\phi>-x_{3}}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{\langle v\rangle^{-4-\delta}}{\alpha(0,z+x,v)}(t\sin\phi)\,\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}\theta\mathrm{d}\phi\\
\lesssim&\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f_{0}\|_{\infty}\int_{\ t\cos\phi>-x_{3}}t\sin\phi|\ln(t\cos\phi+x_{3})|d\phi\\
\lesssim&\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f_{0}\|_{\infty}\int_{0}^{t+x_{3}}|\ln(s)|ds\lesssim\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha f_{0}\|_{\infty},\end{split}

(3.53)

and

\begin{split}|\int_{0}^{2\pi}\sum_{j}\omega_{j}&\left(\delta_{ij}-\frac{(\omega_{i}+\hat{v}_{i})\hat{v}_{j}}{1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega}\right)f(0,z_{\parallel}+x_{\parallel},0,v)\frac{-1}{\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{x_{3}}{t}\right)^{2}}}\frac{-1}{t}\left(t\sqrt{1-\left(\frac{x_{3}}{t}\right)^{2}}\right)\,\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}\theta|\\
=&|\int_{0}^{2\pi}\sum_{j}\omega_{j}\left(\delta_{ij}-\frac{(\omega_{i}+\hat{v}_{i})\hat{v}_{j}}{1+\hat{v}\cdot\omega}\right)f(0,z_{\parallel}+x_{\parallel},0,v)\,\mathrm{d}v\mathrm{d}\theta|\lesssim\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f(0)\|_{\infty}.\end{split}

(3.54)

Therefore, we have

\begin{split}|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}\eqref{Eestinitialpos}|\lesssim&\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f_{0}\|_{\infty},\\
|\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{3}}}\eqref{Eestinitialpos}|\lesssim&\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f_{0}\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f(0)\|_{\infty}.\end{split}

And by the same argument we have the same estimate for
\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{k}}}\eqref{Eestinitialneg}
. Thus

\begin{split}|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}\eqref{Eestinitialpos}|+|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}\eqref{Eestinitialneg}|\lesssim&\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f_{0}\|_{\infty},\\
|\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{3}}}\eqref{Eestinitialpos}|+|\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{3}}}\eqref{Eestinitialneg}|\lesssim&\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f_{0}\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f(0)\|_{\infty}.\end{split}

(3.55)

Finally, we estimate
\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{k}}\eqref{Eest3bdrycontri}
. We have

\begin{split}&|\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{k}}\eqref{Eest3bdrycontri}|\\
&\leq 2|\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{k}}\int_{\sqrt{|z_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2}}<t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{f(t-\sqrt{|z_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2}},x_{\parallel}+z_{\parallel},0,v)}{\sqrt{|z_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2}}}dvdz_{\parallel}|\\
&\lesssim(1-\delta_{k3})\int_{\sqrt{t^{2}-|z_{\parallel}|^{2}}>x_{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\partial_{x_{k}}f(t-(|z_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})^{1/2},z_{\parallel}+x_{\parallel},0,v)|\mathrm{d}v\frac{\mathrm{d}z_{\parallel}}{(|z_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})^{1/2}}\\
&+\delta_{k3}\int_{\sqrt{t^{2}-|z_{\parallel}|^{2}}>x_{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|f(t-(|z_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})^{1/2},z_{\parallel}+x_{\parallel},0,v)\mathrm{d}v\ \partial_{x_{3}}\left(\frac{1}{(|z_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})^{1/2}}\right)|\mathrm{d}z_{\parallel}\\
&+\delta_{k3}\int_{\sqrt{t^{2}-|z_{\parallel}|^{2}}>x_{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\partial_{t}f(t-(|z_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})^{1/2},z_{\parallel}+x_{\parallel},0,v)\mathrm{d}v\frac{-x_{3}}{(|z_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})}|\mathrm{d}z_{\parallel}\\
&-\delta_{k3}\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\frac{|f(0,z_{\parallel}+x_{\parallel},0,v)|}{t}\mathrm{d}v\frac{x_{3}}{\sqrt{t^{2}-x_{3}^{2}}}\sqrt{t^{2}-x_{3}^{2}}d\theta.\end{split}

Similar to the estimate in (
3.42
)-(
3.48
), we get

\int_{\sqrt{t^{2}-|z_{\parallel}|^{2}}>x_{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\partial_{x_{k}}f(t-(|z_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})^{1/2},z_{\parallel}+x_{\parallel},0,v)|\mathrm{d}v\frac{\mathrm{d}z_{\parallel}}{(|z_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})^{1/2}}\lesssim\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|(1+|v|^{4+\delta})\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{\infty},

\begin{split}&\int_{\sqrt{t^{2}-|z_{\parallel}|^{2}}>x_{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|f(t-(|z_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})^{1/2},z_{\parallel}+x_{\parallel},0,v)\mathrm{d}v\ \partial_{x_{3}}\left(\frac{1}{(|z_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})^{1/2}}\right)|\mathrm{d}z_{\parallel}\\
&+\int_{0}^{2\pi}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\frac{|f(0,z_{\parallel}+x_{\parallel},0,v)|}{t}\mathrm{d}v\frac{x_{3}}{\sqrt{t^{2}-x_{3}^{2}}}\sqrt{t^{2}-x_{3}^{2}}d\theta\lesssim\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f(t)\|_{\infty}.\end{split}

And

\begin{split}\int_{\sqrt{t^{2}-|z_{\parallel}|^{2}}>x_{3}}&\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\partial_{t}f(t-(|z_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})^{1/2},z_{\parallel}+x_{\parallel},0,v)\mathrm{d}v\frac{-x_{3}}{(|z_{\parallel}|^{2}+x_{3}^{2})}|\mathrm{d}z_{\parallel}\\
\lesssim&\left(\|(1+|v|^{5+\delta})\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(t)\|_{\infty}+\|(1+|v|^{4+\delta})\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{\infty}\right)+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|(1+|v|^{4+\delta})f(t)\|_{\infty}.\end{split}

Therefore

\begin{split}|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}\eqref{Eest3bdrycontri}|\lesssim&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left\{\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{\infty}\right\},\\
|\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{3}}\eqref{Eest3bdrycontri}|\lesssim&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left\{\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\nabla_{x}f(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{\infty}\right\}.\end{split}

(3.56)

Next, we estimate the
\partial_{x_{k}}
derivatives to
B
. Using the same argument as in (
3.28
)–(
3.31
), we get

|\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{k}}}\eqref{Besttat0pos}|+|\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{k}}}\eqref{Besttat0neg}|\lesssim\|E_{0}\|_{C^{2}}.

(3.57)

Next, using the decay of the kernel in (
3.16
), and following the same argument as in (
3.32
)–(
3.34
), and (
3.42
)–(
3.49
),
we obtain

\begin{split}&|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}\eqref{Bestbulkpos}+\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}\eqref{Bestbdrypos}|+|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}\eqref{Bestbulkneg}+\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}\eqref{Bestbdryneg}|\lesssim\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{\infty}\\
&|\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{3}}}\eqref{Bestbulkpos}+\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{3}}}\eqref{Bestbdrypos}|+|\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{3}}}\eqref{Bestbulkneg}+\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{3}}}\eqref{Bestbdryneg}|\\
\lesssim&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|(1+|v|^{5+\delta})\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(t)\|_{\infty}+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f(t)\|_{\infty}|.\end{split}

(3.58)

Next, from (
3.59
) we have

\begin{split}\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{k}}\eqref{BestSpos}=\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{k}}\left(\int_{B(x;t)\cap\{y_{3}>0\}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mathcal{S}^{B}_{i}(v,\omega)\cdot(E+E_{\text{ext}}+\hat{v}\times(B+B_{\text{ext}})-g\mathbf{e}_{3})f(t-|y-x|,y,v)\mathrm{d}v\frac{\mathrm{d}y}{|y-x|}\right),\end{split}

(3.59)

where
\mathcal{S}^{B}_{i}(v,\omega)
as in (
3.18
). Then using the bound for
\mathcal{S}^{B}_{i}(v,\omega)
in (
3.19
) and applying the same argument as in (
3.35
)–(
3.41
), we obtain

\begin{split}|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}\eqref{BestSpos}|+|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}\eqref{BestSneg}|\lesssim&t^{2}\left(\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}E(t)\|_{\infty}+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}B(t)\|_{\infty}\right)+\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{\infty}\\
|\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{3}}}\eqref{BestSpos}|+|\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{3}}}\eqref{BestSneg}|\lesssim&t^{2}\left(\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\partial_{x_{3}}E(t)\|_{\infty}+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\partial_{x_{3}}B(t)\|_{\infty}\right)\\
&+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(t)\|_{\infty}\right)+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|(1+|v|^{4+\delta})f(t)\|_{\infty}.\end{split}

(3.60)

Next, using the same argument as in (
3.50
)–(
3.55
) together with (
3.20
), we have

\begin{split}|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}\eqref{Bestinitialpos}|+|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}\eqref{Bestinitialneg}|\lesssim&\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f_{0}\|_{\infty},\\
|\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{3}}}\eqref{Bestinitialpos}|+|\frac{\partial}{\partial_{x_{3}}}\eqref{Bestinitialneg}|\lesssim&\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f_{0}\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f(0)\|_{\infty}.\end{split}

(3.61)

Finally, similar (
3.56
), we have

\begin{split}|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}\eqref{Bestbdrycontri}|\lesssim&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left\{\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{\infty}\right\},\\
|\frac{\partial}{\partial x_{3}}\eqref{Bestbdrycontri}|\lesssim&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\big\{\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f(t)\|_{\infty}(1+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|E(t)\|_{\infty}+\|B(t)\|_{\infty}\right))\\
&\quad\quad\quad\quad+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\nabla_{x}f(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{\infty}\big\}.\end{split}

(3.62)

Collecting (
3.32
), (
3.41
), (
3.49
), (
3.55
), and (
3.56
), and (
3.31
)–(
3.62
), and letting
T\ll 1
, we get

\begin{split}\|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}E\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}B\|_{\infty}\lesssim&\|E_{0}\|_{C^{2}}+\|B_{0}\|_{C^{2}}+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{\infty}.\\
\|\partial_{x_{3}}E\|_{\infty}+\|\partial_{x_{3}}B\|_{\infty}\lesssim&\|E_{0}\|_{C^{2}}+\|B_{0}\|_{C^{2}}+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\left(\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(t)\|_{\infty}+\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{\infty}\right)\\
&+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f(t)\|_{\infty}.\end{split}

(3.63)

This concludes (
3.25
) and (
3.26
).

For
\partial_{t}E
, and
\partial_{t}B
, from the Maxwell equations (
0.21
), we have

\|\partial_{t}E\|_{\infty}\leq\|\nabla_{x}B\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f\|_{\infty},\ \|\partial_{t}B\|_{\infty}\leq\|\nabla_{x}E\|_{\infty}.

So from (
3.25
), (
3.26
), we get (
3.27
).

∎

4.
Estimates on trajectories

We have the following crucial lemma:

Lemma 10

(Velocity lemma)
.

Let
\alpha
be defined as in (
0.57
). Suppose

\begin{split}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}&\left(\|E(t)\|_{\infty}+\|B(t)\|_{\infty}\right.\\
&\left.+\|\partial_{t}E_{3}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\partial_{t}(\hat{v}\times B)_{3}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla_{x}E_{3}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla_{x}(\hat{v}\times B)_{3}(t)\|_{\infty}\right)+g+B_{e}<C.\end{split}

(4.1)

And for all
t,x_{\parallel}
,

g-E_{e}-E_{3}(t,x_{\parallel},0)-(\hat{v}\times B)_{3}(t,x_{\parallel},0)>c_{0}\text{ for some }c_{0}>0.

(4.2)

Then for any
(t,x,v)\in(0,T)\times\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
, with the trajectory
X(s;t,x,v)
and
V(s;t,x,v)
satisfies (
0.54
),

e^{-10\frac{C}{c_{0}}|t-s|}\alpha(t,x,v)\leq\alpha(s,X(s;t,x,v),V(s;t,x,v))\leq e^{10\frac{C}{c_{0}}|t-s|}\alpha(t,x,v).

(4.3)

Proof.

Note that

\displaystyle\frac{\partial\hat{v}_{3}}{\partial v_{3}}=\frac{1}{\langle v\rangle}-\frac{(\hat{v}_{3})^{2}}{\langle v\rangle},\ \nabla_{v}\left(\frac{1}{\langle v\rangle}\right)=-\frac{\hat{v}}{\langle v\rangle^{2}}.

By direct computation,

\displaystyle[\partial_{t}

\displaystyle+\hat{v}\cdot\nabla_{x}+\mathfrak{F}\cdot\nabla_{v}](\alpha^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-2\frac{\hat{v}_{3}}{\langle v\rangle}\mathfrak{F}_{3}(t,x_{\parallel},0,v)+2\frac{\hat{v}_{3}}{\langle v\rangle}\mathfrak{F}_{3}(t,x,v)

(4.4)

\displaystyle+2\left(\partial_{t}\mathfrak{F}_{3}(t,x_{\parallel},0,v)\right)\frac{x_{3}}{\langle v\rangle}+2\hat{v}_{3}x_{3}-(2\hat{v}_{\parallel}\cdot\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}\mathfrak{F}_{3}(t,x_{\parallel},0,v))\frac{x_{3}}{\langle v\rangle}

\displaystyle-2\frac{(\hat{v}_{3})^{3}}{\langle v\rangle}\mathfrak{F}_{3}(t,x,v)-2(\hat{v}_{3})^{2}\frac{\hat{v}_{\parallel}}{\langle v\rangle}\cdot\mathfrak{F}_{\parallel}(t,x,v)-2\frac{x_{3}}{\langle v\rangle}\mathfrak{F}(t,x,v)\cdot\nabla_{v}\mathfrak{F}_{3}(t,x_{\parallel},0,v)

\displaystyle+2x_{3}\frac{\hat{v}}{\langle v\rangle^{2}}\cdot\mathfrak{F}(t,x,v)\mathfrak{F}_{3}(t,x_{\parallel},0,v).

(4.5)

Using the fundamental theorem of calculus

\eqref{diffalpha2main}=2\frac{\hat{v}_{3}}{\langle v\rangle}\left(\int_{0}^{x_{3}}\partial_{x_{3}}\mathfrak{F}_{3}(t,x_{\parallel},s,v)ds\right).

Since
\mathfrak{F}_{3}=E_{3}+E_{e}+\hat{v}_{1}B_{2}-\hat{v}_{2}B_{1}-g
, and since
\hat{v}\cdot\mathfrak{F}=\hat{v}\cdot(E+E_{\text{ext}}+\hat{v}\times(B_{3}+B_{\text{ext}})-g\mathbf{e}_{3})=\hat{v}\cdot E-(g-E_{e})\hat{v}_{3}
, we have

\begin{split}[\partial_{t}&+\hat{v}\cdot\nabla_{x}+\mathfrak{F}\cdot\nabla_{v}](\alpha^{2})\\
=&2\frac{\hat{v}_{3}}{\langle v\rangle}\left(\int_{0}^{x_{3}}\partial_{x_{3}}E_{3}(t,x_{\parallel},s)+\hat{v}_{1}\partial_{x_{3}}B_{2}(t,x_{\parallel},s)-\hat{v}_{2}\partial_{x_{3}}B_{1}(t,x_{\parallel},s)ds\right)\\
&+2\frac{x_{3}}{\langle v\rangle}\left((\partial_{t}-\hat{v}_{\parallel}\cdot\nabla_{x_{\parallel}})\left(E_{3}(t,x_{\parallel},0)+\hat{v}_{1}B_{2}(t,x_{\parallel},0)-\hat{v}_{2}B_{1}(t,x_{\parallel},0)\right)\right)+2\hat{v}_{3}x_{3}\\
&-2\frac{(\hat{v}_{3})^{2}}{\langle v\rangle}\left(\hat{v}\cdot E(t,x,v)-(g-E_{e})\hat{v}_{3}\right)\\
&-2\frac{x_{3}}{\langle v\rangle}\left(E+E_{\text{ext}}+(\hat{v}\times(B+B_{\text{ext}})-g\mathbf{e}_{3})\right)\cdot\nabla_{v}(\hat{v}_{1}B_{2}(t,x_{\parallel},0)-\hat{v}_{2}B_{1}(t,x_{\parallel},0))\\
&+2x_{3}\frac{\hat{v}\cdot E-(g-E_{e})\hat{v}_{3}}{\langle v\rangle^{2}}\left(E_{3}(t,x_{\parallel},0)+\hat{v}_{1}B_{2}(t,x_{\parallel},0)-\hat{v}_{2}B_{1}(t,x_{\parallel},0)-g\right)\\
&\underbrace{-2\frac{x_{3}}{\langle v\rangle}(\hat{v}\times B_{\text{ext}})\cdot\nabla_{v}(\hat{v}_{1}B_{2}(t,x_{\parallel},0)-\hat{v}_{2}B_{1}(t,x_{\parallel},0))}_{\eqref{diffalpha3}_{1}}.\end{split}

(4.6)

Now from the assumptions (
4.1
) and (
4.2
), all the terms on the RHS of (
4.6
) except
\eqref{diffalpha3}_{1}
can be bounded by

\frac{C_{1}}{c_{0}}\left(c_{0}\frac{x_{3}}{\langle v\rangle}+(x_{3})^{2}+(\hat{v}_{3})^{2}\right),

(4.7)

where
C_{1}=\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|E(t)\|_{\infty}+\|B(t)\|_{\infty}+\|E_{3}(t)\|_{W^{1,\infty}}+\|B_{1}(t)\|_{W^{1,\infty}}+\|B_{2}(t)\|_{W^{1,\infty}}\right)+E_{e}+g
. And from direct computation,

\begin{split}\eqref{diffalpha3}_{1}=&-2B_{e}\frac{x_{3}}{\langle v\rangle^{2}}\begin{bmatrix}\hat{v}_{2}\\
-\hat{v}_{1}\\
0\end{bmatrix}\cdot\left(\begin{bmatrix}1-\hat{v}_{1}^{2}\\
-\hat{v}_{1}\hat{v}_{2}\\
-\hat{v}_{1}\hat{v}_{3}\end{bmatrix}B_{2}(t,x_{\parallel},0)-\begin{bmatrix}-\hat{v}_{2}\hat{v}_{1}\\
1-\hat{v}_{2}^{2}\\
-\hat{v}_{2}\hat{v}_{3}\end{bmatrix}B_{1}(t,x_{\parallel},0)\right)\\
=&-2B_{e}\frac{x_{3}}{\langle v\rangle^{2}}\left(\hat{v}_{2}B_{2}(t,x_{\parallel},0)+\hat{v}_{1}B_{1}(t,x_{\parallel},0),\right)\end{split}

thus from (
4.2
)

|\eqref{diffalpha3}_{1}|\leq\frac{B_{e}}{c_{0}}c_{0}\frac{x_{3}}{\langle v\rangle}.

(4.8)

Combining (
4.7
) and (
4.8
), we get

\begin{split}|[\partial_{t}&+\hat{v}\cdot\nabla_{x}+\mathfrak{F}\cdot\nabla_{v}](\alpha^{2})|\leq 10(\frac{C_{1}+B_{e}}{c_{0}})c_{0}\frac{x_{3}}{\langle v\rangle}+8C_{1}\left((x_{3})^{2}+(\hat{v}_{3})^{2}\right).\end{split}

(4.9)

From the expression of
\alpha
in (
0.57
) and the assumption (
4.2
), this yields

|[\partial_{t}+\hat{v}\cdot\nabla_{x}+\mathfrak{F}\cdot\nabla_{v}](\alpha^{2})|\leq 20(\frac{C_{1}+B_{e}}{c_{0}})\alpha^{2},

(4.10)

Thus along the characteristics, by the Gröwall’s inequality we get

e^{-20(\frac{C_{1}+B_{e}}{c_{0}})|t-s|}\alpha^{2}(t,x,v)\leq\alpha^{2}(s,X(s;t,x,v),V(s;t,x,v))\leq e^{20(\frac{C_{1}+B_{e}}{c_{0}})|t-s|}\alpha^{2}(t,x,v).

(4.11)

Taking square root we get (
4.3
).
∎

Lemma 11
.

Let
\alpha
be defined as in (
0.57
). Then for any
(t,x)\in[0,T)\times\Omega
, we have

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{|v|\leq M}}{\alpha(t,x,v)}dv\leq 4M^{3}\ln\left(1+\frac{1}{x_{3}}\right),

(4.12)

and

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{1+|v|^{4+\delta}}\frac{1}{\alpha(t,x,v)}dv\leq C_{\delta}\ln\left(1+\frac{1}{x_{3}}\right).

(4.13)

Proof.

From (
0.57
) we have

\begin{split}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{|v|\leq M}}{\alpha(t,x,v)}dv=&\int_{|v|\leq M}\left((x_{3})^{2}+(\hat{v}_{3})^{2}-2\left(E_{3}(t,x_{\parallel},0)+E_{e}+(\hat{v}\times B)_{3}(t,x_{\parallel},0)-g\right)\frac{x_{3}}{\langle v\rangle}\right)^{-1/2}dv\\
\leq&\int_{|v|\leq M}\frac{2}{x_{3}+\frac{|v_{3}|}{\langle v\rangle}}dv\\
\leq&\int_{|v_{3}|\leq M}\frac{2M^{2}}{x_{3}+\frac{|v_{3}|}{M}}dv_{3}\\
=&4M^{3}\ln\left(x_{3}+\frac{v_{3}}{M}\right)\big\rvert_{0}^{M}=4M^{3}\ln\left(1+\frac{1}{x_{3}}\right).\end{split}

Now, for (
4.13
), we have

\begin{split}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{1+|v|^{4+\delta}}\frac{1}{\alpha(t,x,v)}dv\leq&\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{1+|v|^{4+\delta}}\frac{2}{x_{3}+\frac{|v_{3}|}{\langle v\rangle}}dv\\
\lesssim&\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{\left(1+|v|^{3+\delta}\right)\left(x_{3}+|v_{3}|\right)}dv.\end{split}

(4.14)

Using the spherical coordinate
v=(r,\theta,\phi)
we have
dv=r^{2}\sin\phi\,drd\theta d\phi
,
v_{3}=r\cos\phi
, and

\begin{split}&\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\frac{1}{\left(1+|v|^{3+\delta}\right)\left(x_{3}+|v_{3}|\right)}dv\\
=&4\pi\int_{0}^{\infty}\int_{0}^{\pi/2}\frac{r^{2}\sin\phi}{(1+r^{3+\delta})(x_{3}+r\cos\phi)}d\phi dr\\
=&4\pi\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{\pi/2}\frac{r^{2}\sin\phi}{(1+r^{3+\delta})(x_{3}+r\cos\phi)}d\phi dr+4\pi\int_{1}^{\infty}\int_{0}^{\pi/2}\frac{r^{2}\sin\phi}{(1+r^{3+\delta})(x_{3}+r\cos\phi)}d\phi dr.\end{split}

(4.15)

Using change of variables
r\cos\phi=u
,
-r\sin\phi\,d\phi=du
, we have

\begin{split}&\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{\pi/2}\frac{r^{2}\sin\phi}{(1+r^{3+\delta})(x_{3}+r\cos\phi)}d\phi dr\\
\leq&\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{\pi/2}\frac{r^{2}\sin\phi}{x_{3}+r\cos\phi}d\phi dr\\
=&\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{r}\frac{r}{x_{3}+u}dudr=\int_{0}^{1}r\ln\left(1+\frac{r}{x_{3}}\right)dr<\ln\left(1+\frac{1}{x_{3}}\right).\end{split}

(4.16)

And using change of variables
\cos\phi=u
,
-\sin\phi\,d\phi=du
, we have

\begin{split}&\int_{1}^{\infty}\int_{0}^{\pi/2}\frac{r^{2}\sin\phi}{(1+r^{3+\delta})(x_{3}+r\cos\phi)}d\phi dr\\
\leq&C_{\delta}\int_{0}^{\pi/2}\frac{\sin\phi}{x_{3}+\cos\phi}d\phi\\
=&C_{\delta}\int_{0}^{1}\frac{1}{x_{3}+u}du=C_{\delta}\ln\left(1+\frac{1}{x_{3}}\right).\end{split}

(4.17)

Combining (
4.14
), (
4.15
), (
4.16
), and (
4.17
) we conclude (
4.13
).

∎

We have the following estimate on the backward exit time
t_{\mathbf{b}}
for the trajectory.

Lemma 12
.

Let
(t,x,v)\in(0,T)\times\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
, and the trajectory
X(s;t,x,v)
and
V(s;t,x,v)
satisfies (
0.54
). Extending
E(t)=E_{0}
,
B(t)=B_{0}
for
t<0
. Suppose for all
t,x,v
,

g-E_{e}-E_{3}(t,x_{\parallel},0)-(\hat{v}\times B)_{3}(t,x_{\parallel},0)>c_{0},

(4.18)

then there exists a
C
depending on
T
,
g
,
B_{e}
,
\|E\|_{W^{1,\infty}((0,T)\times\Omega)}
,
\|B\|_{W^{1,\infty}((0,T)\times\Omega)}
such that

\frac{t_{\mathbf{b}}(t,x,v)}{\sup_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}<s<t}\sqrt{1+|V(s)|^{2}}}\leq\frac{C}{c_{0}}{\hat{v}_{\mathbf{b}}}_{,3}.

(4.19)

If
t-t_{\mathbf{b}}(t,x,v)\leq 0
, then

\frac{t}{\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\sqrt{1+|V(s)|^{2}}}\leq\frac{C}{c_{0}}\alpha(0,X(0),V(0)).

(4.20)

Proof.

We first prove (
4.19
). For any
(t,x,v)\in(0,T)\times\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
with
t-t_{\mathbf{b}}(t,x,v)>0
, we have

{v_{\mathbf{b}}}_{,3}-v_{3}=\int_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}^{t}-\mathfrak{F}_{3}(s,X(s;t,x,v),V(s;t,x,v))ds.

From (
4.18
) this implies

\int_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}^{t}c_{0}ds<\int_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}^{t}-\mathfrak{F}_{3}(s,X(s;t,x,v),V(s;t,x,v))ds\leq|{v_{\mathbf{b}}}_{,3}|+|v_{3}|

(4.21)

On the other hand, from (
4.3
),

|{v_{\mathbf{b}}}_{,3}|+|v_{3}|=\langle v_{\mathbf{b}}\rangle|\hat{{v_{\mathbf{b}}}}_{,3}|+\langle v\rangle|\hat{v}_{3}|\leq\sup_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}<s<t}\langle V(s)\rangle\left(\hat{{v_{\mathbf{b}}}}_{,3}+\alpha(t,x,v)\right)<C\sup_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}<s<t}\langle V(s)\rangle\hat{{v_{\mathbf{b}}}}_{,3}.

(4.22)

Combining (
4.21
) and (
4.22
) we get

t_{\mathbf{b}}c_{0}<C\sup_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}<s<t}\langle V(s)\rangle\hat{{v_{\mathbf{b}}}}_{,3}.

This implies (
4.19
).

For
t-t_{\mathbf{b}}(t,x,v)\leq 0
, using the same argument we have,

\begin{split}c_{0}t<\int_{0}^{t}-\mathfrak{F}_{3}(s,X(s),V(s))ds\leq&|V_{3}(0)|+|v_{3}|\end{split}

and from (
4.3
),

|V_{3}(0)|+|v_{3}|\leq\sup_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}<s<t}\langle V(s)\rangle\left(\alpha(0,X(0),V(0))+\alpha(t,x,v)\right)<C\sup_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}<s<t}\langle V(s)\rangle\alpha(0,X(0),V(0)),

thus we get

c_{0}t<C\sup_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}<s<t}\langle V(s)\rangle\alpha(0,X(0),V(0)),

and this yields (
4.20
).

∎

Lemma 13
.

Suppose

\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\nabla_{x}E(t)\|_{\infty}+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\nabla_{x}B(t)\|_{\infty}<\infty.

Then for any
s,t\in(0,T)
, we have

\begin{split}&|\partial_{x_{i}}X(s;t,x,v)|\lesssim e^{C_{1}|t-s|},\\
&|\partial_{x_{i}}V(s;t,x,v)|\lesssim(t-s)e^{C_{1}|t-s|},\\
&|\partial_{v_{i}}X(s;t,x,v)|\lesssim\frac{|t-s|}{\langle V(s)\rangle}e^{C_{1}|t-s|},\\
&|\partial_{v_{i}}V(s;t,x,v)|\lesssim e^{C_{1}|t-s|},\end{split}

(4.23)

and for
i\neq j
,

\begin{split}|\partial_{x_{i}}X_{j}(s;t,x,v)|\lesssim e^{C_{1}|t-s|}\frac{|t-s|^{2}}{\langle V(s)\rangle}.\end{split}

(4.24)

where
C_{1}=\left(\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\nabla_{x}E(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla_{x}B(t)\|_{\infty}+\|E(t)\|_{\infty}+\|B(t)\|_{\infty}\right)+g+|B_{e}|\right)^{2}
.

Proof.

The expressions of
X(s;t,x,v)
and
V(s;t,x,v)
are

\begin{split}X(s;t,x,v)=&x-(t-s)\hat{v}+\int^{t}_{s}\int^{t}_{\tau}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}(\tau^{\prime},X(\tau^{\prime}),V(\tau^{\prime}))\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}\mathrm{d}\tau,\\
V(s;t,x,v)=&v-\int_{s}^{t}\mathfrak{F}(\tau,X(\tau),V(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau.\end{split}

(4.25)

We denote

\begin{split}\frac{d}{ds}\hat{V}(s)&=\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+|V(s)|^{2}}}\frac{d}{ds}V(s)-\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+|V(s)|^{2}}}\hat{V}(s)\cdot\frac{d}{ds}V(s)\hat{V}(s)\\
&=\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+|V(s)|^{2}}}\mathfrak{F}(s,X(s),V(s))-\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+|V(s)|^{2}}}\hat{V}(s)\cdot\mathfrak{F}(s,X(s),V(s))\hat{V}(s)\\
&:=\hat{\mathfrak{F}}(s,X(s),V(s)).\end{split}

(4.26)

By direct computation we get

\begin{split}\partial_{x_{i}}X(s;t,x,v)=&e_{i}+\int^{t}_{s}\int^{t}_{\tau}[\nabla_{x}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}(\tau^{\prime})\cdot\partial_{x_{i}}X(\tau^{\prime})+\nabla_{v}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}(\tau^{\prime})\cdot\partial_{x_{i}}V(\tau^{\prime})]\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}\mathrm{d}\tau,\\
\partial_{v_{i}}X(s;t,x,v)=&-(t-s)\partial_{v_{i}}\hat{v}+\int^{t}_{s}\int^{t}_{\tau}[\nabla_{x}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}(\tau^{\prime})\cdot\partial_{v_{i}}X(\tau^{\prime})+\nabla_{v}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}(\tau^{\prime})\cdot\partial_{v_{i}}V(\tau^{\prime})]\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}\mathrm{d}\tau,\\
\partial_{x_{i}}V(s;t,x,v)=&-\int_{s}^{t}[\nabla_{x}{\mathfrak{F}}(\tau^{\prime})\cdot\partial_{x_{i}}X(\tau)+\nabla_{v}{\mathfrak{F}}(\tau^{\prime})\cdot\partial_{x_{i}}V(\tau)]\mathrm{d}\tau\\
\partial_{v_{i}}V(s;t,x,v)=&e_{i}-\int_{s}^{t}[\nabla_{x}{\mathfrak{F}}(\tau^{\prime})\cdot\partial_{v_{i}}X(\tau)+\nabla_{v}{\mathfrak{F}}(\tau^{\prime})\cdot\partial_{v_{i}}V(\tau)]\mathrm{d}\tau.\end{split}

(4.27)

Since
\mathfrak{F}=E+E_{\text{ext}}+\hat{v}\times(B+B_{\text{ext}})-g\mathbf{e}_{3}
, we have

|\nabla_{x}\mathfrak{F}|\leq|\nabla_{x}E|+|\nabla_{x}B|.

(4.28)

And since
\partial_{v_{i}}\hat{v}=\frac{e_{i}}{\langle v\rangle}-\frac{\hat{v}_{i}\hat{v}}{\langle v\rangle}
,

|\nabla_{v}\mathfrak{F}|\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+|v|^{2}}}(|B|+|B_{e}|).

(4.29)

From the expression of
\hat{\mathfrak{F}}
in (
4.26
), we have from (
4.28
),

|\nabla_{x}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}|\leq\frac{2}{\sqrt{1+|v|^{2}}}|\nabla_{x}\mathfrak{F}|\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+|v|^{2}}}\left(|\nabla_{x}E|+|\nabla_{x}B|\right),

(4.30)

and from (
4.29
),

\begin{split}|\nabla_{v}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}|\leq&\left|\nabla_{v}(\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+|v|^{2}}})(\mathfrak{F}-\hat{v}\cdot\mathfrak{F}\cdot\hat{v})\right|+\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+|v|^{2}}}\left(\nabla_{v}\mathfrak{F}-\nabla_{v}(\hat{v}\cdot\mathfrak{F}\cdot\hat{v})\right)\right|\\
\lesssim&\frac{1}{1+|v|^{2}}|\mathfrak{F}|+\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+|v|^{2}}}|\nabla_{v}\mathfrak{F}|\\
\lesssim&\frac{1}{1+|v|^{2}}\left(|E|+|B|+g+|B_{e}|\right)\end{split}

(4.31)

From (
4.27
) and Fubini’s theorem,

\begin{split}\partial_{x_{i}}X(s)=&e_{i}+\int_{s}^{t}\int_{s}^{\tau^{\prime}}[\nabla_{x}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}(\tau^{\prime})\cdot\partial_{x_{i}}X(\tau^{\prime})+\nabla_{v}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}(\tau^{\prime})\cdot\partial_{x_{i}}V(\tau^{\prime})]\mathrm{d}\tau\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}\\
=&e_{i}+\int_{s}^{t}(\tau^{\prime}-s)[\nabla_{x}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}(\tau^{\prime})\cdot\partial_{x_{i}}X(\tau^{\prime})+\nabla_{v}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}(\tau^{\prime})\cdot\partial_{x_{i}}V(\tau^{\prime})]\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}.\end{split}

Therefore, from (
4.30
) and (
4.31
) we have

\begin{split}|\partial_{x_{i}}X(s)|\leq&1+(t-s)\int_{s}^{t}\left(|\nabla_{x}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}(\tau)||\partial_{x_{i}}X(\tau)|+|\nabla_{v}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}(\tau)||\partial_{x_{i}}V(\tau)|\right)\mathrm{d}\tau\\
\lesssim&1+(t-s)\left(\|\nabla_{x}E\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla_{x}B\|_{\infty}\right)\int_{s}^{t}\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+|V(\tau)|^{2}}}|\partial_{x_{i}}X(\tau)|d\tau\\
&\quad+(t-s)\left(\|E\|_{\infty}+\|B\|_{\infty}+g+|B_{e}|\right)\int_{s}^{t}\frac{1}{1+|V(\tau)|^{2}}|\partial_{x_{i}}V(\tau)|d\tau.\end{split}

(4.32)

Thus

\begin{split}&\langle V(s)\rangle|\partial_{x_{i}}X(s)|\\
\lesssim&\langle V(s)\rangle+(t-s)\left(\|\nabla_{x}E\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla_{x}B\|_{\infty}\right)\frac{\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\langle V(s)\rangle}{\inf_{0\leq s\leq t}\langle V(s)\rangle}\int_{s}^{t}\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+|V(\tau)|^{2}}}\langle V(\tau)\rangle|\partial_{x_{i}}X(\tau)|d\tau\\
&\quad+(t-s)\left(\|E\|_{\infty}+\|B\|_{\infty}+g+|B_{e}|\right)\frac{\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\langle V(s)\rangle}{\inf_{0\leq s\leq t}\langle V(s)\rangle}\int_{s}^{t}\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+|V(\tau)|^{2}}}|\partial_{x_{i}}V(\tau)|d\tau\\
\lesssim&\langle V(s)\rangle+C_{1}\int_{s}^{t}\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+|V(\tau)|^{2}}}\left(\langle V(\tau)\rangle|\partial_{x_{i}}X(\tau)|+|\partial_{x_{i}}V(\tau)|\right)d\tau,\end{split}

(4.33)

where
C_{1}=\left(\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\nabla_{x}E(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla_{x}B(t)\|_{\infty}+\|E(t)\|_{\infty}+\|B(t)\|_{\infty}\right)+g+|B_{e}|\right)^{2}
. From (
4.27
), (
4.28
), and (
4.29
),

\begin{split}|\partial_{x_{i}}V(s)|\lesssim&\left(\|\nabla_{x}E\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla_{x}B\|_{\infty}\right)\int_{s}^{t}\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+|V(\tau)|^{2}}}\langle V(\tau)\rangle|\partial_{x_{i}}X(\tau)|d\tau\\
&+(\|B\|_{\infty}+|B_{e}|)\int_{s}^{t}\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+|V(\tau)|^{2}}}|\partial_{x_{i}}V(\tau)|\mathrm{d}\tau\\
\lesssim&C_{1}\int_{s}^{t}\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+|V(\tau)|^{2}}}\left(\langle V(\tau)\rangle|\partial_{x_{i}}X(\tau)|+|\partial_{x_{i}}V(\tau)|\right)d\tau.\end{split}

(4.34)

Combine (
4.33
) and (
4.34
) we have

\langle V(s)\rangle|\partial_{x_{i}}X(s)|+|\partial_{x_{i}}V(s)|\lesssim\langle V(s)\rangle+C_{1}\int_{s}^{t}\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+|V(\tau)|^{2}}}\left(\langle V(\tau)\rangle|\partial_{x_{i}}X(\tau)|+|\partial_{x_{i}}V(\tau)|\right)d\tau.

(4.35)

So from Gronwall’s inequality,

\langle V(s)\rangle|\partial_{x_{i}}X(s)|+|\partial_{x_{i}}V(s)|\lesssim\langle V(s)\rangle e^{C_{1}\int_{s}^{t}\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+|V(\tau)|^{2}}}d\tau}\lesssim e^{C_{1}|t-s|}\langle V(s)\rangle,

(4.36)

Next, using the same argument as (
4.28
)–(
4.35
), from (
4.27
) we get

\begin{split}\langle V(s)\rangle|\partial_{v_{i}}X(s)|+|\partial_{v_{i}}V(s)|\lesssim 1+C_{1}\int_{s}^{t}\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+|V(\tau)|^{2}}}\left(\langle V(\tau)\rangle|\partial_{x_{i}}X(\tau)|+|\partial_{x_{i}}V(\tau)|\right)d\tau.\end{split}

Again by Gronwall’s inequality,

\langle V(s)\rangle|\partial_{v_{i}}X(s)|+|\partial_{v_{i}}V(s)|\lesssim e^{C_{1}|t-s|}.

(4.37)

Thus,

|\partial_{v_{i}}X(s;t,x,v)|\lesssim\frac{e^{C_{1}|t-s|}}{\langle V(s)\rangle},\,|\partial_{v_{i}}V(s;t,x,v)|\lesssim e^{C_{1}|t-s|}.

(4.38)

Now plug (
4.36
), (
4.38
) back to (
4.27
) and using (
4.30
), (
4.31
), and (
4.38
), we have

\begin{split}|\partial_{v_{i}}X(s;t,x,v)|&\lesssim\frac{|t-s|}{\langle v\rangle}+e^{C_{1}|t-s|}\int_{s}^{t}\int_{\tau}^{t}\frac{1}{\langle V(\tau^{\prime})\rangle^{2}}d\tau^{\prime}d\tau\\
&\lesssim\frac{|t-s|}{\langle V(s)\rangle}e^{C_{1}|t-s|},\end{split}

(4.39)

and

\begin{split}|\partial_{x_{i}}V(s;t,x,v)|&\lesssim e^{C_{1}|t-s|}\int_{s}^{t}\frac{1}{\langle V(\tau)\rangle}\langle V(\tau)\rangle\mathrm{d}\tau\lesssim|t-s|e^{C_{1}|t-s|}\end{split}

(4.40)

From (
4.36
), (
4.38
), and (
4.39
) we conclude (
4.23
). Finally, for
i\neq j
, from (
4.27
), (
4.30
), and (
4.31
),

|\partial_{x_{i}}X_{j}(s;t,x,v)|\lesssim e^{C_{1}|t-s|}\int_{s}^{t}\int_{\tau}^{t}\frac{1}{\langle V(\tau^{\prime})\rangle}d\tau^{\prime}d\tau\lesssim e^{C_{1}|t-s|}\frac{|t-s|^{2}}{\langle V(s)\rangle}.

∎

5.
W^{1,\infty}
estimate of inflow problem

In this section, we prove an a priori estimate for the inflow problem (
0.40
), (
0.25
).
From (
0.54
), we have

f(t,x,v)=\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}\geq t}f(0,X(0),V(0))+\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}<t}g(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}}).

(5.1)

From (
0.54
), we have

\begin{split}X_{i}(s;t,x,v)&=x_{i}-\int^{t}_{s}\hat{V}_{i}(\tau;t,x,v)\mathrm{d}\tau\\
&=x_{i}-\int^{t}_{s}\left\{\hat{v}_{i}-\int^{t}_{\tau}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}_{i}(\tau^{\prime})\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}\right\}\mathrm{d}\tau\\
&=x_{i}-(t-s)\hat{v}_{i}+\int^{t}_{s}\int^{t}_{\tau}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}_{i}(\tau^{\prime},X(\tau^{\prime}),V(\tau^{\prime}))\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}\mathrm{d}\tau.\end{split}

(5.2)

Set
s=t-t_{\mathbf{b}}
so that
X_{3}(t-t_{\mathbf{b}};t,x,v)=0
. Then

\begin{split}t_{\mathbf{b}}\hat{v}_{3}&=x_{3}+\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\int^{t}_{\tau}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau^{\prime})\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}\mathrm{d}\tau\\
\end{split}

(5.3)

By taking derivatives we obtain

\begin{split}\partial_{x}x_{3}+\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\int^{t}_{\tau}\partial_{x}[\hat{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau^{\prime},X(\tau^{\prime}),V(\tau^{\prime}))]\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}\mathrm{d}\tau=&\Big(\hat{v}_{3}-\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau^{\prime})\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}\Big)\partial_{x}t_{\mathbf{b}}\\
=&\ {\hat{v}_{\mathbf{b}}}_{,3}\partial_{x}t_{\mathbf{b}},\end{split}

(5.4)

and hence

\begin{split}\partial_{x_{i}}t_{\mathbf{b}}=&\frac{1}{{\hat{v}_{\mathbf{b}}}_{,3}}\left\{\partial_{x_{i}}x_{3}+\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\int^{t}_{\tau}\partial_{x_{i}}[\hat{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau^{\prime},X(\tau^{\prime}),V(\tau^{\prime}))]\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}\mathrm{d}\tau\right\}\\
=&\frac{1}{{\hat{v}_{\mathbf{b}}}_{,3}}\left\{\partial_{x_{i}}x_{3}+\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\int^{t}_{\tau}[\nabla_{x}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau^{\prime})\cdot\partial_{x_{i}}X(\tau^{\prime})+\nabla_{v}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau^{\prime})\cdot\partial_{x_{i}}V(\tau^{\prime})]\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}\mathrm{d}\tau\right\}.\end{split}

(5.5)

Similarly,

\begin{split}\partial_{v_{i}}x_{3}-t_{\mathbf{b}}\partial_{v_{i}}\hat{v}_{3}+\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\int^{t}_{\tau}\partial_{v_{i}}[\hat{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau^{\prime},X(\tau^{\prime}),V(\tau^{\prime}))]\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}\mathrm{d}\tau=&\Big(\hat{v}_{3}-\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau^{\prime})\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}\Big)\partial_{v_{i}}t_{\mathbf{b}}\\
=&\ {\hat{v}_{\mathbf{b}}}_{,3}\partial_{v_{i}}t_{\mathbf{b}},\end{split}

(5.6)

Thus

\begin{split}\partial_{v_{i}}t_{\mathbf{b}}=&\frac{1}{{\hat{v}_{\mathbf{b}}}_{,3}}\left\{-t_{\mathbf{b}}\partial_{v_{i}}\hat{v}_{3}+\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\int^{t}_{\tau}\partial_{v_{i}}[\hat{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau^{\prime},X(\tau^{\prime}),V(\tau^{\prime}))]\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}\mathrm{d}\tau\right\}\\
=&\frac{1}{{\hat{v}_{\mathbf{b}}}_{,3}}\left\{-t_{\mathbf{b}}\partial_{v_{i}}\hat{v}_{3}+\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\int^{t}_{\tau}[\nabla_{x}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau^{\prime})\cdot\partial_{v_{i}}X(\tau^{\prime})+\nabla_{v}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau^{\prime})\cdot\partial_{v_{i}}V(\tau^{\prime})]\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}\mathrm{d}\tau\right\}.\end{split}

(5.7)

And we have

\begin{split}\partial_{x_{i}}x_{\mathbf{b}}=&e_{i}-(\partial_{x_{i}}t_{\mathbf{b}})\hat{v}_{\mathbf{b}}+\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\int^{t}_{\tau}[\nabla_{x}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau^{\prime})\cdot\partial_{x_{i}}X(\tau^{\prime})+\nabla_{v}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau^{\prime})\cdot\partial_{x_{i}}V(\tau^{\prime})]\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}\mathrm{d}\tau\\
\partial_{x_{i}}v_{\mathbf{b}}=&-(\partial_{x_{i}}t_{\mathbf{b}})\mathfrak{F}(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},t_{\mathbf{b}})-\int_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}^{t}[\nabla_{x}{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau)\cdot\partial_{x_{i}}X(\tau)+\nabla_{v}{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau)\cdot\partial_{x_{i}}V(\tau)]\mathrm{d}\tau\\
\partial_{v_{i}}x_{\mathbf{b}}=&-(\partial_{v_{i}}\hat{v})t_{\mathbf{b}}-(\partial_{v_{i}}t_{\mathbf{b}})\hat{v}_{\mathbf{b}}+\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\int^{t}_{\tau}[\nabla_{x}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau^{\prime})\cdot\partial_{v_{i}}X(\tau^{\prime})+\nabla_{v}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau^{\prime})\cdot\partial_{v_{i}}V(\tau^{\prime})]\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}\mathrm{d}\tau\\
\partial_{v_{i}}v_{\mathbf{b}}=&e_{i}-(\partial_{v_{i}}t_{\mathbf{b}})\mathfrak{F}(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},t_{\mathbf{b}})-\int_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}^{t}[\nabla_{x}{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau)\cdot\partial_{v_{i}}X(\tau)+\nabla_{v}{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau)\cdot\partial_{v_{i}}V(\tau)]\mathrm{d}\tau.\end{split}

(5.8)

We have the following calculations for the derivatives of
f
in (
5.1
).

\begin{split}&\partial_{x_{i}}f(t,x,v)\\
=&\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}>t}\{\nabla_{x}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))\cdot\partial_{x_{i}}X(0)+\nabla_{v}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))\cdot\partial_{x_{i}}V(0)\}\\
&+\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}<t}\{-\partial_{t}g(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})\partial_{x_{i}}t_{\mathbf{b}}+\nabla_{x}g(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})\partial_{x_{i}}x_{\mathbf{b}}+\nabla_{v}g(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})\partial_{x_{i}}v_{\mathbf{b}}\}\\
=&\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}>t}\{\nabla_{x}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))\cdot\partial_{x_{i}}X(0)+\nabla_{v}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))\cdot\partial_{x_{i}}V(0)\}\\
&+\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}<t}\bigg(-\partial_{t}g(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})\frac{1}{{\hat{v}_{\mathbf{b}}}_{,3}}\left\{\partial_{x_{i}}x_{3}+\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\int^{t}_{\tau}[\nabla_{x}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau^{\prime})\cdot\partial_{x_{i}}X(\tau^{\prime})+\nabla_{v}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau^{\prime})\cdot\partial_{x_{i}}V(\tau^{\prime})]\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}\mathrm{d}\tau\right\}\\
&\quad+\nabla_{x}g(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})\cdot\left\{e_{i}-(\partial_{x_{i}}t_{\mathbf{b}})\hat{v}_{\mathbf{b}}+\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\int^{t}_{\tau}[\nabla_{x}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau^{\prime})\cdot\partial_{x_{i}}X(\tau^{\prime})+\nabla_{v}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau^{\prime})\cdot\partial_{x_{i}}V(\tau^{\prime})]\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}\mathrm{d}\tau\right\}\\
&\quad+\nabla_{v}g(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})\cdot\left\{-(\partial_{x_{i}}t_{\mathbf{b}})\mathfrak{F}(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},t_{\mathbf{b}})-\int_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}^{t}[\nabla_{x}{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau)\cdot\partial_{v_{i}}X(\tau)+\nabla_{v}{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau)\cdot\partial_{v_{i}}V(\tau)]\mathrm{d}\tau\right\}\bigg),\end{split}

(5.9)

and

\begin{split}&\partial_{v_{i}}f(t,x,v)\\
=&\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}>t}\{\nabla_{x}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))\cdot\partial_{v_{i}}X(0)+\nabla_{v}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))\cdot\partial_{v_{i}}V(0)\}\\
&+\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}<t}\{-\partial_{t}g(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})\partial_{v_{i}}t_{\mathbf{b}}+\nabla_{x}g(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})\partial_{v_{i}}x_{\mathbf{b}}+\nabla_{v}g(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})\partial_{v_{i}}v_{\mathbf{b}}\}\\
=&\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}>t}\{\nabla_{x}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))\cdot\partial_{v_{i}}X(0)+\nabla_{v}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))\cdot\partial_{v_{i}}V(0)\}\\
&+\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}<t}\bigg(-\partial_{t}g(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})\frac{1}{{\hat{v}_{\mathbf{b}}}_{,3}}\left\{-t_{\mathbf{b}}\partial_{v_{i}}\hat{v}_{3}+\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\int^{t}_{\tau}[\nabla_{x}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau^{\prime})\cdot\partial_{v_{i}}X(\tau^{\prime})+\nabla_{v}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau^{\prime})\cdot\partial_{v_{i}}V(\tau^{\prime})]\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}\mathrm{d}\tau\right\}\\
&\quad+\nabla_{x}g(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})\cdot\left\{-(\partial_{v_{i}}\hat{v})t_{\mathbf{b}}-(\partial_{v_{i}}t_{\mathbf{b}})\hat{v}_{\mathbf{b}}++\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\int^{t}_{\tau}[\nabla_{x}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau^{\prime})\cdot\partial_{v_{i}}X(\tau^{\prime})+\nabla_{v}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau^{\prime})\cdot\partial_{v_{i}}V(\tau^{\prime})]\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}\mathrm{d}\tau\right\}\\
&\quad+\nabla_{v}g(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})\cdot\left\{e_{i}-(\partial_{v_{i}}t_{\mathbf{b}})\mathfrak{f}(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},t_{\mathbf{b}})-\int_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}^{t}[\nabla_{x}{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau)\cdot\partial_{v_{i}}X(\tau)+\nabla_{v}{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau)\cdot\partial_{v_{i}}V(\tau)]\mathrm{d}\tau\right\}\bigg)\end{split}

(5.10)

Proposition 3
.

Let
(f,E,B)
be a solution of (
0.40
), (
0.25
), (
0.21
). Suppose the fields satisfies (
4.18
), and

\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\nabla_{x}E(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla_{x}B(t)\|_{\infty}\right)<\infty.

And assume that for
\delta>0
,

\begin{split}\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f_{0}\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f_{0}\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{v}f_{0}\|_{\infty}&<\infty,\\
\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\partial_{t}g\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}g\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{v}g\|_{\infty}&<\infty.\end{split}

then for
0<T\ll 1
small enough, we have

\begin{split}&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{v}f(t)\|_{\infty}\right)<\infty.\end{split}

(5.11)

Proof.

For notational simplicity, we assume that the lower order terms of
E
and
B
are smaller than the higher order terms:

\begin{split}&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|E(t)\|_{\infty}+g+|B_{e}|\lesssim\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\nabla_{x}E(t)\|_{\infty},\\
&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|B(t)\|_{\infty}+g+|B_{e}|\lesssim\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\nabla_{x}B(t)\|_{\infty}.\end{split}

(5.12)

From (
5.9
), (
5.10
), we have

\begin{split}&|\partial_{x_{i}}f(t,x,v)|\\
\leq&\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}>t}\{|\nabla_{x}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))||\partial_{x_{i}}X(0)|+|\nabla_{v}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))||\partial_{x_{i}}V(0)|\}\\
&+\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}<t}\bigg(|\partial_{t}g(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})|\left|\frac{1}{{\hat{v}_{\mathbf{b}}}_{,3}}\left\{\partial_{x_{i}}x_{3}+\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\int^{t}_{\tau}[\nabla_{x}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau^{\prime})\cdot\partial_{x_{i}}X(\tau^{\prime})+\nabla_{v}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau^{\prime})\cdot\partial_{x_{i}}V(\tau^{\prime})]\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}\mathrm{d}\tau\right\}\right|\\
&\quad+|\nabla_{x}g(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})|\left|e_{i}-(\partial_{x_{i}}t_{\mathbf{b}})\hat{v}_{\mathbf{b}}+\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\int^{t}_{\tau}[\nabla_{x}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau^{\prime})\cdot\partial_{x_{i}}X(\tau^{\prime})+\nabla_{v}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau^{\prime})\cdot\partial_{x_{i}}V(\tau^{\prime})]\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}\mathrm{d}\tau\right|\\
&\quad+|\nabla_{v}g(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})|\left|-(\partial_{x_{i}}t_{\mathbf{b}})\mathfrak{F}(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},t_{\mathbf{b}})-\int_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}^{t}[\nabla_{x}{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau)\cdot\partial_{x_{i}}X(\tau)+\nabla_{v}{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau)\cdot\partial_{x_{i}}V(\tau)]\mathrm{d}\tau\right|\bigg).\end{split}

(5.13)

\begin{split}&|\partial_{v_{i}}f(t,x,v)|\\
\leq&\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}>t}\{|\nabla_{x}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))||\partial_{v_{i}}X(0)|+|\nabla_{v}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))||\partial_{v_{i}}V(0)|\}\\
&+\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}<t}\bigg(|\partial_{t}g(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})|\left|\frac{1}{{\hat{v}_{\mathbf{b}}}_{,3}}\left\{-t_{\mathbf{b}}\partial_{v_{i}}\hat{v}_{3}+\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\int^{t}_{\tau}[\nabla_{x}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau^{\prime})\cdot\partial_{v_{i}}X(\tau^{\prime})+\nabla_{v}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau^{\prime})\cdot\partial_{v_{i}}V(\tau^{\prime})]\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}\mathrm{d}\tau\right\}\right|\\
&\quad+|\nabla_{x}g(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})|\left|-(\partial_{v_{i}}\hat{v})t_{\mathbf{b}}-(\partial_{v_{i}}t_{\mathbf{b}})\hat{v}_{\mathbf{b}}+\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\int^{t}_{\tau}[\nabla_{x}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau^{\prime})\cdot\partial_{v_{i}}X(\tau^{\prime})+\nabla_{v}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau^{\prime})\cdot\partial_{v_{i}}V(\tau^{\prime})]\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}\mathrm{d}\tau\right|\\
&\quad+|\nabla_{v}g(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})|\left|e_{i}-(\partial_{v_{i}}t_{\mathbf{b}})\mathfrak{F}(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},t_{\mathbf{b}})-\int_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}^{t}[\nabla_{x}{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau)\cdot\partial_{v_{i}}X(\tau)+\nabla_{v}{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau)\cdot\partial_{v_{i}}V(\tau)]\mathrm{d}\tau\right|\bigg).\end{split}

(5.14)

From (
4.30
), (
4.31
), (
4.23
), (
5.5
), and (
5.7
), we have

\begin{split}|\partial_{x_{i}}t_{\mathbf{b}}|\leq&\frac{1}{{\hat{v}_{\mathbf{b}}}_{,3}}\left\{|\partial_{x_{i}}x_{3}|+\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\int^{t}_{\tau}|\nabla_{x}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau^{\prime})\cdot\partial_{x_{i}}X(\tau^{\prime})+\nabla_{v}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau^{\prime})\cdot\partial_{x_{i}}V(\tau^{\prime})|\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}\mathrm{d}\tau\right\}\\
\leq&\frac{1}{{\hat{v}_{\mathbf{b}}}_{,3}}\left\{|\partial_{x_{i}}x_{3}|+\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\int^{t}_{\tau}|\nabla_{x}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau^{\prime})||\partial_{x_{i}}X(\tau^{\prime})|+|\nabla_{v}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau^{\prime})||\partial_{x_{i}}V(\tau^{\prime})|\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}\mathrm{d}\tau\right\}\\
\lesssim&\frac{1}{{\hat{v}_{\mathbf{b}}}_{,3}}\left(|\partial_{x_{i}}x_{3}|+\frac{Ct_{\mathbf{b}}^{2}}{\langle v\rangle}(\|\nabla_{x}E\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}+\|\nabla_{x}B\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}})\right),\end{split}

(5.15)

and

\begin{split}|\partial_{v_{i}}t_{\mathbf{b}}|\leq&\frac{1}{{\hat{v}_{\mathbf{b}}}_{,3}}\left\{|t_{\mathbf{b}}\partial_{v_{i}}\hat{v}_{3}|+\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\int^{t}_{\tau}|\nabla_{x}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau^{\prime})\cdot\partial_{v_{i}}X(\tau^{\prime})+\nabla_{v}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau^{\prime})\cdot\partial_{v_{i}}V(\tau^{\prime})|\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}\mathrm{d}\tau\right\}\\
\leq&\frac{1}{{\hat{v}_{\mathbf{b}}}_{,3}}\left\{|t_{\mathbf{b}}\partial_{v_{i}}\hat{v}_{3}|+\int^{t}_{t-t_{\mathbf{b}}}\int^{t}_{\tau}|\nabla_{x}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau^{\prime})||\partial_{v_{i}}X(\tau^{\prime})|+|\nabla_{v}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau^{\prime})||\partial_{v_{i}}V(\tau^{\prime})|\mathrm{d}\tau^{\prime}\mathrm{d}\tau\right\}\\
\lesssim&\frac{1}{{\hat{v}_{\mathbf{b}}}_{,3}}\left(\frac{t_{\mathbf{b}}}{\langle v\rangle}+\frac{Ct_{\mathbf{b}}^{2}}{\langle v\rangle^{2}}(\|\nabla_{x}E\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}+\|\nabla_{x}B\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}})\right).\end{split}

(5.16)

Thus from (
5.13
) and (
5.15
), for
i=1,2
,

\displaystyle|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\partial_{x_{i}}f(t,x,v)|

\displaystyle\leq\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\big(|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))||\partial_{x_{i}}X_{\parallel}(0)|+|\partial_{x_{3}}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))||\partial_{x_{i}}X_{3}(0)|

(5.17)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+|\nabla_{v}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))||\nabla_{x}V(0)|\big)

\displaystyle+\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}|\partial_{t}g(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})|\frac{Ct_{\mathbf{b}}^{2}}{{\hat{v}_{\mathbf{b}}}_{,3}\langle v\rangle}(\|\nabla_{x}E\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}+\|\nabla_{x}B\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}})

(5.18)
