\displaystyle+\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}|\nabla_{x}g(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})|\left(1+\left(\frac{1}{{\hat{v}_{\mathbf{b}}}_{,3}}+1\right)\frac{Ct_{\mathbf{b}}^{2}}{\langle v\rangle}(\|\nabla_{x}E\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}+\|\nabla_{x}B\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}})\right)

(5.19)

\displaystyle+\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}|\nabla_{v}g(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})|C\left(\frac{t_{\mathbf{b}}^{2}}{{\hat{v}_{\mathbf{b}}}_{,3}\langle v\rangle}|\mathfrak{F}(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},t_{\mathbf{b}})|+t_{\mathbf{b}}\right)(\|\nabla_{x}E\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}+\|\nabla_{x}B\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}).

From (
4.23
), (
4.27
), and (
4.20
), we have

\begin{split}|\eqref{vnablaparaf0}|\leq&C\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\left(|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))|+t|\partial_{x_{3}}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))|+(1+|v|)|\nabla_{v}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))|\right)\\
\leq&(C^{2}+1)\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{v}f_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\right).\end{split}

(5.20)

And

\begin{split}&|\eqref{vnablaparaptg}|+|\eqref{vnablaparaxg}|+|\eqref{vnablaparaxg}|\\
\leq&(C+t_{\mathbf{b}}C(\|\nabla_{x}E\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}+\|\nabla_{x}B\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}))\\
&\times\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\partial_{t}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{v}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}\right).\end{split}

(5.21)

And from (
5.13
) and (
5.15
), for
i=3
,

\displaystyle|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(t,x,v)|

\displaystyle\leq\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha(t,x,v)\big(|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))||\partial_{x_{3}}X_{\parallel}(0)|+|\partial_{x_{3}}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))||\partial_{x_{3}}X_{3}(0)|

(5.22)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+|\nabla_{v}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))||\nabla_{x}V(0)|\big)

\displaystyle+\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}|\partial_{t}g(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})|\frac{\alpha(t,x,v)}{{\hat{v}_{\mathbf{b}}}_{,3}}(1+\frac{Ct_{\mathbf{b}}^{2}}{\langle v\rangle}(\|\nabla_{x}E\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}+\|\nabla_{x}B\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}))

(5.23)

\displaystyle+\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}|\nabla_{x}g(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})|\frac{\alpha(t,x,v)}{{\hat{v}_{\mathbf{b}}}_{,3}}(1+\frac{Ct_{\mathbf{b}}^{2}}{\langle v\rangle}(\|\nabla_{x}E\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}+\|\nabla_{x}B\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}))

(5.24)

\displaystyle+\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}|\nabla_{v}g(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})|\frac{\alpha(t,x,v)}{{\hat{v}_{\mathbf{b}}}_{,3}}|\mathfrak{F}(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},t_{\mathbf{b}})|(1+t_{\mathbf{b}}C(\|\nabla_{x}E\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}+\|\nabla_{x}B\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}})).

(5.25)

Now from (
4.23
) and the velocity lemma (
4.3
),

\begin{split}|\eqref{alphavnablaf0}|\leq&C\frac{\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha(t,x,v)}{(1+|V(0)|)^{5+\delta}\alpha(0,X(0),V(0))}\big(\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\nabla_{v}f_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\big)\\
\leq&C\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\nabla_{v}f(0)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\right),\end{split}

(5.26)

and

\begin{split}&|\eqref{alphavptg}|+|\eqref{alphavnablaxg}|+|\eqref{alphavnablavg}|\\
\leq&\frac{\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha(t,x,v)}{(1+|v_{\mathbf{b}}|)^{5+\delta}\hat{{v_{\mathbf{b}}}_{,3}}}(1+t_{\mathbf{b}}C(\|\nabla_{x}E\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}+\|\nabla_{x}B\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}))\\
&\times\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\partial_{t}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{v}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}\right)\\
\leq&(C+t_{\mathbf{b}}C(\|\nabla_{x}E\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}+\|\nabla_{x}B\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}))\\
&\times\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\partial_{t}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{v}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}\right).\end{split}

(5.27)

Also, similarly from (
5.14
) and (
5.16
),

\displaystyle|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\partial_{v_{i}}f(t,x,v)|

\displaystyle\leq\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\big(|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))||\partial_{v_{i}}X_{\parallel}(0)|+|\partial_{x_{3}}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))||\partial_{v_{i}}X_{3}(0)|

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+|\nabla_{v}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))||\nabla_{v_{i}}V(0)|\big)

\displaystyle+\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}|\partial_{t}g(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})|\frac{t_{\mathbf{b}}}{{\hat{v}_{\mathbf{b}}}_{,3}\langle v\rangle}\times(1+\frac{t_{\mathbf{b}}}{\langle v\rangle}C(\|\nabla_{x}E\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}+\|\nabla_{x}B\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}))

\displaystyle+\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}|\nabla_{x}g(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})|\left(\frac{t_{\mathbf{b}}}{\langle v\rangle}+\left(\frac{1}{{\hat{v}_{\mathbf{b}}}_{,3}}+1\right)\frac{t_{\mathbf{b}}^{2}}{\langle v\rangle}C(\|\nabla_{x}E\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}+\|\nabla_{x}B\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}})\right)

\displaystyle+\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}|\nabla_{v}g(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})|\left((1+\frac{t_{\mathbf{b}}}{{\hat{v}_{\mathbf{b}}}_{,3}\langle v\rangle}|\mathfrak{F}(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},t_{\mathbf{b}})|+t_{\mathbf{b}}C(\|\nabla_{x}E\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}+\|\nabla_{x}B\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}})\right)

\displaystyle\leq(C_{2}^{2}+1)\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{v}f_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+(C+t_{\mathbf{b}}C(\|\nabla_{x}E\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}+\|\nabla_{x}B\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}))

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\partial_{t}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{v}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}\right).

(5.28)

Now from (
3.26
) we have

\begin{split}&\|\nabla_{x}E\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}+\|\nabla_{x}B\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}\\
&\lesssim\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}+C,\end{split}

thus combining (
5.20
), (
5.21
), (
5.26
), (
5.27
), and (
5.28
), and by choosing
0<T\ll 1
small enough
we have

\begin{split}&\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{v}f(t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}\\
\leq&2\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{v}f_{0}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\right)\\
&+C_{1}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\partial_{t}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{x}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{v}g\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}\right)\\
&+\frac{1}{2}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t,x}}\right)<\infty.\end{split}

(5.29)

This conclude (
5.11
).

∎

We state and prove a variation of Ukai’s trace theorem in
[
61
]
.

Lemma 14
.

Suppose
f\in L^{\infty}((0,T)\times\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3})
, and
\mathfrak{F}\in W^{1,\infty}((0,T)\times\mathbb{R}^{3})
satisfy

\partial_{t}f+\hat{v}\cdot\nabla_{x}f+\mathfrak{F}\cdot\nabla_{v}f=h\in L^{\infty}((0,T)\times\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}).

(5.30)

Then
f\in L^{\infty}((0,T)\times(\gamma\setminus\gamma_{0}))
, and

\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|f(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma\setminus\gamma_{0})}\leq\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|f(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}.

(5.31)

Proof.

Denote the characteristics
X(s;t,x,v),V(s;t,x,v)
which solves

\begin{split}\frac{d}{ds}X(s;t,x,v)=&\hat{V}(s;t,x,v),\\
\frac{d}{ds}V(s;t,x,v)=&\mathfrak{F}(s,X(s;t,x,v),V(s;t,x,v)),\end{split}

(5.32)

and
X(t;t,x,v)=x
,
V(t;t,x,v)=v
. Then since
\mathfrak{F}\in W^{1,\infty}((0,T)\times\mathbb{R}^{3})
, the characteristics (
5.32
) is Hölder continuous. From (
5.30
), for almost every
(t,x,v)\in(0,T)\times\gamma_{+}
, and
\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}(t,x,v)\}
,

f(t,x,v)=f(s,X(s;t,x,v),V(s;t,x,v))+\int_{s}^{t}h(\tau;X(\tau;t,x,v),V(\tau;t,x,v))d\tau.

Thus

\sup_{0<t<T}\|f(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma_{+})}\leq\sup_{0<t<T}\|f(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}+(t-s)\|h\|_{L^{\infty}((0,T)\times\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}.

Since
t-s>0
can be arbitrarily small, we have

\sup_{0<t<T}\|f(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma_{+})}\leq\sup_{0<t<T}\|f(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}.

Now, for
(x,v)\in\gamma_{-}
and
s\in(t,\max\{T,t_{\mathbf{f}}(t,x,v)\})
, we have

f(t,x,v)=f(s,X(s;t,x,v),V(s;t,x,v))-\int_{t}^{s}h(\tau;X(\tau;t,x,v),V(\tau;t,x,v))d\tau.

Using the same argument we get

\sup_{0<t<T}\|f(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma_{-})}\leq\sup_{0<t<T}\|f(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}.

This proves (
5.31
).

∎

Next, we prove a trace theorem for the derivatives of
f
.

Lemma 15
.

Let
(f,E,B)
be a solution of (
0.40
), (
0.25
), (
0.21
). Suppose

\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\nabla_{x}E(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla_{x}B(t)\|_{\infty}\right)<\infty,

(5.33)

\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\partial_{t}g\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}g\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{v}g\|_{\infty}<\infty,

(5.34)

and

\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{v}f(t)\|_{\infty}\right)<\infty.

(5.35)

Then

\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma\setminus\gamma_{0})}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma\setminus\gamma_{0})}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{v}f(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma\setminus\gamma_{0})}\right)<\infty

(5.36)

Proof.

The proof uses similar argument as Ukai’s proof of a trace theorem in
[
61
]
. Next, notice that for
\partial_{\mathbf{e}}\in\{\nabla_{x_{\parallel}},\nabla_{v}\}
, and
p=4+\delta,5+\delta
, we have

\partial_{t}(\langle v\rangle^{p}\partial_{\mathbf{e}}f)+\hat{v}\cdot\nabla_{x}(\langle v\rangle^{p}\partial_{\mathbf{e}}f)+\mathfrak{F}\cdot\nabla_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{p}\partial_{\mathbf{e}}f)=-\langle v\rangle^{p}\partial_{\mathbf{e}}\hat{v}\cdot\nabla_{x}f-\langle v\rangle^{p}\partial_{\mathbf{e}}\mathfrak{F}\cdot\nabla_{v}f-\mathfrak{F}\cdot\nabla_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{p})\partial_{\mathbf{e}}f.

(5.37)

Then for almost every
(x,v)\in\gamma_{+}
, and
s\in(\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}(t,x,v)\},t)
, we have

\begin{split}\langle v\rangle^{p}\partial_{\mathbf{e}}f(t,x,v)=&\langle V(s;t,x,v)\rangle^{p}\partial_{\mathbf{e}}f(s,X(s;t,x,v),V(s;t,x,v))\\
&+\int_{s}^{t}\left(-\langle v\rangle^{p}\partial_{\mathbf{e}}\hat{v}\cdot\nabla_{x}f-\langle v\rangle^{p}\partial_{\mathbf{e}}\mathfrak{F}\cdot\nabla_{v}f-\mathfrak{F}\cdot\nabla_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{p})\partial_{\mathbf{e}}f\right)(\tau,X(\tau;t,x,v),V(\tau;t,x,v))d\tau.\end{split}

(5.38)

Thus, from (
5.33
) and (
5.35
),

\begin{split}&|\langle v\rangle^{p}\partial_{\mathbf{e}}f(t,x,v)|\\
\leq&\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|\langle v\rangle^{p}\partial_{\mathbf{e}}f(s)\|_{\infty}+\frac{C(t-s)}{\alpha(t,x,v)}\bigg((\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}(\|\nabla_{x}E(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla_{x}B(t)\|_{\infty})+g+|B_{e}|)\\
&\quad\quad\quad\quad\times\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{v}f(t)\|_{\infty}\right)\bigg),\end{split}

(5.39)

since we can choose
s
close enough to
t
such that

\begin{split}&\frac{C(t-s)}{\alpha(t,x,v)}\bigg((\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}(\|\nabla_{x}E(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla_{x}B(t)\|_{\infty})+g+|B_{e}|)\\
&\quad\quad\quad\quad\times\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{v}f(t)\|_{\infty}\right)\bigg)<\varepsilon\ll 1,\end{split}

we have

|\langle v\rangle^{p}\partial_{\mathbf{e}}f(t,x,v)|\leq\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|\langle v\rangle^{p}\partial_{\mathbf{e}}f(s)\|_{\infty}+\varepsilon,

for any
\varepsilon>0
. Therefore, we get

\begin{split}&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma_{+})}\leq\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{\infty},\\
&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{v}f(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma_{+})}\leq\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{v}f(t)\|_{\infty}.\end{split}

(5.40)

Similarly, since

\begin{split}&(\partial_{t}+\hat{v}\cdot\nabla_{x}+\mathfrak{F}\cdot\nabla_{v})(\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f)\\
&=-\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}\mathfrak{F}\cdot\nabla_{v}f-\mathfrak{F}\cdot\nabla_{v}(\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta})\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f-[(\partial_{t}+\hat{v}\cdot\nabla_{x}+\mathfrak{F}\cdot\nabla_{v})\alpha]\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\partial_{x_{3}}f\\
&=:G_{\alpha}(t,x,v).\end{split}

Then for almost every
(x,v)\in\gamma_{+}
, and
s\in(\max\{0,t-t_{\mathbf{b}}(t,x,v)\},t)
, we have

\begin{split}&\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(t,x,v)\\
=&\langle V(s;t,x,v)\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(s,X(s;t,x,v),V(s;t,x,v))+\int_{s}^{t}G_{\alpha}(\tau,X(\tau;t,x,v),V(\tau;t,x,v))d\tau.\end{split}

(5.41)

Since

\begin{split}|G_{\alpha}(t,x,v)|\leq&C\big(((\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\nabla_{x}E(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla_{x}B(t)\|_{\infty})+g+|B_{e}|)\\
&\quad\quad\quad\quad\times\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{v}f(t)\|_{\infty}\right)\big),\end{split}

using the same argument as (
5.39
)–(
5.40
) , we obtain

\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma_{+})}\leq\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(t,x,v)\|_{\infty}.

(5.42)

Now, for
(x,v)\in\gamma_{-}
, and any
s\in(t,\max\{T,t_{\mathbf{f}}(t,x,v)\})
we have the same formula (
5.38
) and (
5.41
) for
\langle v\rangle^{p}\partial\mathbf{e}f
and
\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f
respectively. Therefore by the same argument, we get

\begin{split}&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma_{-})}\leq\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{\infty},\\
&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{v}f(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma_{-})}\leq\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{v}f(t)\|_{\infty},\\
&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma_{-})}\leq\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(t,x,v)\|_{\infty}.\end{split}

(5.43)

Combining (
5.40
), (
5.42
), and (
5.43
), we conclude (
5.36
).
∎

6.
Local existence

We prove the local existence for RVM system with the inflow boundary condition in this section. We recursively define a sequence of functions:

f^{0}(t,x,v)=f_{0}(x,v),\ E^{0}(t,x)=E_{0}(t,x),\ B^{0}(t,x)=B_{0}(x).

For
\ell\geq 1
, let
f^{\ell}
be the solution of

\begin{split}\partial_{t}f^{\ell}+\hat{v}\cdot\nabla_{x}f^{\ell}+\mathfrak{F}^{\ell-1}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{\ell}=&0,\text{ where }\mathfrak{F}^{\ell-1}=E^{\ell-1}+E_{\text{ext}}+\hat{v}\times(B^{\ell-1}+B_{\text{ext}})-g\mathbf{e}_{3},\\
f^{\ell}(0,x,v)=&f_{0}(x,v),\\
f^{\ell}(t,x,v)|_{\gamma_{-}}=&g(t,x,v).\end{split}

(6.1)

Let
\rho^{\ell}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}f^{\ell}dv,j^{\ell}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\hat{v}f^{\ell}dv
. Let

E^{\ell}=\eqref{Eesttat0pos}+\dots+\eqref{Eest3bdrycontri},\ B^{\ell}=\eqref{Besttat0pos}+\dots+\eqref{Bestbdrycontri},\text{ with }f\text{ changes to }f^{\ell}.

(6.2)

We prove several uniform-in-
\ell
bounds for the sequence before passing the limit.

Lemma 16
.

Suppose
f_{0}
satisfies (
0.32
),
E_{0}
,
B_{0}
satisfy (
0.16
), (
0.35
), then there exits
M_{1},M_{2}
, and
c_{0}
, such that for
0<T\ll 1
,

\begin{split}\sup_{\ell}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f^{\ell}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\bar{\Omega}\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}\right)<&M_{1},\ \\
\sup_{\ell}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|E^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|B^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}\right)+|B_{e}|+E_{e}+g<&M_{2},\\
\inf_{\ell}\inf_{t,x_{\parallel}}\left(g-E_{e}-E^{\ell}_{3}(t,x_{\parallel},0)-(\hat{v}\times B^{\ell})_{3}(t,x_{\parallel},0)\right)>&c_{0}.\end{split}

(6.3)

Proof.

Let
\ell\geq 1
. By induction hypothesis we assume that

\begin{split}\sup_{0\leq i\leq\ell}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f^{\ell-i}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\bar{\Omega}\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}\right)<&M_{1},\\
\sup_{0\leq i\leq\ell}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|E^{\ell-i}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|B^{\ell-i}(t)\|_{\infty}\right)+|B_{e}|+E_{e}+g<&M_{2}.\end{split}

(6.4)

Denote the characteristics
(X^{\ell},V^{\ell})
which solves

\begin{split}\frac{d}{ds}X^{\ell}(s;t,x,v)&=\hat{V}^{\ell}(s;t,x,v),\\
\frac{d}{ds}V^{\ell}(s;t,x,v)&=\mathfrak{F}^{\ell}(s,X^{\ell}(s;t,x,v),V^{\ell}(s;t,x,v)).\end{split}

(6.5)

First, we note that for any
0s<t
, since

V^{\ell}(s;t,x,v)=v-\int_{s}^{t}\mathfrak{F}^{\ell}(\tau,X^{\ell}(\tau),V^{\ell}(\tau))d\tau,

from (
6.4
), we have

|v|-(t-s)M_{2}\leq|V^{\ell}(s;t,x,v)|\leq|v|+(t-s)M_{2}.

Thus

\left(1+(t-s)(M_{2}+g)\right)^{-1}\langle v\rangle\leq\langle V^{\ell}(s;t,x,v)\rangle\leq\left(1+(t-s)(M_{2}+g)\right)\langle v\rangle.

(6.6)

From (
6.1
) we have for any
(t,x,v)\in(0,T)\times\bar{\Omega}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
,

\begin{split}f^{\ell+1}(t,x,v)=&\mathbf{1}_{{t_{\mathbf{b}}}^{\ell}\leq 0}f^{\ell+1}(0,X^{\ell}(0),V^{\ell}(0))+\mathbf{1}_{{t_{\mathbf{b}}}^{\ell}\geq 0}g({t_{\mathbf{b}}}^{\ell},X^{\ell}({t_{\mathbf{b}}}^{\ell};t,x,v),V^{\ell}({t_{\mathbf{b}}}^{\ell};t,x,v)).\end{split}

(6.7)

And (
6.6
) gives

\begin{split}&\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}|f^{\ell+1}(t,x,v)|\\
\leq&\mathbf{1}_{{t_{\mathbf{b}}}^{\ell}\leq 0}(1+T(M_{2}+g))^{4+\delta}|\langle V^{\ell}(0)\rangle^{4+\delta}f^{\ell+1}(0,X^{\ell}(0),V^{\ell}(0))|\\
&+\mathbf{1}_{{t_{\mathbf{b}}}^{\ell}\geq 0}(1+T(M_{2}+g))^{4+\delta}\langle V^{\ell}({t_{\mathbf{b}}}^{\ell})\rangle^{4+\delta}g({t_{\mathbf{b}}}^{\ell},X^{\ell}({t_{\mathbf{b}}}^{\ell};t,x,v),V^{\ell}({t_{\mathbf{b}}}^{\ell};t,x,v)).\end{split}

(6.8)

Thus, by choosing
T\ll 1
we have

\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f^{\ell+1}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\bar{\Omega}\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}<M_{1}.

(6.9)

Now from (
6.2
), using the same argument in the proof of Lemma
8
, we have

\begin{split}&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|E^{\ell+1}(t)\|_{\infty}+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|B^{\ell+1}(t)\|_{\infty}\\
\leq&C(\|E_{0}\|_{\infty}+\|B_{0}\|_{\infty})+CT(\|E_{0}\|_{C^{1}}+\|B_{0}\|_{C^{1}})\\
&+CT\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}\left(1+T(\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|E^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|B^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}\right)+g+|B_{e}|)\right)\\
\leq&C(\|E_{0}\|_{C^{1}}+\|B_{0}\|_{C^{1}})+CTM_{1}\left(1+T(M_{2}+g+|B_{e}|)\right).\end{split}

(6.10)

Letting
M_{2}=(C+1)(\|E_{0}\|_{C^{1}}+\|B_{0}\|_{C^{1}})+|B_{e}|+E_{e}+g
and
T\ll 1
, we get

\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|E^{\ell+1}(t)\|_{\infty}+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|B^{\ell+1}(t)\|_{\infty}+|B_{e}|+E_{e}+g<M_{2}.

(6.11)

Next, from (
0.16
) and the proof of Lemma
8
, by letting
c_{0}=\frac{c_{1}}{2}
in (
0.16
), we get

\begin{split}\inf_{t,x_{\parallel}}\left(g-E_{e}-E^{\ell+1}_{3}(t,x_{\parallel},0)-(\hat{v}\times B^{\ell+1})_{3}(t,x_{\parallel},0)\right)>&2c_{0}-CTM_{1}\left(1+T(M_{2}+g+|B_{e}|)\right).\end{split}

By choosing
T\ll 1
small enough, we have

\inf_{t,x_{\parallel}}\left(g-E_{e}-E^{\ell+1}_{3}(t,x_{\parallel},0)-(\hat{v}\times B^{\ell+1})_{3}(t,x_{\parallel},0)\right)>c_{0}.

(6.12)

Combining with (
6.9
) and (
6.11
), we conclude (
6.3
) by induction.

∎

Next, we consider the derivative of the sequences. Define
\alpha^{\ell}
as

\begin{split}\alpha^{\ell}(t,x,v)=\sqrt{(x_{3})^{2}+(\hat{v}_{3})^{2}-2\left(E^{\ell}_{3}(t,x_{\parallel},0)+E_{e}+(\hat{v}\times B^{\ell})_{3}(t,x_{\parallel},0)-g\right)\frac{x_{3}}{\langle v\rangle}}.\end{split}

(6.13)

We have the following estimate.

Lemma 17
.

Suppose
f_{0}
satisfies (
0.32
), (
0.33
),
E_{0}
,
B_{0}
satisfy (
0.35
), then there exits
M_{3},M_{4}
such that for
0<T\ll 1
,

\begin{split}&\sup_{\ell}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha^{\ell-1}\partial_{x_{3}}f^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{v}f^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}\right)\\
&+\sup_{\ell}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f^{\ell}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma\setminus\gamma_{0})}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha^{\ell-1}\partial_{x_{3}}f^{\ell}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma\setminus\gamma_{0})}+\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{v}f^{\ell}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma\setminus\gamma_{0})}\right)<M_{3},\\
&\sup_{\ell}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\partial_{t}E^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\partial_{t}B^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla_{x}E^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla_{x}B^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}\right)<M_{4}.\end{split}

(6.14)

Proof.

The proof is essentially the same as the proof of Proposition
3
.
From the uniform estimate (
6.3
), and from the velocity lemma (Lemma
10
), we have for some
C>0
,

e^{-C|t-s|}\alpha^{\ell}(t,x,v)\leq\alpha^{\ell}(s,X^{\ell}(s;t,x,v),V^{\ell}(s;t,x,v))\leq e^{C|t-s|}\alpha^{\ell}(t,x,v),\ \text{ for all }\ell.

(6.15)

Therefore, following the same proof of Proposition
3
and Lemma
15
, we get

\begin{split}&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f^{\ell+1}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha^{\ell}\partial_{x_{3}}f^{\ell+1}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{v}f^{\ell+1}(t)\|_{\infty}\right)\\
+&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f^{\ell}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma\setminus\gamma_{0})}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha^{\ell-1}\partial_{x_{3}}f^{\ell}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma\setminus\gamma_{0})}+\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{v}f^{\ell}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma\setminus\gamma_{0})}\right)\\
\leq&C_{k}\bigg(\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f_{0}\|_{\infty}+\|\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f_{0}\|_{\infty}+\|\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{v}f_{0}\|_{\infty}\right)\\
&+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}g(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma_{-})}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{v}g(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma_{-}}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\partial_{t}g(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma_{-})}\right)\bigg).\end{split}

(6.16)

Thus, by choosing
M_{3}\gg 1
, we conclude

\begin{split}&\sup_{\ell}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha^{\ell-1}\partial_{x_{3}}f^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{v}f^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}\right)\\
&+\sup_{\ell}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f^{\ell}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma\setminus\gamma_{0})}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha^{\ell-1}\partial_{x_{3}}f^{\ell}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma\setminus\gamma_{0})}+\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{v}f^{\ell}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma\setminus\gamma_{0})}\right)<M_{3}.\end{split}

(6.17)

From this, we use the same argument to get (
3.63
) in the proof of Lemma
9
and obtain

\begin{split}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}&\left(\|\partial_{t}E^{\ell+1}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\partial_{t}B^{\ell+1}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla_{x}E^{\ell+1}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla_{x}B^{\ell+1}(t)\|_{\infty}\right)\\
\leq&TC\sup_{1\leq i\leq\ell}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\partial_{t}E^{i}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\partial_{t}B^{i}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla_{x}E^{i}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla_{x}B^{i}(t)\|_{\infty}\right)\\
&C\left(\|E_{0}\|_{C^{2}}+\|B_{0}\|_{C_{2}}\right)+C\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f^{\ell+1}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{\ell+\delta}\alpha^{n}\partial_{x_{3}}f^{\ell+1}(t)\|_{\infty}\right)\\
&+C\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f^{\ell}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma\setminus\gamma_{0})}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha^{\ell-1}\partial_{x_{3}}f^{\ell}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma\setminus\gamma_{0})}\right)\\
&+C\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f^{\ell+1}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|E^{\ell+1}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|B^{\ell+1}(t)\|_{\infty}\right).\end{split}

From (
6.3
) and (
6.17
), this gives

\begin{split}\sup_{\ell}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}&\left(\|\partial_{t}E^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\partial_{t}B^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla_{x}E^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla_{x}B^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}\right)\\
\leq&TC\sup_{\ell}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\partial_{t}E^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\partial_{t}B^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla_{x}E^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla_{x}B^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}\right)\\
&+C\left(\|E_{0}\|_{C^{2}}+\|B_{0}\|_{C_{2}}\right)+C(M_{1}+M_{2}+M_{3}).\end{split}

Therefore, by choosing
M_{4}\gg 1
and
T\ll 1
, we get

\sup_{\ell}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\partial_{t}E^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\partial_{t}B^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla_{x}E^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla_{x}B^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}\right)<M_{4}.

(6.18)

Together with (
6.17
), we conclude (
6.14
).
∎

We have the following trace properties for
E^{\ell}
and
B^{\ell}
:

Lemma 18
.

Suppose
E^{\ell},B^{\ell}
satisfies (
6.14
). Then for any
\ell\geq 1
,
0<t<T
,

E^{\ell}(t,\cdot,0)\in L^{\infty}(\partial\Omega),\ B^{\ell}(t,\cdot,0)\in L^{\infty}(\partial\Omega).

(6.19)

Proof.

From (
6.14
) we have

E^{\ell}(t,x)\in W^{1,\infty}((0,T)\times\Omega),\ B^{\ell}(t,x)\in W^{1,\infty}((0,T)\times\Omega),

in particular, from the Morrey’s inequality,
E(t)
,
B(t)
are Lipschitz continuous on
\Omega
. Now pick any
x_{\parallel}\in\mathbb{R}^{2}
, and
0<x_{3}<1
, from the fundamental theorem of calculus, we have

\begin{split}E^{\ell}(t,x_{\parallel},0)=&E^{\ell}(t,x_{\parallel},x_{3})-\int_{0}^{x_{3}}\partial_{3}E^{\ell}(t,x_{\parallel},y)dy,\\
B^{\ell}(t,x_{\parallel},0)=&B^{\ell}(t,x_{\parallel},x_{3})-\int_{0}^{x_{3}}\partial_{3}B^{\ell}(t,x_{\parallel},y)dy.\end{split}

Therefore

\begin{split}&\|E^{\ell}(t,\cdot,0)\|_{L^{\infty}(\partial\Omega)}\leq\|E^{\ell}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}+x_{3}\|\partial_{3}E^{\ell}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)},\\
&\|B^{\ell}(t,\cdot,0)\|_{L^{\infty}(\partial\Omega)}\leq\|B^{\ell}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}+x_{3}\|\partial_{3}B^{\ell}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)},\end{split}

for any
x_{3}>0
. Thus

\|E^{\ell}(t,\cdot,0)\|_{L^{\infty}(\partial\Omega)}\leq\|E^{\ell}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}<\infty,\ \|B^{\ell}(t,\cdot,0)\|_{L^{\infty}(\partial\Omega)}\leq\|B^{\ell}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}<\infty,

and we conclude (
6.19
).
∎

Next, we prove the strong convergence of the sequence
f^{\ell}
.

Lemma 19
.

Suppose
f_{0},g
satisfies (
0.32
), (
0.33
),
E_{0}
,
B_{0}
satisfy (
0.16
), (
0.35
). There exists functions
(f,E,B)
with
\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f(t,x,v)\in L^{\infty}((0,T);L^{\infty}(\bar{\Omega}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}))
, and
(E,B)\in L^{\infty}((0,T);L^{\infty}(\Omega)\cap L^{\infty}(\partial\Omega))
, such that as
\ell\to\infty
,

\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|E^{\ell}(t)-E(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}+\|E^{\ell}(t)-E(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\partial\Omega)}+\|B^{\ell}(t)-B(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}+\|B^{\ell}(t)-B(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\partial\Omega)}\right)\to 0,

(6.20)

and

\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\ell}f^{\ell}(t)-\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\bar{\Omega}\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}\to 0.

(6.21)

Moreover,
(f,E,B)
is a (weak) solution of the system (
0.40
)-(
0.42
), and (
0.25
).

Proof.

Let
m>n\geq 1
. Note that
f^{m}-f^{n}
satisfies
(f^{m}-f^{n})|_{t=0}=0
and

(f^{m}-f^{n})|_{\gamma_{-}}=0.

The equation for
f^{m}-f^{n}
is

\partial_{t}(f^{m}-f^{n})+\hat{v}\cdot\nabla_{x}(f^{m}-f^{n})+\mathfrak{F}^{m-1}\cdot\nabla_{v}(f^{m}-f^{n})=-(\mathfrak{F}^{m-1}-\mathfrak{F}^{n-1})\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{n}.

Thus, for any
(t,x,v)\in(0,T)\times\bar{\Omega}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
, using (
6.6
), we get

\begin{split}&|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}(f^{m}-f^{n})(t,x,v)|\\
\leq&C_{1}\int_{\max\{{t_{\mathbf{b}}}^{m-1},0\}}^{t}|\langle V^{m-1}(s)\rangle^{4+\delta}(\mathfrak{F}^{m-1}-\mathfrak{F}^{n-1})\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{n})(s,X^{m-1}(s),V^{m-1}(s))|ds\end{split}

where
C_{1}=(1+T(M_{2}+g))^{4+\delta}
. Then from (
6.14
),

\begin{split}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f^{m}(t)-\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f^{n})(t)\|_{\infty}\leq&C_{1}\left(\sup_{\ell}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{v}f^{\ell}(s)\|_{\infty}\right)\int_{0}^{t}\|\mathfrak{F}^{m-1}(s)-\mathfrak{F}^{n-1}(s)\|_{\infty}ds\\
\leq&C_{2}\int_{0}^{t}\|\mathfrak{F}^{m-1}(s)-\mathfrak{F}^{n-1}(s)\|_{\infty}ds,\end{split}

(6.22)

where
C_{2}=C_{1}M_{3}
.

Now,
from (
6.1
) and using the same argument as Lemma
8
with (
6.22
), we have

\begin{split}\|\mathfrak{F}^{m-1}(s)-\mathfrak{F}^{n-1}(s)\|_{\infty}\leq&\|E^{n-1}(s)-E^{m-1}(s)\|_{\infty}+\|B^{n-1}(s)-B^{m-1}(s)\|_{\infty}\\
\leq&C\left(\sup_{0\leq s^{\prime}\leq s}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}(f^{n-1}-f^{m-1})(s^{\prime})\|_{\infty}+\int_{0}^{s}\|\mathfrak{F}^{m-2}(s^{\prime})-\mathfrak{F}^{n-2}(s^{\prime})\|_{\infty}ds^{\prime}\right)\\
\leq&C\int_{0}^{s}\|\mathfrak{F}^{m-2}(s^{\prime})-\mathfrak{F}^{n-2}(s^{\prime})\|_{\infty}ds^{\prime}\\
\leq&C\int_{0}^{s}\left(\|E^{m-2}(s^{\prime})-E^{n-2}(s^{\prime})\|_{\infty}+\|B^{m-2}(s^{\prime})-B^{n-2}(s^{\prime})\|_{\infty}\right)ds^{\prime}.\end{split}

(6.23)

Iteration of (
6.23
) and using (
6.3
) yields

\begin{split}&\|E^{m}(t)-E^{n}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|B^{m}(t)-B^{n}(t)\|_{\infty}\\
\leq&C^{2}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{0}^{s}\left(\|E^{m-2}(s^{\prime})-E^{n-2}(s^{\prime})\|_{\infty}+\|B^{m-2}(s^{\prime})-B^{n-2}(s^{\prime})\|_{\infty}\right)ds^{\prime}ds\\
=&C^{2}\int_{0}^{t}\tau\left(\|E^{m-2}(\tau)-E^{n-2}(\tau)\|_{\infty}+\|B^{m-2}(\tau)-B^{n-2}(\tau)\|_{\infty}\right)d\tau\\
\leq&C^{l}\int_{0}^{t}\frac{\tau^{l-1}}{(l-1)!}\left(\|E^{m-i}(\tau)-E^{n-k}(\tau)\|_{\infty}+\|B^{m-k}(\tau)-B^{n-k}(\tau)\|_{\infty}\right)d\tau\\
\leq&M_{2}\frac{C^{l}t^{l}}{l!}.\end{split}

Thus the sequences
E^{\ell}
,
B^{\ell}
are Cauchy in
L^{\infty}((0,T)\times\Omega)
, moreover, from Lemma
18
,
E^{\ell},B^{\ell}\in L^{\infty}([0,T]\times\partial\Omega)
. Therefore, there exists functions
E,B\in L^{\infty}((0,T);L^{\infty}(\Omega)\cap L^{\infty}(\partial\Omega))
, such that

E^{\ell}\to E,B^{\ell}\to B\text{ in }L^{\infty}((0,T)\times\Omega)\cap L^{\infty}((0,T)\times\partial\Omega).

(6.24)

This proves (
6.20
). Also, from (
6.22
), (
6.23
),

\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f^{m}(t)-\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f^{n})(t)\|_{L^{\infty}((0,T)\times\bar{\Omega})}\leq M_{2}\frac{C^{l-1}t^{l-1}}{(l-2)!},

therefore we get (
6.21
).

Now, take any
\phi(t,x,v)\in C_{c}^{\infty}([0,T)\times\bar{\Omega}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
with
\text{supp }\phi\subset\{[0,T)\times\bar{\Omega}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}\}\setminus\{(0\times\gamma)\cup(0,T)\times\gamma_{0}\}
, from (
6.1
), we have

\begin{split}&\int_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}f_{0}\phi(0)dvdt+\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}f^{\ell}\left(\partial_{t}\phi+\hat{v}\cdot\nabla_{x}\phi+\mathfrak{F}^{\ell-1}\cdot\nabla_{v}\phi\right)dvdxdt\\
=&\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\gamma_{+}}\phi f^{\ell}\hat{v}_{3}dvdS_{x}+\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\gamma_{-}}\phi g\hat{v}_{3}dvdS_{x}.\end{split}

(6.25)

Because of the strong convergence (
6.20
), (
6.21
), we have that as
\ell\to\infty
, each term in (
6.25
) goes to the corresponding terms with
f^{\ell}
replaced by
f
and
\mathfrak{F}^{\ell}
replaced by
\mathfrak{F}
. Therefore we conclude that
(f,E,B)
satisfy (
0.28
).

Next, from Proposition
1
and Proposition
2
, we have that
E^{\ell}
and
B^{\ell}
are (weak) solutions to the wave equations with the initial data, boundary condition and forcing term in (
6.43
)-(
6.46
), with
\rho,J
changed to
\rho^{\ell},J^{\ell}
. Then from (
6.14
) and Lemma
5
, we have

\begin{split}\partial_{t}E^{\ell}&=\nabla_{x}\times B^{\ell}-4\pi J^{\ell},\,\nabla_{x}\cdot E^{\ell}=4\pi\rho^{\ell},\\
\partial_{t}B^{\ell}&=-\nabla_{x}\times E^{\ell},\,\nabla_{x}\cdot B^{\ell}=0,\end{split}

(6.26)

with

E^{\ell}_{1}=E^{\ell}_{2}=0,B^{\ell}_{3}=0,\ \text{ on }\partial\Omega.

(6.27)

Clearly, (
0.31
) is satisfied. Now, for any test functions
\Psi(t,x)\in C_{c}^{\infty}([0,T)\times\bar{\Omega};\mathbb{R}^{3}),\ \Phi(t,x)\in C_{c}^{\infty}([0,T)\times\Omega;\mathbb{R}^{3})
, from (
6.26
) and (
6.27
), we have

\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}E^{\ell}\cdot\partial_{t}\Psi dxdt-\int_{\Omega}\Psi(0,x)\cdot E_{0}dx=-\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}(\nabla_{x}\times\Psi)\cdot B^{\ell}dxdt+4\pi\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}\Psi\cdot J^{\ell}dxdt,

(6.28)

and

\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}B^{\ell}\cdot\partial_{t}\Phi dxdt+\int_{\Omega}\Phi(0,x)\cdot B_{0}dx=\int_{0}^{T}\int_{\Omega}(\nabla_{x}\times\Phi)\cdot E^{\ell}dxdt,

(6.29)

Then from the strong convergence (
6.21
), (
6.20
), we can pass
\ell\to\infty
and deduce that each term in (
6.28
) and (
6.29
) converges to the corresponding term with
E^{\ell}
,
B^{\ell}
,
J^{\ell}
replace by
E
,
B
, and
J
respectively. Therefore,
(f,E,B)
satisfy (
0.29
) and (
0.30
).
So we conclude that
(f,E,B)
is a (weak) solution of the RVM system (
0.40
)-(
0.42
) with inflow BC (
0.25
).
∎

In the next lemma, we consider the regularity of the solution.

Lemma 20
.

Let
\alpha(t,x,v)
be defined as in (
0.57
). The solution
(f,E,B)
obtained in Lemma
24
satisfies

\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{v}f(t)\|_{\infty}<\infty,

(6.30)

and

\|\partial_{t}E(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\partial_{t}B(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla_{x}E(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla_{x}B(t)\|_{\infty}<\infty.

(6.31)

Proof.

From the
L^{\infty}
strong convergence (
6.20
), and the uniform-in-
\ell
bound (
6.14
), we can pass the limit up to subsequence if necessary and get the weak
-*
convergence

\partial_{t}E^{\ell}\overset{\ast}{\rightharpoonup}\partial_{t}E,\ \nabla_{x}E^{\ell}\overset{\ast}{\rightharpoonup}\nabla_{x}E,\ \partial_{t}B^{\ell}\overset{\ast}{\rightharpoonup}\partial_{t}B,\ \nabla_{x}B^{\ell}\overset{\ast}{\rightharpoonup}\nabla_{x}B\text{ in }L^{\infty}((0,T)\times\Omega),

(6.32)

and

\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f^{\ell}\overset{\ast}{\rightharpoonup}\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f,\ \langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{v}f^{\ell}\overset{\ast}{\rightharpoonup}\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{v}f\text{ in }L^{\infty}((0,T)\times\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}).

(6.33)

We also claim

\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha^{\ell-1}\partial_{x_{3}}f^{\ell}\overset{\ast}{\rightharpoonup}\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f\text{ in }L^{\infty}((0,T)\times\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}).

(6.34)

For any test function
\phi\in C^{\infty}_{c}((0,T)\times\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3})
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha^{\ell-1}\partial_{x_{3}}f^{\ell}-\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f)\phi dvdxdt

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha^{\ell-1}f^{\ell}-\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha f)\partial_{x_{3}}\phi dvdxdt

(6.37)

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\partial_{x_{3}}\alpha^{\ell-1}f^{\ell}-\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\partial_{x_{3}}\alpha^{\ell-1}f)\phi dvdxdt

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}(\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\partial_{x_{3}}\alpha^{\ell-1}f-\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\partial_{x_{3}}\alpha f)\phi dvdxdt.

From (
6.13
) and (
6.20
) we have

\|\alpha^{\ell}-\alpha\|_{L^{\infty}((0,T)\times\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}\to 0\text{ as }\ell\to\infty.

(6.38)

Thus, together with (
6.21
), we have
\eqref{alphanpfn1in}\to 0
as
n\to\infty
. Next, note that

\partial_{x_{3}}\alpha^{\ell}=\frac{x_{3}-\left(E^{\ell}_{3}(t,x_{\parallel},0)+E_{e}+(\hat{v}\times B^{\ell})_{3}(t,x_{\parallel},0)-g\right)\frac{1}{\langle v\rangle}}{\alpha^{\ell}}.

For
(t,x,v)\in
supp
\phi
,
x_{3}>0
for some
c>0
. Thus,
\frac{1}{\alpha^{\ell}(t,x,v)}<\frac{1}{c}
, so

|\partial_{x_{3}}\alpha^{\ell}\mathbf{1}_{\text{supp}(\phi)}(t,x,v)|<\frac{CM_{2}}{c}

From (
6.21
), this yields

|\eqref{alphanpfn2in}|\leq\frac{CM_{2}}{c}\int_{0}^{t}\iint_{\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}(f^{\ell}-f)\phi|dvdxdt\to 0.

For (
6.37
), since

\begin{split}&\partial_{x_{3}}\alpha^{\ell}-\partial_{x_{3}}\alpha\\
=&\frac{x_{3}-\left(E^{\ell}_{3}(t,x_{\parallel},0)+E_{e}+(\hat{v}\times B^{\ell})_{3}(t,x_{\parallel},0)-g\right)\frac{1}{\langle v\rangle}}{\alpha^{\ell}}-\frac{x_{3}-\left(E_{3}(t,x_{\parallel},0)+E_{e}+(\hat{v}\times B)_{3}(t,x_{\parallel},0)-g\right)\frac{1}{\langle v\rangle}}{\alpha}\\
=&\frac{-\left((E^{\ell}-E)_{3}(t,x_{\parallel},0)+(\hat{v}\times(B^{\ell}-B))_{3}(t,x_{\parallel},0)\right)\frac{1}{\langle v\rangle}}{\alpha^{\ell}}+\\
&+\left(x_{3}-\left(E_{3}(t,x_{\parallel},0)+E_{e}+(\hat{v}\times B)_{3}(t,x_{\parallel},0)-g\right)\right)\frac{1}{\langle v\rangle}\frac{\alpha-\alpha^{\ell}}{\alpha^{\ell}\alpha}.\end{split}

Again, for
(t,x,v)\in
supp
\phi
,
x_{3}>0
for some
c>0
. Thus
\frac{1}{\alpha^{\ell}(t,x,v)}<\frac{1}{c}
,
\frac{1}{\alpha(t,x,v)}<\frac{1}{c}
. So from (
6.20
), (
6.38
), we have

\begin{split}&\|(\partial_{x_{3}}\alpha^{\ell}-\partial_{x_{3}}\alpha)\mathbf{1}_{\text{supp}(\phi)}(t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}((0,T)\times\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}\\
&\leq\frac{1}{c}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|E(t)-E^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|B(t)-B^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}\right)+\frac{CM_{2}}{c^{2}}\|\alpha^{\ell}-\alpha\|_{L^{\infty}((0,T)\times\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}\to 0\text{ as }\ell\to\infty.\end{split}

(6.39)

Thus, we have
\eqref{alphanpfn3in}\to 0
, and this gives (
6.34
).

Therefore, from using the weak lower semi-continuity of the weak-
*
convergence (
6.32
), (
6.33
), (
6.34
), and the uniform-in-
\ell
bound (
6.14
), we conclude (
6.30
), (
6.31
).

∎

Next, we prove the uniqueness of the solutions of the RVM system (
0.40
)–(
0.42
), (
0.25
).

Lemma 21
.

Suppose
(f,E_{f},B_{f})
and
(g,E_{g},B_{g})
are solutions to the VM system (
0.40
)–(
0.42
), (
0.25
) with
f(0)=g(0)
,
E_{f}(0)=E_{g}(0)
,
B_{f}(0)=B_{g}(0)
, and that

E_{f},B_{f},E_{g},B_{g}\in W^{1,\infty}((0,T)\times\Omega),\ \nabla_{x}\rho_{f},\nabla_{x}J_{f},\partial_{t}J_{f},\nabla_{x}\rho_{g},\nabla_{x}J_{g},\partial_{t}J_{g}\in L^{\infty}((0,T);L_{\text{loc}}^{p}(\Omega))\text{ for some }p>1.

And

\sup_{0<t<T}\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{v}f(t)\|_{\infty}<\infty,\sup_{0<t<T}\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{v}g(t)\|_{\infty}<\infty.

(6.40)

Then
f=g,E_{f}=E_{g},B_{f}=B_{g}
.

Proof.

The difference function
f-g
satisfies

\begin{split}(\partial_{t}+\hat{v}\cdot\nabla_{x}+\mathfrak{F}_{f}\cdot\nabla_{v})(f-g)=(\mathfrak{F}_{g}-\mathfrak{F}_{f})\cdot\nabla_{v}g,\\
(f-g)(0)=0,\,(f-g)|_{\gamma_{-}}=0,\end{split}

(6.41)

where

\mathfrak{F}_{f}=E_{f}+E_{\text{ext}}+\hat{v}\times(B_{f}+B_{\text{ext}})-g\mathbf{e}_{3},\,\mathfrak{F}_{g}=E_{g}+E_{\text{ext}}+\hat{v}\times(B_{g}+B_{\text{ext}})-g\mathbf{e}_{3},

so

\mathfrak{F}_{g}-\mathfrak{F}_{f}=E_{f}-E_{g}+\hat{v}\times(B_{f}-B_{g}).

(6.42)

From Lemma
1
we have
E_{f,1}-E_{g,1},E_{f,2}-E_{g,2},B_{f,3}-B_{g,3}
solve the wave equation with the Dirichlet boundary condition (
1.11
) in the sense of (
1.12
) with

\displaystyle u_{0}=0,\ u_{1}=0,\ G=-4\pi\partial_{x_{i}}(\rho_{f}-\rho_{g})-4\pi\partial_{t}(J_{f,i}-J_{g,i}),\ g=0,\ \ \text{for}\ E_{f,i}-E_{g,i},i=1,2,

(6.43)

\displaystyle u_{0}=0,\ u_{1}=0,\ G=4\pi(\nabla_{x}\times(J_{f}-J_{g}))_{3},\ g=0,\ \ \text{for}\ B_{f,3}-B_{g,3},

(6.44)

respectively. And
E_{f,3}-E_{g,3},B_{f,1}-B_{g,1},B_{f,2}-B_{g,2}
solve the wave equation with the Neumann boundary condition (
1.9
) in the sense of (
1.10
)
with

\displaystyle u_{0}=0,\ u_{1}=0,\ G=-4\pi\partial_{x_{3}}(\rho_{f}-\rho_{g})-4\pi\partial_{t}(J_{f,3}-J_{g,3}),\ g=-4\pi(\rho_{f}-\rho_{g}),\ \ \text{for}\ E_{f,3}-E_{g,3},

(6.45)

\displaystyle u_{0}=0,\ u_{1}=0,\ G=4\pi(\nabla_{x}\times(J_{f}-J_{g}))_{i},\ g=(-1)^{i+1}4\pi(J_{f,{\underline{i}}}-J_{g,\underline{i}}),\ \ \text{for}\ B_{f,i}-B_{j,i},\ i=1,2,

(6.46)

respectively. Therefore, from Lemma
2
and Lemma
4
, we know that
E_{f}-E_{g}
and
B_{f}-B_{g}
would have the form of

\begin{split}&E_{f}-E_{g}=\eqref{Eesttat0pos}+\dots+\eqref{Eest3bdrycontri},\ B_{f}-B_{g}=\eqref{Besttat0pos}+\dots+\eqref{Bestbdrycontri},\\
&\text{ with }E_{0},B_{0}\text{ changes to }0,\text{ and }f\text{ changes to }f-g.\end{split}

(6.47)

Now consider the characteristics

\begin{split}\dot{X}_{f}(s;t,x,v)=&\hat{V}_{f}(s;t,x,v),\\
\dot{V}_{f}(s;t,x,v)=&\mathfrak{F}_{f}(s,X_{f}(s;t,x,v),V_{f}(s;t,x,v)).\end{split}

Then from (
6.41
), same as (
6.8
), we obtain

\begin{split}&|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}(f-g)(t,x,v)|\\
\leq&C_{1}\int_{0}^{t}|\langle V_{f}(s)\rangle^{4+\delta}(\mathfrak{F}_{g}-\mathfrak{F}_{g})\cdot\nabla_{v}f)(s,X_{f}(s),V_{f}(s))|ds\end{split}

(6.48)

So using (
6.14
), we have

\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}(f-g)(s)\|_{\infty}\leq C\int^{t}_{0}\|(\mathfrak{F}_{g}-\mathfrak{F}_{f})(s)\|_{\infty}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{v}g(s)\|_{\infty}ds.

(6.49)

Now, from (
6.47
) and the estimate in Lemma
8
, we have

\begin{split}\|(\mathfrak{F}_{g}-\mathfrak{F}_{f})(s)\|_{\infty}\leq&\|(E_{f}-E_{g})(s)\|_{\infty}+\|(B_{f}-B_{g})(s)\|_{\infty}\\
\leq&C\sup_{0\leq s^{\prime}\leq s}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}(f-g)(s^{\prime})\|_{\infty},\end{split}

(6.50)

and from the assumption (
6.40
),
\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{v}g(s)\|_{\infty}<C
. Therefore from (
6.49
) and (
6.50
), we have

\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}(f-g)(s)\|_{\infty}\leq C^{\prime}\int^{t}_{0}\sup_{0\leq s^{\prime}\leq s}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}(f-g)(s^{\prime})\|_{\infty}ds.

(6.51)

Therefore from Gronwall

\sup_{0\leq s^{\prime}\leq t}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}(f-g)(s^{\prime})\|_{\infty}\leq e^{C^{\prime}t}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}(f-g)(0)\|_{\infty}=0.

Therefore we conclude that the solutions to (
0.40
)–(
0.42
), (
0.25
), is unique.
∎

proof of Theorem
1
.

Using the sequence
f^{\ell},E^{\ell},B^{\ell}
constructed in (
6.1
), (
6.2
), we have from Lemma
19
that the limit
(f,E,B)
is a solution to the VM system (
0.40
)–(
0.42
), (
0.25
). This proves the existence. From Lemma
20
, we have the regularity estimate (
0.36
), (
0.37
). And from Lemma
21
, we conclude the uniqueness.
∎

7.
Diffuse BC

In (
0.26
), we denote
\mu=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{3/2}}e^{-\frac{|v|^{2}}{2}}
. And let the constant
c_{\mu}
be such that
c_{\mu}\int_{v_{3}>0}\hat{v}_{3}\mu(v)dv=1
.

We first prove an a priori estimate for diffuse BC.

Proposition 4
.

Let
(f,E,B)
be a solution of (
0.40
)–(
0.42
), (
0.26
). Suppose the fields satisfies (
4.2
),
and

\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\nabla_{x}E(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla_{x}B(t)\|_{\infty}\right)<\infty.

(7.1)

Assume that for
\delta>0
,
\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f,\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f,\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{v}f\in L^{\infty}((0,T)\times\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3})
,
then for
0<T\ll 1
, there exists a
C>0
such that

\begin{split}&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{v}f(t)\|_{\infty}\right)\\
&+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma\setminus\gamma_{0})}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma\setminus\gamma_{0})}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{v}f(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma\setminus\gamma_{0})}\right)\\
<&C\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f_{0}\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f_{0}\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{v}f_{0}\|_{\infty}\right).\end{split}

(7.2)

Proof.

For any
(t,x,v)\in(0,T)\times\Omega\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
, from (
5.13
) and (
5.14
), we have

\begin{split}&|\partial_{x_{i}}f(t,x,v)|\\
\lesssim&\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}>t}\{|\nabla_{x}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))||\partial_{x_{i}}X(0)|+|\nabla_{v}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))||\partial_{x_{i}}V(0)|\}\\
&+\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}<t}\left(|\partial_{t}f(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})|\frac{\delta_{i3}+t_{\mathbf{b}}}{\hat{v_{\mathbf{b}}}_{,3}}+|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})|\frac{\delta_{i3}+t_{\mathbf{b}}}{\hat{v_{\mathbf{b}}}_{,3}}+|\nabla_{v}f(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})|\left(\frac{\delta_{i3}+t_{\mathbf{b}}}{\hat{v_{\mathbf{b}}}_{,3}}+\langle v\rangle\right)\right),\end{split}

(7.3)

and

\begin{split}&|\partial_{v_{i}}f(t,x,v)|\\
\lesssim&\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}>t}\{|\nabla_{x}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))||\partial_{v_{i}}X(0)|+|\nabla_{v}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))||\partial_{v_{i}}V(0)|\}\\
&+\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}<t}\left(|\partial_{t}f(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})|\frac{t_{\mathbf{b}}}{\hat{v_{\mathbf{b}}}_{,3}}+|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})|\frac{t_{\mathbf{b}}}{\hat{v_{\mathbf{b}}}_{,3}}+|\nabla_{v}f(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})|\left(\frac{t_{\mathbf{b}}}{\hat{v_{\mathbf{b}}}_{,3}}+\langle v\rangle\right)\right)\end{split}

(7.4)

Now, using the boundary condition (
0.26
) and equation (
0.40
), we have

\begin{split}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})=&c_{\mu}\mu(v_{\mathbf{b}})\int_{u_{3}<0}-\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},u)\hat{u}_{3}du,\\
\nabla_{v}f(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})=&c_{\mu}v_{\mathbf{b}}\mu(v_{\mathbf{b}})\int_{u_{3}<0}f(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},u)\hat{u}_{3}du,\\
\partial_{t}f(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},v_{\mathbf{b}})=&c_{\mu}\mu(v_{\mathbf{b}})\int_{u_{3}<0}-\partial_{t}f(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},u)\hat{u}_{3}du\\
=&c_{\mu}\mu(v_{\mathbf{b}})\int_{u_{3}<0}\left(\hat{u}\cdot\nabla_{x}f(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},u)+\mathfrak{F}\cdot\nabla_{v}f(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},u)\right)\hat{u}_{3}du\\
=&c_{\mu}\mu(v_{\mathbf{b}})\int_{u_{3}<0}\hat{u}\cdot\nabla_{x}f(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},u)\hat{u}_{3}+f(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},u)\mathfrak{F}\cdot\nabla_{u}(\hat{u}_{3})du.\end{split}

Therefore, for
i=1,2
,

\begin{split}&|\partial_{x_{i}}f(t,x,v)|\\
\lesssim&\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}>t}\{|\nabla_{x}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))||\partial_{x_{i}}X(0)|+|\nabla_{v}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))||\partial_{x_{i}}V(0)|\}\\
&+\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}<t}\left(c_{\mu}\mu(v_{\mathbf{b}})\langle v\rangle^{2}\int_{u_{3}<0}\left(|\nabla_{x}f(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},u)|\hat{u}_{3}+f(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},u)\right)du\right)\\
\lesssim&\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}>t}\{|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))||\partial_{x_{i}}X_{\parallel}(0)|+|\partial_{x_{3}}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))||\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}X_{3}(0)|+|\nabla_{v}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))||\partial_{x_{i}}V(0)|\}\\
&+\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}<t}\left(c_{\mu}\mu(v_{\mathbf{b}})\langle v\rangle^{2}\int_{u_{3}<0}\left(|\nabla_{x}f(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},u)|\hat{u}_{3}+f(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},u)\right)du\right),\end{split}

(7.5)

where we’ve used (
4.19
). And for
i=3
, we have

\begin{split}&|\partial_{x_{3}}f(t,x,v)|\\
\lesssim&\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}>t}\{|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))||\partial_{x_{3}}X_{\parallel}(0)|+|\partial_{x_{3}}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))||\nabla_{x_{3}}X_{3}(0)|+|\nabla_{v}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))||\partial_{x_{i}}V(0)|\}\\
&+\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}<t}\left(c_{\mu}\frac{1}{\hat{v_{\mathbf{b}}}_{,3}}\mu(v_{\mathbf{b}})\langle v\rangle^{2}\int_{u_{3}<0}\left(|\nabla_{x}f(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},u)|\hat{u}_{3}+f(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},u)\right)du\right).\end{split}

(7.6)

Also,

\begin{split}&|\partial_{v_{i}}f(t,x,v)|\\
\lesssim&\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}>t}\{|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))||\partial_{v_{i}}X_{\parallel}(0)|+|\partial_{x_{3}}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))||\nabla_{v_{i}}X_{3}(0)|+|\nabla_{v}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))||\partial_{v_{i}}V(0)|\}\\
&+\mathbf{1}_{t_{\mathbf{b}}<t}\left(c_{\mu}\mu(v_{\mathbf{b}})|v_{\mathbf{b}}|\langle v\rangle^{2}\int_{u_{3}<0}\left(|\nabla_{x}f(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},u)|\hat{u}_{3}+f(t-t_{\mathbf{b}},x_{\mathbf{b}},u)\right)du\right).\end{split}

(7.7)

Let
(x,v)\notin\gamma_{0}
and
(t^{0},x^{0},v^{0})=(t,x,v)
. For the characteristic

\begin{split}\frac{d}{ds}X(s;t,x,v)&=\hat{V}(s;t,x,v),\\
\frac{d}{ds}V(s;t,x,v)&=\mathfrak{F}(s,X(s;t,x,v),V(s;t,x,v)),\end{split}

(7.8)

we define the stochastic (diffuse) cycles as

\begin{split}&t^{1}=t-t_{\mathbf{b}}(t,x,v),\,x^{1}=x_{\mathbf{b}}(t,x,v)=X(t-t_{\mathbf{b}}(t,x,v);t,x,v),\\
&v_{b}^{0}=V(t-t_{\mathbf{b}}(t,x,v);t,x,v)=v_{\mathbf{b}}(t,x,v),\end{split}

(7.9)

and
v^{1}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}
with
n(x^{1})\cdot v^{1}>0
.
For
l\geq 1
, define

\begin{split}&t^{l+1}=t^{l}-t_{\mathbf{b}}(t^{l},x^{l},v^{l}),x^{l+1}=x_{\mathbf{b}}(t^{l},x^{l},v^{l}),\\
&v_{b}^{l}=v_{\mathbf{b}}(t^{l},x^{l},v^{l}),\end{split}

(7.10)

and
v^{l+1}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}\text{ with }n(x^{l+1})\cdot v^{l+1}>0
.
Also, define

X^{l}(s)=X(s;t^{l},x^{l},v^{l}),\,V^{l}(s)=V(s;t^{l},x^{l},v^{l}),

(7.11)

so
X(s)=X^{0}(s),V(s)=V^{0}(s)
.

Expanding
\nabla_{x}f(t^{1},x^{1},v^{1})+f(t^{1},x^{1},v^{1})
in (
7.6
) again, we get for
i=1,2
,

\begin{split}&|\partial_{x_{i}}f(t,x,v)|\\
\lesssim&\mathbf{1}_{t^{1}<0}\{|\nabla_{x}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))||\partial_{x_{i}}X(0)|+|\nabla_{v}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))||\partial_{x_{i}}V(0)|\}\\
&+\mathbf{1}_{t^{2}<0<t^{1}}\bigg\{c_{\mu}\mu(v_{\mathbf{b}})\langle v\rangle^{2}\int_{v^{1}_{3}<0}\big(\left(|\nabla_{x}f(0,X^{1}(0),V^{1}(0))+\langle v^{1}\rangle|\nabla_{v}f(0,X^{1}(0),V^{1}(0))|\right)|\hat{v}^{1}_{3}\\
&\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+f(0,X^{1}(0),V^{1}(0))\big)dv^{1}\bigg\}\\
&+\mathbf{1}_{t^{2}>0}\left(c_{\mu}\mu(v_{\mathbf{b}})\langle v\rangle^{2}\int_{v^{1}_{3}<0}\left(c_{\mu}\mu(v_{\mathbf{b}}^{1})\langle v_{1}\rangle^{2}\frac{\hat{v}^{1}_{3}}{\hat{v}_{\mathbf{b}}{}_{,3}^{1}}\int_{v^{2}_{3}<0}|\left(\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t^{2},x^{2},v^{2})|\hat{v}^{2}_{3}+f(t^{2},x^{2},v^{2})\right)dv^{2}\right)dv^{1}\right),\end{split}

keep doing the expansion we get for
\ell>1
,

\begin{split}&|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t,x,v)|\\
\lesssim&\mathbf{1}_{t^{1}<0}\{|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))||\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}X_{\parallel}(0)|+|\partial_{x_{3}}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))||\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}X_{3}(0)|+|\nabla_{v}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))||\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}V(0)|\}\\
&+\mu(v_{\mathbf{b}})\langle v\rangle^{2}\int_{\prod_{j=1}^{l-1}\mathcal{V}_{j}}\sum_{i=1}^{l-1}\textbf{1}_{\{t^{i+1}<0<t^{i}\}}\bigg(\left(|\nabla_{x}f(0,X^{i}(0),V^{i}(0))|+\langle v^{i}\rangle|\nabla_{v}f(0,X^{i}(0),V^{i}(0))|\right)\hat{v}^{i}_{3}\\
&\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+f(0,X^{i}(0),V^{i}(0))\bigg)\,d\Sigma_{i}^{l-1}\\
&+\mu(v_{\mathbf{b}})\langle v\rangle^{2}\int_{\prod_{j=1}^{l-1}\mathcal{V}_{j}}\textbf{1}_{\{t^{l}>0\}}\int_{\mathcal{V}_{l}}\left(|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t^{l},x^{l},v^{l})|\hat{v}^{l}_{3}+f(t^{l},x^{l},v^{l})\right)dv^{l}d\Sigma_{l-1}^{l-1},\end{split}

(7.12)

where
\mathcal{V}_{j}=\{v^{j}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}:v^{J}_{3}<0\}
,
and

\begin{split}d\Sigma_{i}^{l-1}=&\{\prod_{j=i+1}^{l-1}\mu(v^{j})c_{\mu}|\hat{v}_{3}^{j}|dv^{j}\}\{\prod_{j=1}^{i-1}c_{\mu}\mu(v_{\mathbf{b}}^{j})\langle v^{j}\rangle^{2}\frac{\hat{v}^{J}_{3}}{\hat{v}_{\mathbf{b}}{}_{,3}^{j}}dv^{j}\},\end{split}

where
c_{\mu}
is the constant that
c_{\mu}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\mu(v^{j})|\hat{v}_{3}^{j}|dv^{j}=1
. Similarly, we get

\begin{split}&|\partial_{x_{3}}f(t,x,v)|\\
\lesssim&\mathbf{1}_{t^{1}<0}\{|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))||\partial_{x_{3}}X_{\parallel}(0)|+|\partial_{x_{3}}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))||\partial_{x_{3}}X_{3}(0)|+|\nabla_{v}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))||\partial_{x_{3}}V(0)|\}\\
&+\frac{\mu(v_{\mathbf{b}})}{\hat{v_{\mathbf{b}}}_{,3}}\langle v\rangle^{2}\int_{\prod_{j=1}^{l-1}\mathcal{V}_{j}}\sum_{i=1}^{l-1}\textbf{1}_{\{t^{i+1}<0<t^{i}\}}\bigg(\left(|\nabla_{x}f(0,X^{i}(0),V^{i}(0))|+\langle v^{i}\rangle|\nabla_{v}f(0,X^{i}(0),V^{i}(0))|\right)\hat{v}^{i}_{3}\\
&\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+f(0,X^{i}(0),V^{i}(0))\bigg)\,d\Sigma_{i}^{l-1}\\
&+\frac{\mu(v_{\mathbf{b}})}{\hat{v_{\mathbf{b}}}_{,3}}\langle v\rangle^{2}\int_{\prod_{j=1}^{l-1}\mathcal{V}_{j}}\textbf{1}_{\{t^{l}>0\}}\int_{\mathcal{V}_{l}}\left(|\nabla_{x}f(t^{l},x^{l},v^{\ell})|\hat{v}^{\ell}_{3}+f(t^{\ell},x^{\ell},v^{\ell})\right)dv^{l}d\Sigma_{l-1}^{l-1}.\end{split}

(7.13)

And

\begin{split}&|\nabla_{v}f(t,x,v)|\\
\lesssim&\mathbf{1}_{t^{1}<0}\{|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))||\nabla_{v}X_{\parallel}(0)|+|\partial_{x_{3}}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))||\nabla_{v}X_{3}(0)|+|\nabla_{v}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))||\nabla_{v}V(0)|\}\\
&+\mu(v_{\mathbf{b}})|v_{\mathbf{b}}|\langle v\rangle^{2}\int_{\prod_{j=1}^{l-1}\mathcal{V}_{j}}\sum_{i=1}^{l-1}\textbf{1}_{\{t^{i+1}<0<t^{i}\}}\bigg(\left(|\nabla_{x}f(0,X^{i}(0),V^{i}(0))|+\langle v^{i}\rangle|\nabla_{v}f(0,X^{i}(0),V^{i}(0))|\right)\hat{v}^{i}_{3}\\
&\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+f(0,X^{i}(0),V^{i}(0))\bigg)\,d\Sigma_{i}^{l-1}\\
&+\mu(v_{\mathbf{b}})|v_{\mathbf{b}}|\langle v\rangle^{2}\int_{\prod_{j=1}^{l-1}\mathcal{V}_{j}}\textbf{1}_{\{t^{l}>0\}}\int_{\mathcal{V}_{l}}\left(|\nabla_{x}f(t^{l},x^{l},v^{l})|\hat{v}^{l}_{3}+f(t^{l},x^{l},v^{l})\right)dv^{l}d\Sigma_{l-1}^{l-1},\end{split}

(7.14)

Next, we claim that there exists
l_{0}\gg 1
such that for
l\geq l_{0}
, we have

\int_{\prod_{j=1}^{l-1}\mathcal{V}_{j}}\textbf{1}_{\{t^{l}(t,x,v,v^{1},...,v^{l-1})>0\}}\,d\,\Sigma_{l-1}^{l-1}\lesssim\left(\frac{1}{2}\right)^{l}.

(7.15)

Since

|v_{\mathbf{b}}^{j}|^{2}\lesssim|v^{j}|^{2}+(t^{j}-t^{j+1})^{2}(\|E\|_{\infty}^{2}+\|B\|_{\infty}^{2}),\,\langle v_{\mathbf{b}}^{j}\rangle\lesssim\langle v^{j}\rangle+(t^{j}-t^{j+1})(\|E\|_{\infty}+\|B\|_{\infty}),

and using (
4.3
), we have for some fixed constant
C_{0}>0
,

\begin{split}\,d\,\Sigma_{l-1}^{l-1}\leq(C_{0})^{l}\prod_{j=1}^{l-1}\sqrt{\mu(v_{j})}\langle v^{j}\rangle^{2}dv^{j}.\end{split}

Choose a sufficiently small
\delta=\delta(C_{0})>0
. Define

\mathcal{V}_{j}^{\delta}=\{v^{j}\in\mathcal{V}_{j}:v^{J}_{3}\geq\delta\},

where we have
\int_{\mathcal{V}_{j}\setminus\mathcal{V}_{j}^{\delta}}C_{0}\sqrt{\mu(v_{j})}\langle v^{j}\rangle^{2}dv^{j}\lesssim\delta
.

On the other hand if
v^{j}\in\mathcal{V}_{j}^{\delta}
, we have from (
5.2
)

\begin{split}|(t^{j}-t^{j+1})\hat{v}_{3}^{j}|=&|\int^{t^{j}}_{t^{j+1}}\int^{t^{j}}_{s}\hat{\mathfrak{F}}_{3}(\tau,X^{j}(\tau),V^{j}(\tau))\mathrm{d}\tau\mathrm{d}s|\\
\leq&\int^{t^{j}}_{t^{j+1}}\int^{t^{j}}_{s}\frac{2g}{\langle V^{j}(\tau)\rangle}d\tau ds\leq\frac{(t^{j}-t^{j+1})^{2}g}{\min_{0\leq\tau\leq T}\langle V^{j}(\tau)\rangle}.\end{split}

Thus

|t^{j}-t^{j+1}|\geq\frac{v^{J}_{3}}{g}\frac{\min_{0\leq\tau\leq T}\langle V^{j}(\tau)\rangle}{\max_{0\leq\tau\leq T}\langle V^{j}(\tau)\rangle}\gtrsim v^{J}_{3}\geq\delta.

Now if
t^{l}\geq 0
then there are at most
\left[\frac{C_{\Omega}}{\delta}\right]+1
numbers of
v^{m}\in\mathcal{V}^{\delta}_{m}
for
1\leq m\leq l-1
. Equivalently there are at least
l-2-\left[\frac{C_{\Omega}}{\delta}\right]
numbers of
v^{m_{i}}\in\mathcal{V}_{m_{i}}\setminus\mathcal{V}_{m_{i}}^{\delta}
. Therefore we have:

\begin{split}\int_{\prod_{j=1}^{l-1}\mathcal{V}_{j}}&\textbf{1}_{\{t^{l}(t,x,v,v^{1},...,v^{l-1})>0\}}\,d\,\Sigma_{l-1}^{l-1}\\
\leq&\sum_{m=1}^{\left[\frac{C_{\Omega}}{\delta}\right]+1}\int_{\left\{\parbox{122.9171pt}{there are exactly $m$ of $v^{m_{i}}\in\mathcal{V}_{m_{i}}^{\delta}$ and $l-1-m$ of $v^{m_{i}}\in\mathcal{V}_{m_{i}}\setminus\mathcal{V}^{\delta}_{m_{i}}$ }\right\}}\prod_{j=1}^{l-1}C_{0}\sqrt{\mu(v_{j})}\langle v^{j}\rangle^{2}dv^{j}\\
\leq&\sum_{m=1}^{\left[\frac{C_{\Omega}}{\delta}\right]+1}{l-1\choose m}\left\{\int_{\mathcal{V}}C_{0}\sqrt{\mu(v)}\langle v\rangle^{2}dv\right\}^{m}\left\{\int_{\mathcal{V}\setminus\mathcal{V}^{\delta}}C_{0}\sqrt{\mu(v)}\langle v\rangle^{2}dv\right\}^{l-1-m}\\
\leq&\left(\left[\frac{C_{\Omega}}{\delta}\right]+1\right)(l-1)^{\left[\frac{C_{\Omega}}{\delta}\right]+1}(\delta)^{l-2-\left[\frac{C_{\Omega}}{\delta}\right]}\left\{\int_{\mathcal{V}}C_{0}\sqrt{\mu(v)}\langle v\rangle^{2}dv\right\}^{\left[\frac{C_{\Omega}}{\delta}\right]+1}\\
\leq&C\delta^{l/2}\leq C(\frac{1}{2})^{l},\end{split}

(7.16)

if
l\gg 1
, say
l=2\left(\left[\frac{C_{\Omega}}{\delta}\right]+1\right)^{2}
.

Therefore, from (
4.20
), (
4.23
), (
4.27
), (
7.12
),(
7.15
), and (
7.16
) we have

\begin{split}&\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t,x,v)|\\
\lesssim&\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\{|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))|+t|\partial_{x_{3}}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))|+\langle v\rangle|\nabla_{v}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))|\}\\
&+\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\mu(v_{\mathbf{b}})\langle v\rangle^{2}\int_{\prod_{j=1}^{l-1}\mathcal{V}_{j}}\sum_{i=1}^{l-1}\textbf{1}_{\{t^{i+1}<0<t^{i}\}}\bigg(\left(|\nabla_{x}f(0,X^{i}(0),V^{i}(0))|+\langle v^{i}\rangle|\nabla_{v}f(0,X^{i}(0),V^{i}(0))|\right)\hat{v}^{i}_{3}\\
&\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+f(0,X^{i}(0),V^{i}(0))\bigg)\,d\Sigma_{i}^{l-1}\\
&+\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\mu(v_{\mathbf{b}})\langle v\rangle^{2}\int_{\prod_{j=1}^{l-1}\mathcal{V}_{j}}\textbf{1}_{\{t^{l}>0\}}\int_{\mathcal{V}_{l}}\left(|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t^{l},x^{l},v^{l})|\hat{v}^{l}_{3}+f(t^{l},x^{l},v^{l})\right)dv^{l}d\Sigma_{l-1}^{l-1}\\
\lesssim&C_{l}\left(\|(1+|v|^{4+\delta})\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f_{0}\|_{\infty}+\|(1+|v|^{4+\delta})\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f_{0}\|_{\infty}+\|(1+|v|^{5+\delta})\nabla_{v}f_{0}\|_{\infty}\right)\\
&+C\left(\frac{1}{2}\right)^{l}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{\infty},\end{split}

(7.17)

and similarly,

\begin{split}&\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}|\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(t,x,v)|\\
\lesssim&\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha(t,x,v)\{|\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))|+|\partial_{x_{3}}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))|+\langle v\rangle|\nabla_{v}f_{0}(X(0),V(0))|\}\\
&+\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\frac{\alpha(t,x,v)\mu(v_{\mathbf{b}})}{\hat{v_{\mathbf{b}}}_{,3}}\langle v\rangle^{2}\int_{\prod_{j=1}^{l-1}\mathcal{V}_{j}}\sum_{i=1}^{l-1}\textbf{1}_{\{t^{i+1}<0<t^{i}\}}\bigg(\left(|\nabla_{x}f(0,X^{i}(0),V^{i}(0))|+\langle v^{i}\rangle|\nabla_{v}f(0,X^{i}(0),V^{i}(0))|\right)\hat{v}^{i}_{3}\\
&\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+f(0,X^{i}(0),V^{i}(0))\bigg)\,d\Sigma_{i}^{l-1}\\
&+\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\frac{\alpha(t,x,v)\mu(v_{\mathbf{b}})}{\hat{v_{\mathbf{b}}}_{,3}}\langle v\rangle^{2}\int_{\prod_{j=1}^{l-1}\mathcal{V}_{j}}\textbf{1}_{\{t^{l}>0\}}\int_{\mathcal{V}_{l}}\left(|\nabla_{x}f(t^{l},x^{l},v^{l})|\hat{v}^{l}_{3}+f(t^{l},x^{l},v^{l})\right)dv^{l}d\Sigma_{l-1}^{l-1}\\
\lesssim&C_{l}\left(\|(1+|v|^{5+\delta})\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f_{0}\|_{\infty}+\|(1+|v|^{5+\delta})\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f_{0}\|_{\infty}+\|(1+|v|^{5+\delta})\nabla_{v}f_{0}\|_{\infty}\right)\\
&+C\left(\frac{1}{2}\right)^{l}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\nabla_{x}f(t)\|_{\infty},\end{split}

(7.18)

and

\begin{split}&\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}|\nabla_{v}f(t,x,v)|\\
\lesssim&C_{l}\left(\|(1+|v|^{4+\delta})\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f_{0}\|_{\infty}+\|(1+|v|^{4+\delta})\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f_{0}\|_{\infty}+\|(1+|v|^{5+\delta})\nabla_{v}f_{0}\|_{\infty}\right)\\
&+C\left(\frac{1}{2}\right)^{l}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\nabla_{x}f(t)\|_{\infty},\end{split}

(7.19)

where we’ve used (
4.3
). Adding (
7.12
), (
7.18
), and (
7.19
) and choosing
l\gg 1
, we get for a large
C>0
,

\begin{split}&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{v}f(t)\|_{\infty}\right)\\
<&C\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f_{0}\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f_{0}\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{v}f_{0}\|_{\infty}\right).\end{split}

(7.20)

Next, using the same argument in Lemma
15
, we obtain

\begin{split}&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma\setminus\gamma_{0})}\leq\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f(t)\|_{\infty},\\
&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{v}f(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma\setminus\gamma_{0})}\leq\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{v}f(t)\|_{\infty},\\
&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma\setminus\gamma_{0})}\leq\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f(t,x,v)\|_{\infty}.\end{split}

(7.21)

Together (
7.20
), we conclude (
7.2
).

∎

In order to construct a solution to the system (
0.40
)–(
0.42
), (
0.26
), we define a sequence of functions:

f^{0}(t,x,v)=f_{0}(x,v),\ E^{0}(t,x)=E_{0}(t,x),\ B^{0}(t,x)=B_{0}(x).

For
\ell\geq 1
, let
f^{\ell}
be the solution of

\begin{split}\partial_{t}f^{\ell}+\hat{v}\cdot\nabla_{x}f^{\ell}+\mathfrak{F}^{\ell-1}\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{\ell}=&0,\text{ where }\mathfrak{F}^{\ell-1}=E^{\ell-1}+E_{\text{ext}}+\hat{v}\times(B^{\ell-1}+B_{\text{ext}})-g\mathbf{e}_{3},\\
f^{\ell}(0,x,v)=&f_{0}(x,v),\\
f^{\ell}(t,x,v)|_{\gamma_{-}}=&c_{\mu}\mu(v)\int_{u_{3}<0}-f^{\ell-1}(t,x,u)\hat{u}_{3}du.\end{split}

(7.22)

Let
\rho^{\ell}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}f^{\ell}dv,j^{\ell}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}}\hat{v}f^{\ell}dv
. Let

E^{\ell}=\eqref{Eesttat0pos}+\dots+\eqref{Eest3bdrycontri},\ B^{\ell}=\eqref{Besttat0pos}+\dots+\eqref{Bestbdrycontri},\text{ with }f\text{ changes to }f^{\ell}.

(7.23)

And let

\mathfrak{F}^{\ell}=E^{\ell}+E_{\text{ext}}-\hat{v}\times(B^{\ell}+B_{\text{ext}})-g\mathbf{e}_{3}.

(7.24)

We prove several uniform-in-
\ell
bounds for the sequence before passing the limit.

Lemma 22
.

Suppose
f_{0}
satisfies (
0.32
),
E_{0}
,
B_{0}
satisfy (
0.16
), (
0.35
), then there exits
M_{1},M_{2}
, and
c_{0}
, such that for
0<T\ll 1
,

\begin{split}\sup_{\ell}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f^{\ell}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\bar{\Omega}\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}\right)<&M_{1},\ \\
\sup_{\ell}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|E^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|B^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}\right)+|B_{e}|+E_{e}+g<&M_{2},\\
\inf_{\ell}\inf_{t,x_{\parallel}}\left(g-E_{e}-E^{\ell}_{3}(t,x_{\parallel},0)-(\hat{v}\times B^{\ell})_{3}(t,x_{\parallel},0)\right)>&c_{0}.\end{split}

(7.25)

Proof.

Let
\ell\geq 1
. By induction hypothesis we assume that

\begin{split}\sup_{0\leq i\leq\ell}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f^{\ell-i}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\bar{\Omega}\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}\right)<&M_{1},\\
\sup_{0\leq i\leq\ell}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|E^{\ell-i}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|B^{\ell-i}(t)\|_{\infty}\right)+|B_{e}|+E_{e}+g<&M_{2}.\end{split}

(7.26)

Let the characteristics
(X^{\ell},V^{\ell})
be defined as in (
6.5
). We define the stochastic cycles:

\begin{split}t^{\ell}_{1}(t,x,v)&:=\sup\{s<t:X^{\ell}(s;t,x,v)\in\partial\Omega\},\\
x^{\ell}_{1}(t,x,v)&:=X^{\ell}(t^{\ell}_{1}(t,x,v);t,x,v),\\
t^{\ell-1}_{2}(t,x,v,v_{1})&:=\sup\{s<t^{\ell}_{1}:X^{\ell-1}(s;t^{\ell}_{1}(t,x,v),x^{\ell}_{1}(t,x,v),v_{1})\in\partial\Omega\},\\
x^{\ell-1}_{2}(t,x,v,v_{1})&:=X^{\ell-1}(t^{\ell-1}_{2}(t,x,v,v_{1});t^{\ell}_{1}(t,x,v),x^{\ell}_{1}(t,x,v),v_{1}),\\
\end{split}

(7.27)

and inductively

\begin{split}&t^{\ell-(k-1)}_{k}(t,x,v,v_{1},\cdots,v_{k-1})\\
&:=\sup\big\{s<t^{\ell-(k-2)}_{k-1}:X^{\ell-1}(s;t_{k-1}^{\ell-(k-2)},x_{k-1}^{\ell-(k-2)},v_{k-1})\in\partial\Omega\big\},\\
&x_{k}^{\ell-(k-1)}(t,x,v,v_{1},\cdots,v_{k-1})\\
&:=X^{\ell-(k-2)}(t_{k}^{\ell-(k-1)};t_{k-1}^{\ell-(k-2)},x_{k-1}^{\ell-(k-2)},v_{k-1}).\end{split}

(7.28)

Here,

\begin{split}t^{\ell-(i-1)}_{i}&:=t^{\ell-(i-1)}_{i}(t,x,v,v_{1},\cdots,v_{i-1}),\\
x^{\ell-(i-1)}_{i}&:=x^{\ell-(i-1)}_{i}(t,x,v,v_{1},\cdots,v_{i-1}).\end{split}

First, we note that for any
t^{\ell-i}_{i+1}\leq s<t^{\ell-(i-1)}_{i}
, since

V^{\ell-i}(s;t^{\ell-(i-1)}_{i},x^{\ell-(i-1)}_{i},v_{i})=v_{i}-\int_{s}^{t^{\ell-(i-1)}_{i}}\mathfrak{F}^{\ell-i}(\tau,X^{\ell-i}(\tau),V^{\ell-i}(\tau))d\tau,

from (
7.26
), we have

|v_{i}|-(t^{\ell-(i-1)}_{i}-t^{\ell-i}_{i+1})M_{2}\leq|V^{\ell-i}(s;t^{\ell-(i-1)}_{i},x^{\ell-(i-1)}_{i},v_{i})|\leq|v_{i}|+(t^{\ell-(i-1)}_{i}-t^{\ell-i}_{i+1})M_{2}.

Thus

\left(1+(t^{\ell-(i-1)}_{i}-t^{\ell-i}_{i+1})M_{2}\right)^{-1}\langle v\rangle\leq\langle V^{\ell-i}(s;t^{\ell-(i-1)}_{i},x^{\ell-(i-1)}_{i},v_{i})\rangle\leq\left(1+(t^{\ell-(i-1)}_{i}-t^{\ell-i}_{i+1})M_{2}\right)\langle v\rangle.

(7.29)

From (
7.22
) we have for any
(t,x,v)\in(0,T)\times\bar{\Omega}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
,

\begin{split}f^{\ell+1}(t,x,v)=&\mathbf{1}_{t^{\ell}_{1}\leq 0}f^{\ell+1}(0,X^{\ell}(0),V^{\ell}(0))+\mathbf{1}_{t_{1}^{\ell}\geq 0}f^{\ell+1}(t_{1}^{\ell},X^{\ell}(t_{1}^{\ell};t,x,v),V^{\ell}(t_{1}^{\ell};t,x,v))\\
=&\mathbf{1}_{t^{\ell}_{1}\leq 0}f^{\ell+1}(0,X^{\ell}(0),V^{\ell}(0))-\mathbf{1}_{t_{1}^{\ell}\geq 0}c_{\mu}\mu(V^{\ell}(t^{\ell}_{1}))\int_{v_{1,3}<0}f^{\ell}(t^{\ell}_{1},x^{\ell}_{1},v_{1})\hat{v}_{1,3}\mathrm{d}v_{1}.\end{split}

(7.30)

And (
7.29
) gives

\begin{split}&\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}|f^{\ell+1}(t,x,v)|\\
\leq&\mathbf{1}_{t^{\ell}_{1}\leq 0}(1+T(M_{2}+g))^{4+\delta}|\langle V^{\ell}(0)\rangle^{4+\delta}f^{\ell+1}(0,X^{\ell}(0),V^{\ell}(0))|\\
&+\mathbf{1}_{t_{1}^{\ell}\geq 0}c_{\mu}(1+T(M_{2}+g))^{4+\delta}|\langle V^{\ell}(t^{\ell}_{1})\rangle^{4+\delta}\mu(V^{\ell}(t^{\ell}_{1}))\int_{v_{1,3}<0}\langle v_{1}\rangle^{4+\delta}f^{\ell}(t^{\ell}_{1},x^{\ell}_{1},v_{1})\frac{\hat{v}_{1,3}}{\langle v_{1}\rangle^{4+\delta}}\mathrm{d}v_{1}|.\end{split}

(7.31)

Then inductively, we obtain

\begin{split}&\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}|f^{\ell+1}(t,x,v)|\leq\mathbf{1}_{t^{\ell}_{1}\leq 0}(1+t(M_{2}+g))^{4+\delta}||\langle V^{\ell}(0)\rangle^{4+\delta}f^{\ell+1}(0,X^{\ell}(0),V^{\ell}(0))|\\
&+(1+T(M_{2}+g))^{4+\delta}\int_{\prod_{j=1}^{k-1}\mathcal{V}_{j}}\sum_{i=1}^{k-1}\textbf{1}_{\{t^{\ell-i}_{i+1}\leq 0<t^{\ell-(i-1)}_{i}\}}|\langle V^{\ell-i}(0;v_{i})\rangle^{4+\delta}f^{\ell-(i-1)}(0,X^{\ell-i}(0;v_{i}),V^{\ell-i}(0;v_{i}))|\,d\Sigma_{i}^{k-1}\\
&+(1+T(M_{2}+g))^{4+\delta}\int_{\prod_{j=1}^{k-1}\mathcal{V}_{j}}\textbf{1}_{\{t_{k}^{\ell-(k-1)}>0\}}\int_{\mathcal{V}_{k}}|f^{\ell-k}(t_{k}^{\ell-(k-1)},x_{k}^{\ell-(k-1)},v_{k})|dv_{k}d\Sigma_{k-1}^{k-1},\end{split}

(7.32)

where

\begin{split}X^{\ell-i}(0;v_{i})=&X^{\ell-i}(0;t^{\ell-(i-1)}_{i},x^{\ell-(i-1)}_{i},v_{i}),\\
V^{\ell-i}(0;v_{i})=&V^{\ell-i}(0;t^{\ell-(i-1)}_{i},x^{\ell-(i-1)}_{i},v_{i}),\end{split}

\mathcal{V}_{j}=\{v_{j}\in\mathbb{R}^{3}:v_{j,3}<0\}
,
and

\begin{split}d\Sigma_{i}^{k-1}=&\{\prod_{j=1}^{i-1}c_{\mu}(1+T(M_{2}+g))^{4+\delta}\mu(V^{\ell-(j-1)}(t^{\ell-(j-1)}_{j}))\frac{\hat{v}_{j,3}\langle V^{\ell-(j-1)}(t^{\ell-(j-1)}_{j})\rangle^{4+\delta}}{{\hat{V}}^{\ell-(j-1)}_{3}(t^{\ell-(j-1)}_{j})\langle v_{j}\rangle^{4+\delta}}dv_{j}\}\{\prod_{j=i+1}^{k-1}\mu(v_{j})c_{\mu}|\hat{v}_{j,3}|dv_{j}\}.\end{split}

(7.33)

From the same argument as in (
7.15
)-(
7.16
), we get there exists
k_{0}\gg 1
such that for
k\geq k_{0}
,

\int_{\prod_{j=1}^{k-1}\mathcal{V}_{j}}\textbf{1}_{\{t_{k}^{\ell-(k-1)}>0\}}d\Sigma_{k-1}^{k-1}\leq\left(\frac{1}{2}\right)^{k}.

(7.34)

Thus, from (
7.32
), (
7.34
), we have

\begin{split}&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f^{\ell+1}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\bar{\Omega}\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}\\
&\leq k(1+TM_{2})^{4+\delta}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f_{0}\|_{\infty}+(1+T(M_{2}+g))^{4+\delta}\left(\frac{1}{2}\right)^{k}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f^{\ell-i}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\bar{\Omega}\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}.\end{split}

(7.35)

By choosing
M_{1}\gg 1
and then
T\ll 1
, we get

\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f^{\ell+1}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\bar{\Omega}\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}<M_{1}.

(7.36)

Now from (
7.23
) and (
0.16
), using the same argument as (
6.10
)–(
6.12
), we get

\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|E^{\ell+1}(t)\|_{\infty}+\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|B^{\ell+1}(t)\|_{\infty}+|B_{e}|+E_{e}+g<M_{2},

(7.37)

and

\inf_{t,x_{\parallel}}\left(g-E_{e}-E^{\ell+1}_{3}(t,x_{\parallel},0)-(\hat{v}\times B^{\ell+1})_{3}(t,x_{\parallel},0)\right)>c_{0}.

(7.38)

Thus we conclude (
7.25
) by induction.
∎

Next, we consider the derivative of the sequences. Define
\alpha^{\ell}
as in (
6.13
).
We have the following estimate.

Lemma 23
.

Suppose
f_{0}
satisfies (
0.32
),
E_{0}
,
B_{0}
satisfy (
0.35
), then there exits
M_{3},M_{4}
such that for
0<T\ll 1
,

\begin{split}&\sup_{\ell}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha^{\ell-1}\partial_{x_{3}}f^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{v}f^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}\right)\\
&+\sup_{\ell}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f^{\ell}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma\setminus\gamma_{0})}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha^{\ell-1}\partial_{x_{3}}f^{\ell}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma\setminus\gamma_{0})}+\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{v}f^{\ell}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma\setminus\gamma_{0})}\right)<M_{3},\\
&\sup_{\ell}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\partial_{t}E^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\partial_{t}B^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla_{x}E^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla_{x}B^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}\right)<M_{4}.\end{split}

(7.39)

Proof.

The proof is essentially the same as the proof of Proposition
4
. The only difference is that instead of using the stochastic cycles (
7.8
)-(
7.11
) that flows under fixed
E(t,x),B(t,x)
, we use the (
7.27
)-(
7.28
) that flows with a different
E^{\ell}(t,x),B^{\ell}(t,x)
after each bounce.

From the uniform estimate (
7.25
), and from the velocity lemma (Lemma
10
), we have for some
C>0
,

e^{-C|t-s|}\alpha^{\ell}(t,x,v)\leq\alpha^{\ell}(s,X^{\ell}(s;t,x,v),V^{\ell}(s;t,x,v))\leq e^{C|t-s|}\alpha^{\ell}(t,x,v),\ \text{ for all }\ell.

(7.40)

Therefore, following the same proof of Proposition
4
we get

\begin{split}&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f^{\ell+1}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha^{\ell}\partial_{x_{3}}f^{\ell+1}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{v}f^{\ell+1}(t)\|_{\infty}\right)\\
+&\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f^{\ell}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma\setminus\gamma_{0})}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha^{\ell-1}\partial_{x_{3}}f^{\ell}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma\setminus\gamma_{0})}+\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{v}f^{\ell}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma\setminus\gamma_{0})}\right)\\
\leq&C_{k}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f_{0}\|_{\infty}+\|\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f_{0}\|_{\infty}+\|\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{v}f_{0}\|_{\infty}\right)\\
&+C\left(\frac{1}{2}\right)^{k}\sup_{0\leq i\leq\ell}\bigg(\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f^{i}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha\partial_{x_{3}}f^{i}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\nabla_{v}f^{i}(t)\|_{\infty}\right)\\
&+\sup_{0\leq i\leq\ell}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f^{i}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma\setminus\gamma_{0})}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha^{i-1}\partial_{x_{3}}f^{i}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma\setminus\gamma_{0})}+\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{v}f^{i}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma\setminus\gamma_{0})}\right)\bigg).\end{split}

(7.41)

Thus, by choosing
k\gg 1
and
M_{3}\gg 1
, we conclude

\begin{split}&\sup_{\ell}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha^{\ell-1}\partial_{x_{3}}f^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{v}f^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}\right)\\
&+\sup_{\ell}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f^{\ell}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma\setminus\gamma_{0})}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha^{\ell-1}\partial_{x_{3}}f^{\ell}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma\setminus\gamma_{0})}+\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{v}f^{\ell}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma\setminus\gamma_{0})}\right)<M_{3}.\end{split}

(7.42)

From this, we use the same argument to get (
3.63
) in the proof of Lemma
9
and obtain

\begin{split}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}&\left(\|\partial_{t}E^{\ell+1}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\partial_{t}B^{\ell+1}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla_{x}E^{\ell+1}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla_{x}B^{\ell+1}(t)\|_{\infty}\right)\\
\leq&TC\sup_{1\leq i\leq\ell}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\partial_{t}E^{i}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\partial_{t}B^{i}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla_{x}E^{i}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla_{x}B^{i}(t)\|_{\infty}\right)\\
&C\left(\|E_{0}\|_{C^{2}}+\|B_{0}\|_{C_{2}}\right)+C\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f^{\ell+1}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\langle v\rangle^{\ell+\delta}\alpha^{n}\partial_{x_{3}}f^{\ell+1}(t)\|_{\infty}\right)\\
&+C\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{x_{\parallel}}f^{\ell}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma\setminus\gamma_{0})}+\|\langle v\rangle^{5+\delta}\alpha^{\ell-1}\partial_{x_{3}}f^{\ell}(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\gamma\setminus\gamma_{0})}\right)\\
&+C\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f^{\ell+1}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|E^{\ell+1}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|B^{\ell+1}(t)\|_{\infty}\right).\end{split}

From (
7.25
) and (
7.42
), this gives

\begin{split}\sup_{\ell}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}&\left(\|\partial_{t}E^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\partial_{t}B^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla_{x}E^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla_{x}B^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}\right)\\
\leq&TC\sup_{\ell}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\partial_{t}E^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\partial_{t}B^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla_{x}E^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla_{x}B^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}\right)\\
&+C\left(\|E_{0}\|_{C^{2}}+\|B_{0}\|_{C_{2}}\right)+C(M_{1}+M_{2}+M_{3}).\end{split}

Therefore, by choosing
M_{4}\gg 1
and
T\ll 1
, we get

\sup_{\ell}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|\partial_{t}E^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\partial_{t}B^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla_{x}E^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|\nabla_{x}B^{\ell}(t)\|_{\infty}\right)<M_{4}.

(7.43)

Together with (
7.42
), we conclude (
7.39
).
∎

Note that from (
7.39
), using the argument in Lemma
18
we have
E^{\ell}|_{\partial\Omega},B^{\ell}|_{\partial\Omega}\in L^{\infty}((0,T)\times\partial\Omega)
for all
\ell
. Next, we prove the strong convergence of the sequence
f^{\ell}
.

Lemma 24
.

Suppose
f_{0}
satisfies (
0.32
),
E_{0}
,
B_{0}
satisfy (
0.16
), (
0.35
). There exists functions
(f,E,B)
with
\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f(t,x,v)\in L^{\infty}((0,T);L^{\infty}(\bar{\Omega}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}))
, and
(E,B)\in L^{\infty}((0,T);L^{\infty}(\Omega)\cap L^{\infty}(\partial\Omega))
, such that as
\ell\to\infty
,

\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\left(\|E^{\ell}(t)-E(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}+\|E^{\ell}(t)-E(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\partial\Omega)}+\|B^{\ell}(t)-B(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\Omega)}+\|B^{\ell}(t)-B(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\partial\Omega)}\right)\to 0,

(7.44)

and

\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\ell}f^{\ell}(t)-\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f(t)\|_{L^{\infty}(\bar{\Omega}\times\mathbb{R}^{3})}\to 0.

(7.45)

Moreover,
(f,E,B)
is a (weak) solution of the system (
0.40
)–(
0.42
), and (
0.26
).

Proof.

Let
m>n\geq 1
. Note that
f^{m}-f^{n}
satisfies
(f^{m}-f^{n})|_{t=0}=0
and

(f^{m}-f^{n})|_{\gamma_{-}}=c_{\mu}\mu\int_{\gamma_{+}}(f^{m-1}-f^{n-1})(t,x,u)\hat{u}_{3}du.

The equation for
f^{m}-f^{n}
is

\partial_{t}(f^{m}-f^{n})+\hat{v}\cdot\nabla_{x}(f^{m}-f^{n})+\mathfrak{F}^{m-1}\cdot\nabla_{v}(f^{m}-f^{n})=-(\mathfrak{F}^{m-1}-\mathfrak{F}^{n-1})\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{n}.

Thus, for any
(t,x,v)\in(0,T)\times\bar{\Omega}\times\mathbb{R}^{3}
, using (
7.29
), we get

\begin{split}&|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}(f^{m}-f^{n})(t,x,v)|\\
\leq&C_{1}\int_{\max\{t^{m-1}_{1},0\}}^{t}|\langle V^{m-1}(s)\rangle^{4+\delta}(\mathfrak{F}^{m-1}-\mathfrak{F}^{n-1})\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{n})(s,X^{m-1}(s),V^{m-1}(s))|ds\\
&+\mathbf{1}_{t^{m-1}_{1}>0}C_{1}c_{\mu}V^{m-1}(t_{1}^{m})\mu(V^{m}(t_{1}^{m}))\int_{v_{1,3}<0}|(f^{m-1}-f^{n-1})(t_{1}^{m-1},x_{1}^{m-1},v_{1})\hat{v}_{1,3}|dv_{1},\end{split}

where
C_{1}=(1+T(M_{2}+g))^{4+\delta}
. Doing this inductively, we obtain

\begin{split}&|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}(f^{m}-f^{n})(t,x,v)|\\
\leq&C_{1}\int_{\prod_{j=1}^{k-1}\mathcal{V}_{j}}\sum_{i=1}^{k-1}\int_{\max\{t^{m-i}_{i},0\}}^{t^{m-(i-1)}_{i-1}}\mathbf{1}_{\{t^{m-i}_{i}\leq 0<t^{m-(i-1)}_{i-1}\}}\\
&\quad\quad\quad\quad\times|\langle V^{m-i}(s)\rangle^{4+\delta}(\mathfrak{F}^{m-i}-\mathfrak{F}^{n-i})\cdot\nabla_{v}f^{n-(i-1)})(s,X^{m-i}(s),V^{m-i}(s))|dsd\Sigma_{i}^{k-1}\\
&+C_{1}\int_{\prod_{j=1}^{k-1}\mathcal{V}_{j}}\textbf{1}_{\{t_{k-1}^{m-(k-1)}>0\}}\int_{\mathcal{V}_{k}}|(f^{m-k}-f^{n-k})(t_{k}^{m-(k-1)},x_{k}^{m-(k-1)},v_{k})|dv_{k}d\Sigma_{k-1}^{k-1}.\end{split}

(7.46)

Where,
\mathcal{V}_{j}
and
\Sigma_{i}^{k-1}
are in (
7.33
). Then from (
7.34
) and (
7.39
), by fixing
k\gg 1
,we get

\begin{split}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f^{m}(t)-\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}f^{n})(t)\|_{\infty}\leq&C_{k}C_{1}\left(\sup_{\ell}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}\nabla_{v}f^{\ell}(s)\|_{\infty}\right)\int_{0}^{t}\sup_{1\leq i\leq k}\|\mathfrak{F}^{m-i}(s)-\mathfrak{F}^{n-i}(s)\|_{\infty}ds\\
\leq&C_{2}\int_{0}^{t}\sup_{1\leq i\leq k}\|\mathfrak{F}^{m-i}(s)-\mathfrak{F}^{n-i}(s)\|_{\infty}ds,\end{split}

(7.47)

where
C_{2}=C_{k}C_{1}M_{3}
.

Now,
from (
7.24
) and using the same argument as Lemma
8
with (
7.47
), we have

\begin{split}\|\mathfrak{F}^{m-i}(s)-\mathfrak{F}^{n-i}(s)\|_{\infty}\leq&\|E^{n-i}(s)-E^{m-i}(s)\|_{\infty}+\|B^{n-i}(s)-B^{m-i}(s)\|_{\infty}\\
\leq&C\left(\sup_{0\leq s^{\prime}\leq s}\|\langle v\rangle^{4+\delta}(f^{n-i}-f^{m-i})(s^{\prime})\|_{\infty}+\int_{0}^{s}\|\mathfrak{F}^{m-i-1}(s^{\prime})-\mathfrak{F}^{n-i-1}(s^{\prime})\|_{\infty}ds^{\prime}\right)\\
\leq&C\int_{0}^{s}\sup_{1\leq i_{1}\leq k}\|\mathfrak{F}^{m-i-i_{1}}(s^{\prime})-\mathfrak{F}^{n-i-i_{1}}(s^{\prime})\|_{\infty}ds^{\prime}\\
\leq&C\int_{0}^{s}\sup_{1\leq i\leq 2k}\left(\|E^{m-i}(s^{\prime})-E^{n-i}(s^{\prime})\|_{\infty}+\|B^{m-i}(s^{\prime})-B^{n-i}(s^{\prime})\|_{\infty}\right)ds^{\prime}.\end{split}

(7.48)

Iteration of (
7.48
) and using (
7.25
) yields

\begin{split}&\|E^{m}(t)-E^{n}(t)\|_{\infty}+\|B^{m}(t)-B^{n}(t)\|_{\infty}\\
